\documentclass[11pt]{amsart}
\usepackage{amsmath,amssymb,amsthm}
\newcommand{\C}{\mathbb C}
\newcommand{\R}{\mathbb R}
\newcommand{\HH}{\mathcal H}
\newcommand{\G}{\mathcal G}
\newcommand{\N}{\mathcal N}

\begin{document}
\title{HBL \S2.13: the two original moving Kummer Green channels}
\date{November 8, 2026}
\maketitle

\noindent\textbf{Scope.}
The statements below concern the actual three-dimensional inverse-square
quartic Hartree source, its origin-normalized Kummer Green integrals,
and the matched physical field.  They do not assert the still-open
full nonlinear physical parametrix or its $s^{-9/4}$ residual bound.

Let $a>0$, $L\ge 1$, $r_0=L/a\le B<R_K$,
and let $K$ denote the original origin-normalized Kummer basis.
For the actual physical source $\mathcal S_\HH$ put
\[
 F(r):=-a^2\mathcal S_\HH(ar),\qquad
 M_\HH:=C_{\mathrm{phys}}(B_z,A_z)\,\ell^{1/8}.
\]
The previously established source and normalized basis bounds read
\[
 |\mathcal S_\HH(y)|\le M_\HH(1+y)^{-19/8},\qquad
 |X(r)|+|Y(r)|\le 2G/r,\qquad r_0\le r\le B,
\]
where $X$ and $Y$ are the actual physical fundamental solutions
and the Wronskian, with its original normalization, satisfies
\[
 W(r)=r^{-2}e^{-ir^2/4},\qquad |W(r)^{-1}|=r^2.
\]
Consequently, for $Z\in\{X,Y\}$ and $r\in[r_0,B]$,
\begin{align*}
 \left|\frac{Z(r)F(r)}{W(r)}\right|
 &\le G a^2 M_\HH\,r(1+ar)^{-19/8}\\
 &\le G M_\HH a\,(ar)^{-11/8}.
\end{align*}
For every $\rho\in[r_0,B]$ define the \emph{original}, unswapped
Green coefficients
\[
 A(\rho)=\int_\rho^B\frac{X(r)F(r)}{W(r)}\,dr,\qquad
 B(\rho)=\int_{r_0}^{\rho}\frac{Y(r)F(r)}{W(r)}\,dr.
\]
The exact physical change of variables $x=ar$ gives
\begin{align*}
 |A(\rho)|
 &\le GM_\HH\int_{a\rho}^{aB}x^{-11/8}\,dx
 \le \frac83 GM_\HH L^{-3/8},\\
 |B(\rho)|
 &\le GM_\HH\int_L^{a\rho}x^{-11/8}\,dx
 \le \frac83 GM_\HH L^{-3/8},\\
 |A(\rho)|+|B(\rho)|
 &\le \frac{16}{3}GM_\HH L^{-3/8}.
\end{align*}
In particular, \emph{neither Green coefficient carries a positive
power of $a$}.  The estimate applies at every normalized radius,
not only at the upper interface.

With the genuine coupled compatibility obstruction $\sigma=0$,
the matched Kummer field is exactly
\[
 w(\rho)=Y(\rho)A(\rho)+X(\rho)\bigl(B(\rho)+\gamma\bigr),
 \qquad
 \gamma=\frac{Y(r_0)DU-Y'(r_0)U}{W(r_0)}.
\]
The first equality is a statement about the complete inhomogeneous
Green branch, not an indicator-supported outer repair.  The second
equality fixes the regular coefficient from the actual left Fredholm
Cauchy jet $(U,DU)$, rather than introducing a free amplitude.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Remaining quantitative distinction.}
The coefficient estimate cannot be multiplied by $|Y(\rho)|\le G/\rho$
and declared uniform as $r_0=L/a\to0$.
A source-and-left-jet fixed-annulus estimate instead gives
\[
 |w(\rho)|+|w'(\rho)|
 \le E,\qquad
 E:=2C^3\bigl((B-r_0)M+\lvert U\rvert+\lvert DU\rvert\bigr),
\]
where $C$ is the original \emph{annulus-dependent} basis budget
and $M$ bounds the original Kummer forcing on this annulus.
For $v(y)=w(y/a)$ one obtains
\[
 \int_L^{aB}y^2\lvert v'(y)\rvert^2\,dy
 \le a(B-r_0)(BE)^2,\qquad
 |v(y)|\le (1+aB)E(1+y^2)^{-1/2}.
\]
These last physical source-controlled energy and bubble inequalities
are separate Lean candidates at the checkpoint; the entire time-uniform
inner/Green/repair/remote field estimate does not follow until
the moving-origin basis, exact cancellations, and true time derivative
are controlled.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Formal verification correspondence.}
The fixed-annulus interior norm certificate is strictly accepted
(CI 42439861649).  The two endpoint Green coefficient estimates
are strictly accepted (CI 42440630160 and the older regular-channel
certificate).  The all-$\rho$ two-channel bridge and the physical
Green-region energy/bubble and four-piece $C^1$ bridges require their
independent CI results before citation as accepted theorems.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Unchanged final target.}
For one genuine radial Hartree orbit, establish the complete nonlinear
weighted residual and its two-parameter Lipschitz difference at
$O(s^{-9/4})$, then derive the Type--II blowup exponent dynamically,
without prescribing the rate.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Left outgoing trace: positive radius gain.}
The original regular normalized physical Kummer mode $X$ has the genuine
Frobenius estimates on $0<r\le B\le 1$,
\[
 |X(r)|\le 1+(D+1)G B^2,\qquad
 |X'(r)|\le 2(D+1)Gr.
\]
Together with $|W(r)^{-1}|=r^2$, the original Wronskian projection
of the \emph{actual} left Cauchy data satisfies
\begin{align*}
 J_-(r_0)
  &:=\frac{U X'(r_0)-DU\,X(r_0)}{W(r_0)},\\
 |J_-(r_0)|
  &\le C_{\mathrm{reg}}\,r_0\bigl(|U|+r_0|DU|\bigr),\\
 C_{\mathrm{reg}}
  &:=1+(D+1)G(B^2+2).
\end{align*}
The original normalized two-channel Fredholm inverse proves
\[
 |U|+r_0|DU|
 \le C_{\mathrm{F}}\,
  \bigl(\|F_+\|_{L^{2,7/4}}+\|F_-\|_{L^{2,7/4}}\bigr),
 \qquad r_0=L/a,
\]
and hence, without a $r_0^{-1}$ loss,
\[
 |J_-(r_0)|\le C_{\mathrm{reg}}C_{\mathrm F}\,r_0
  \bigl(\|F_+\|_{L^{2,7/4}}+\|F_-\|_{L^{2,7/4}}\bigr).
\]
These formulas use the actual origin-normalized basis, its regular
mode, and the exact two-channel Fredholm source; they do not
prescribe a Type--II scaling rate.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Transport of the genuine compatible source moment.}
The exact compatibility equation at the left interface is
\[
 A(r_0)=J_-(r_0).
\]
For every $r\in[r_0,B]$, interval additivity of the original
Green source yields
\begin{equation}
 A(r)=J_-(r_0)-
  \int_{r_0}^{r}\frac{X(\rho)F(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho.
 \label{eq:actual-singular-green-transport}
\end{equation}
The matched origin Green field is therefore exactly
\[
 w(r)
  =Y(r)\left[J_-(r_0)-
      \int_{r_0}^{r}\frac{X(\rho)F(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho
    \right]
   +X(r)\left[\gamma+\int_{r_0}^{r}
      \frac{Y(\rho)F(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho
     \right].
\]
The first integral vanishes at $r=r_0$; this is stronger than
estimating $A(r)$ by a global constant and is essential when
the physical singular mode is multiplied back in.
A genuine near-$r_0$ weighted local-moment estimate remains to be
established to convert the algebraic cancellation into uniform
full-field $H^1$ and Hartree source bounds.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Near-interface quantitative local Hartree source moment.}
In addition to the whole-annulus coefficient estimate, the original
physical source and regular Kummer mode give the pointwise bound
\[
 \left|\frac{X(r)F(r)}{W(r)}\right|
 \le GM_{\HH}\,a(ar)^{-11/8}
 \le GM_{\HH}\,aL^{-11/8},\qquad ar\ge L.
\]
Integrating over the genuine moving segment $[L/a,r]$ yields
\begin{equation}
 \left|\int_{L/a}^{r}
       \frac{X(\rho)F(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho\right|
 \le GM_{\HH}\,(ar-L)L^{-11/8}.
 \label{eq:actual-local-hartree-green-moment}
\end{equation}
This is precisely zero at the left interface. Together with
\eqref{eq:actual-singular-green-transport} it separates the singular
mode into a source-selected left-jet contribution and a locally
vanishing true PDE source moment. Multiplication by the singular
fundamental solution and the resulting global spatial and time estimates
still require further quantitative control. The corresponding new
Lean source is
\texttt{ActualHartreeKummerOutgoingLocalMomentGain.lean};
its strict CI acceptance remains pending at this checkpoint.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Lean verification scope (new candidates).}
The preceding singular Green transport and the positive-radius
Wronskian trace bounds correspond respectively to
\texttt{ActualHartreeKummerSingularGreenCompatibilityTransport.lean}
and
\texttt{ActualHartreeOriginOutgoingTraceNoRadiusLoss.lean},
and their same-source Fredholm composition is
\texttt{ActualHartreeFredholmOutgoingNoRadiusLoss.lean}.
Each new declaration must pass its own strict kernel/axiom CI before
being labelled a verified theorem. The full fixed-order
physical profile at $s^{-9/4}$ remains unproved.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Sharper estimate for the \emph{product} with the singular Green mode.}
The preceding estimate \eqref{eq:actual-local-hartree-green-moment}
used the coarse bound $|X(r)|\le G/r$, although $X$ is the
\emph{regular} origin mode. The actual Frobenius jet improves it to
\[
 |X(r)|\le C_{\rm reg}:=1+(D+1)G B^2
 \quad(0<r\le B).
\]
Retaining this improvement \emph{before} estimating the source
density and using the exact physical Wronskian gives, with $y=ar\ge L\ge1$,
\begin{align*}
 \left|\frac{X(r)F(r)}{W(r)}\right|
 &\le C_{\rm reg}a^2 M_{\HH}r^2(1+ar)^{-19/8}\\
 &=C_{\rm reg}M_{\HH}y^2(1+y)^{-19/8}
 \le C_{\rm reg}M_{\HH}y^{-3/8}
 \le C_{\rm reg}M_{\HH}.
\end{align*}
Therefore, for all $L/a\le r\le B$,
\begin{align}
 \left|\int_{L/a}^r\frac{X(\rho)F(\rho)}{W(\rho)}d\rho\right|
 &\le C_{\rm reg}M_{\HH}(r-L/a),\\
 \left|Y(r)\int_{L/a}^r
     \frac{X(\rho)F(\rho)}{W(\rho)}d\rho\right|
 &\le G C_{\rm reg}M_{\HH}\frac{r-L/a}{r}
 \le G C_{\rm reg}M_{\HH}.
 \label{eq:true-hartree-singular-green-product}
\end{align}
This is the needed \emph{physical product bound}: the apparent positive
power of the Kummer scale $a$ disappears \emph{after the singular mode
is multiplied back in}. Since $M_{\HH}$ contains the independently
established factor $\ell^{1/8}$, the bound retains a genuine
positive smallness factor instead of an artificial $a$-loss.
The estimate remains a \emph{candidate} pending strict CI
\texttt{ActualHartreeSingularGreenPhysicalSourceGain.lean};
it must not be counted as accepted before kernel replay.

The remaining part of $Y(r)A(r)$ is $Y(r)J_-(r_0)$,
to be estimated from the real two-channel Fredholm source and the
Wronskian trace; this is a distinct candidate. Neither estimate
replaces the true time derivative, the remote-region matching, nor
the complete nonlinear $s^{-9/4}$ Hartree residual.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Formal audit status at the latest checkpoint.}
The actual source-selected origin Wronskian outgoing trace
(the positive $r_0$ gain) has strict Lean Success
\texttt{42447225839}; its composition with the original two-channel
Fredholm source has strict Lean Success \texttt{42447225916}.
The full \emph{four-piece} physical Fredholm/Green/repair/zero
field is now strictly proved $C^1$ at all three interfaces:
\texttt{42447225879}. The singular Green transport identity
\eqref{eq:actual-singular-green-transport} is strictly replayed in
\texttt{42442134533}. These statements are verified auxiliary
components. The stronger \emph{scale-free product} estimate
\eqref{eq:true-hartree-singular-green-product} still awaits the
separate strict kernel replay and has not been counted.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The genuine regular homogeneous coefficient also avoids
an inverse matching-radius loss.}
For the singular origin basis $Y(r)=y(r)/r$, the original bounds
$|y(r)|+|y'(r)|\le 2G$ yield
\[
 |Y(r)|\le G/r,\qquad
 |Y'(r)|\le G/r+G/r^2.
\]
The exact Wronskian therefore converts its actual left-jet coefficient to
\begin{align*}
 |\gamma|
 &=\left|\frac{Y(r_0)DU-Y'(r_0)U}{W(r_0)}\right|\\
 &\le Gr_0|DU|+G(1+r_0)|U|\\
 &\le 2G\bigl(|U|+r_0|DU|\bigr),
 \qquad 0<r_0\le1.
\end{align*}
The already accepted two-channel weighted Fredholm shell theorem gives
$|U|+r_0|DU|\le C_{\rm F}N_{\rm F}$; thus
\[
 |\gamma|\le 2GC_{\rm F}N_{\rm F}.
\]
These are formulas for the \emph{actual regular origin homogeneous
coefficient}, not a freely adjusted amplitude.  The corresponding
new Lean module
\texttt{ActualHartreeRegularOriginCoefficientSourceBound.lean}
is a pending strict-CI candidate and is not counted as accepted.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The original nonlocal Hartree source as the selector input.}
Set
\[
 F_0(r)=-a^2\mathcal S_{\HH}(\ell,c,z)(ar),\qquad
 Q_{\HH}=C_{\rm phys}(B_z,A_z)\ell^{1/8}.
\]
The original measured physical Riesz source, without any abstract
forcing packet, satisfies
\[
 \|F_0(r)\|\le Q_{\HH}/r^2,\qquad
 F_0\in L^1_{\rm loc}((0,\infty);\C).
\]
For the genuine origin-normalized regular Green coefficient,
\[
 \left|\int_{L/a}^B\frac{Y(r)F_0(r)}{W(r)}\,dr\right|
 \le\frac83 GQ_{\HH}L^{-3/8}
 \le\frac83GQ_{\HH}\quad(L\ge1).
\]
The previously proved real two-channel Cramer compatibility selector
can therefore be applied to the \emph{original} Hartree source:
for each admissible fixed annular slice there is one unique
$p=(p_b,p_\omega)\in\R^2$ satisfying the actual coupled
Fredholm--Kummer compatibility equation, with
\[
 |p_b|+|p_\omega|
 \le\frac{C_{\rm sel}}a
 \bigl(N_{\rm F}+Q_{\HH}\bigr).
\]
The selected adjusted outer forcing is
\[
 F_{\rm adj}(r)=F_0(r)-p_bS_b(r)-p_\omega S_\omega(r).
\]
This is not the same as imposing compatibility on $F_0$ with
$p_b=p_\omega=0$; the latter would be an unjustified restriction.
The actual selected-speed and selected-upper-Kummer-jet
specializations are new strict-CI candidates
\texttt{ActualHartreeTrueSourceSpeedSelection.lean} and
\texttt{ActualHartreeTrueSourceSelectedUpperJet.lean}.
The time-dependent selected $p(s)$ and the physical all-region
residual $O(s^{-9/4})$ are \emph{not} consequences of a fixed-slice
Cramer theorem; they still require the real differentiated source,
compatibility motion, and nonlocal Hartree feedback estimates.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The genuinely selected speed-source Green product.}
The nonlinear baseline Hartree source and the two modulation-speed
sources have \emph{different} spatial decay and must be treated
separately.  For the actual scale and phase source directions
$S_b,S_\omega$, the verified inverse-radius bound is
\[
 |S_b(r)|,\ |S_\omega(r)|\le C_s\,a/r,\qquad L/a\le r\le B.
\]
The real selected speed is unique, with
\[
 |p_b|+|p_\omega|
  \le \frac{C_{\rm sel}}{a}
     \left(N_{\mathrm{Fredholm}}+M_{\HH}\right).
\]
Put $F_{\rm sp}=-p_bS_b-p_\omega S_\omega$. The original
fundamental-solution and exact Wronskian estimates give
\begin{align*}
 \left|\frac{X(r)F_{\rm sp}(r)}{W(r)}\right|
 &\le (G/r)\frac{C_s a}{r}
       (|p_b|+|p_\omega|)\,r^2\\
 &=G C_s a(|p_b|+|p_\omega|).
\end{align*}
Integrating \emph{from the same moving left endpoint} and multiplying
back by the genuine physical singular basis yields
\begin{align}
 \left|Y(r)\int_{L/a}^{r}
       \frac{X(\rho)F_{\rm sp}(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho\right|
 &\le G^2 C_s a(|p_b|+|p_\omega|)
       \frac{r-L/a}{r}\notag\\
 &\le G^2 C_s C_{\rm sel}
       (N_{\mathrm{Fredholm}}+M_{\HH}).
 \label{eq:true-selected-speed-singular-green}
\end{align}
This is the missing compensation of the selector's $a^{-1}$
by the physical Green integral's factor $a$. It does \emph{not}
incorrectly assign the baseline source's $(1+ar)^{-19/8}$
decay to the speed correction.

The underlying original-source unique-selector theorem has a strict
Lean Success (\texttt{42449888073}). The speed-source physical Green bound
\texttt{ActualHartreeSelectedSpeedSingularGreenGain.lean}
is strictly accepted (CI \texttt{42451607569}).
The composed true-source selector estimate in
\texttt{ActualHartreeSelectedSpeedPhysicalGreenNoScale.lean}
is now strictly accepted, with commit \texttt{378783e}
and independent CI \texttt{42453696842} (Success).
Equation \eqref{eq:true-selected-speed-singular-green}
is not yet a full three-region, time-dependent Hartree residual
of size $s^{-9/4}$.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The complete selected \emph{local} singular Green source.}
The genuine regular-origin Frobenius bound on the normalized annulus gives
\[
 \left|Y(r)\int_{r_0}^{r}
     \frac{X(\rho)F_0(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho\right|
 \le G C_{\rm reg} M_{\HH},\qquad
 C_{\rm reg}=1+(D+1)GB^2,\quad r_0=\frac La.
\]
The source densities are interval integrable by the original Hartree
Riesz estimates and the actual scale/phase source integrability
certificates. Thus the original Green map is linear for the
\emph{same} \(F_0,F_{\rm sp}\) and selected \(p\):
\[
 \int_{r_0}^{r}\frac{X(\rho)F_{\rm adj}(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho
 =
 \int_{r_0}^{r}\frac{X(\rho)F_0(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho+
 \int_{r_0}^{r}\frac{X(\rho)F_{\rm sp}(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho.
\]
Consequently,
\begin{align}
 \left|Y(r)\int_{r_0}^{r}
       \frac{X(\rho)F_{\rm adj}(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho\right|
 &\le GC_{\rm reg}M_{\HH}
       +G^2C_sC_{\rm sel}
          (N_{\mathrm{Fredholm}}+M_{\HH}).
 \label{eq:true-adjusted-hartree-local-green}
\end{align}
No additional moving-scale \(a\) or \(a^{-1}\) is introduced.
This precise local source-composition theorem is newly submitted as
\texttt{ActualHartreeTrueSourceSelectedAdjustedLocalGreenNoScale.lean}
(commit \texttt{dffbae9}). Its first strict CI \texttt{42453950339}
failed on a redundant non-progressing \texttt{dsimp} tactic at line 121;
this Lean tactic error was corrected by commit \texttt{58933d7}.
The restarted strict CI \texttt{42454972883}
is \emph{Success}: this local same-source, selected-speed Green bound
is now strictly accepted.
The local bound is distinct from the full right-endpoint coefficient
(which also contains the corrected Fredholm left trace) and from the
time-dependent complete nonlinear Hartree residual.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The full compatible selected singular Green component.}
The unique genuine real speed is also used in the corrected left
Fredholm sources \(F_{+,{\rm adj}}\) and \(F_{-,{\rm adj}}\), and in their
\emph{single} normalized Cauchy jet \(J=(U,DU)\). Write
\[
 N_{\rm adj}
 :=\|F_{+,{\rm adj}}\|_{L^2_{7/4}}
   +\|F_{-,{\rm adj}}\|_{L^2_{7/4}},\qquad
 C_{\rm tr}:=1+(D+1)G(B^2+2),
\]
and let \(C_{\rm F}\) denote the previously verified
two-channel Fredholm value/derivative source constant.
The original outgoing Wronskian trace obeys
\[
 |J_-(r_0)|\le C_{\rm tr}r_0 C_{\rm F}N_{\rm adj},
 \qquad
 \frac{r_0}{r}\le1.
\]
Because the genuine selected compatibility equation cancels the
\emph{right} singular obstruction, the original Green transport law is
\[
 A_B[F_{\rm adj}](r)
 =J_-(r_0)-\int_{r_0}^{r}
    \frac{X(\rho)F_{\rm adj}(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho.
\]
Combining the preceding same-source local estimate with the left trace
projection yields the explicitly scale-free physical singular component
\begin{align}
 |Y(r)A_B[F_{\rm adj}](r)|
 &\le GC_{\rm tr}C_{\rm F}N_{\rm adj}
       +GC_{\rm reg}M_{\HH}
       +G^2C_sC_{\rm sel}
           (N_{\rm Fredholm}+M_{\HH}).
 \label{eq:true-selected-full-singular-green}
\end{align}
This is an auxiliary proof undergoing a strict independent source audit in
\texttt{ActualHartreeTrueSourceSelectedFullSingularGreenGain.lean},
initial commit \texttt{047fc66}, whose first strict CI
\texttt{42454285470} failed on the old imported local module.
The updated CI \texttt{42454972909} reached this module
but failed only on a redundant no-op \texttt{dsimp}.
That tactic was removed in commit \texttt{5637b71};
strict CI \texttt{42455814268} exposed redundant unused proof binders.
They were removed without changing the bound in commit \texttt{89ecbc6}.
The replacement upstream \texttt{42456627583}, original-norm composite
\texttt{42456627562}, and one-original-source wrapper
\texttt{42456627579} all independently report \emph{Success} on
that same commit. These are strictly accepted fixed-slice auxiliary
statements, not a completed \S2.13 environment.
It controls the full right-endpoint singular Green coefficient
but not the separate regular Green component, interface-repair field,
their time derivatives or the complete nonlocal Hartree PDE residual.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Eliminating the adjusted Fredholm norm.}
The original Hartree Riesz forcing itself feeds the already established
two-channel source correction theorem.  In the same compatible speed,
let \(Q_{\rm adj}\ge0\) be the \emph{derived} adjusted outer envelope,
not an independent input.  The actual selected source theorem gives
\[
 N_{\rm adj}+Q_{\rm adj}\le C_{\rm adj}
                  (N_{\rm Fredholm}+M_{\HH}),
 \qquad Q_{\rm adj}\ge0.
\]
The precise genuine-Hartree-source specialization is encoded in
\texttt{ActualHartreeTrueSourceSelectedCorrectedNorm.lean}
(commit \texttt{d882d6f}), now \emph{strictly accepted}
by CI \texttt{42454532669} (Success).
Substitution into \eqref{eq:true-selected-full-singular-green}
reduces the right-hand side to the \emph{original} source size:
\begin{align}
 |Y(r)A_B[F_{\rm adj}](r)|
 &\le \bigl(GC_{\rm tr}C_{\rm F}C_{\rm adj}
            +GC_{\rm reg}+G^2C_sC_{\rm sel}\bigr)
         (N_{\rm Fredholm}+M_{\HH}).
 \label{eq:true-selected-original-norm-singular-green}
\end{align}
Both the source-selected speed and the corrected source envelope are
produced, not assumed, and all constants are independent of the
moving Kummer scale \(a\) within the stated annular range.
The same-source composite proof is
\texttt{ActualHartreeTrueSourceSelectedFullSingularOriginalNorm.lean},
initial commit \texttt{d4e8da4}.  Its first strict CI
\texttt{42454628449} failed because an imported local module
had not yet been corrected; redundant local simplification steps
were subsequently removed in commit \texttt{7b6da73}.
The corrected CI \texttt{42455841142} is \emph{strictly successful}
on commit \texttt{7b6da73}. It accepts this original-source norm
bound as an auxiliary theorem, not the complete \S2.13 parametrix.
The complete, time-differentiable four-piece physical Hartree field and
its \(s^{-9/4}\) nonlinear residual remain open.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Identifying both Fredholm channels with one physical source.}
To exclude the spurious possibility of choosing unrelated inner and
outer right-hand sides, fix the same physical interaction
\(\mathcal S_{\HH}(\ell,c,z)\) and take the canonical conjugated,
localized transmission channels
\[
 F_+:=\Ree\!\left(r\chi_{\mathrm{in}}\mathcal S_{\HH}(\ell,c,z)\right),
 \qquad
 F_-:=\operatorname{Im}\!\left(r\chi_{\mathrm{in}}
                         \mathcal S_{\HH}(\ell,c,z)\right),
 \qquad
 F_0(r)=-a^2\mathcal S_{\HH}(\ell,c,z)(ar).
\]
The previously accepted original slow-inner \(1/16\) weighted
source theorem yields \(F_\pm\in L^2_{7/4}\) for this \emph{same}
Hartree interaction. The genuine two-speed selector then
generates the full singular-component estimate
\eqref{eq:true-selected-original-norm-singular-green}
with these identical data.
The new source-locked Lean wrapper
\texttt{ActualHartreeOnePhysicalSourceFullSelectedSingularGreen.lean},
commit \texttt{47a907b}, has \emph{strictly passed}
CI \texttt{42455841135} (Success, commit \texttt{7b6da73});
it does \emph{not} infer the required continuity of \(F_+\) and
\(F_-\) from measurability alone.
These fixed-slice facts still do not produce the
time-dependent full original-PDE residual or the blowup rate.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The remaining genuinely time-dependent Cramer bridge.}
For an actual slow-time Hartree orbit, let \(b(s),w(s)\in\C\) denote
the two original compatibility columns and \(Z(s)\in\C\) its
unmodified, original-source obstruction.
If these \emph{actual} quantities are differentiable and the genuine
Cramer determinant has the verified lower bound, the selected real
pair satisfies
\begin{align*}
 b(s)p_b(s)+w(s)p_\omega(s)+Z(s)&=0,\\
 b(s)p_b'(s)+w(s)p_\omega'(s)
  &=-Z'(s)-p_b(s)b'(s)-p_\omega(s)w'(s),\\
 |p_b'(s)|+|p_\omega'(s)|
 &\le \frac{1024}{a(s)B^2}
      \bigl|Z'(s)+p_b(s)b'(s)+p_\omega(s)w'(s)\bigr|.
\end{align*}
The last implication is encoded in the existing Lean
\texttt{ActualHartreeCramerSpeedDerivativeBound.lean}.
However, this conditional implication alone does not establish
differentiability of the \emph{actual} Hartree interaction, its moving
Kummer coefficients or the full nonlinear source along an orbit.
The previously implemented
\texttt{ActualHartreeSelectedGreenTimeDerivative.lean}
treats frozen spatial source profiles; its hypotheses do not
constitute a proof of the true time-varying \(\mathcal S_{\HH}(s,r)\)
derivative. Those missing physical derivatives and cross-region
Riesz interactions remain essential for the relative residual gain.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Time differentiation without freezing the three physical
source profiles (strictly verified conditional bridge).}
Writing
\[
 F_{\mathrm{adj}}(s,r)=F_0(s,r)
   -p_b(s)S_b(s,r)-p_\omega(s)S_\omega(s,r),
 \qquad
 F_0(s,r)=-a(s)^2\mathcal S_{\HH}(\ell(s),c(s),z(s);a(s)r),
\]
the selected pair is not a new differentiable input:
\[
 (p_b(s),p_\omega(s))
  =\operatorname{Cramer}\bigl(b(s),w(s),Z(s)\bigr).
\]
Under the explicitly stated differentiability assumptions on the
\emph{original} sources \(F_0,S_b,S_\omega\) and on the compatibility
columns and obstruction, the exact product rule gives
\[
 \partial_s F_{\mathrm{adj}}
 =\partial_s F_0
  -p_b'S_b-p_b\,\partial_sS_b
  -p_\omega'S_\omega-p_\omega\,\partial_sS_\omega,
 \qquad
 |p_b'|+|p_\omega'|
 \le\frac{1024}{aB^2}
  |Z'+p_b b'+p_\omega w'|.
\]
This chain is formalized in
\texttt{ActualHartreeTrueMovingAdjustedSourceDerivative.lean}
(initial commit \texttt{c2ae7be}; strict proof correction
\texttt{3e278d0}), and the pinned trust-zero audit
\texttt{42458168170} has \emph{Success}. This is a conditional
source-calculus statement, not proof that all physical derivatives exist.
In contrast to the frozen-profile Green derivative, all three
physical sources depend on \(s\) in this identity. The assumptions
\(\partial_sF_0,\partial_sS_b,\partial_sS_\omega\) remain
\emph{visible}; their derivation from the one-orbit 3D Hartree
equation, differentiation of the moving Kummer basis, and
\(O(s^{-9/4})\) weighted residual and Lipschitz estimates are
separate, unsolved analytic steps.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Real time-varying Hartree source under the original regular Green integral.}
The original Green coefficient is linear in its forcing. Denote
\(
 B_K[F](r_0,B)=
 \int_{r_0}^{B} Y_K(x)F(x)/W_K(x)\,dx
\)
with \(K\) held fixed. Provided the genuine moving physical forcing
\(F_{\mathrm{adj}}(s,x)\) and its derived derivative from the preceding
Cramer bridge satisfy the dominated-parametric-integral hypotheses,
including a common \(L^1\) majorant for
\(Y_K\partial_sF_{\mathrm{adj}}/W_K\), then
\[
 \frac{d}{ds}B_K[F_{\mathrm{adj}}(s,\cdot)](r_0,B)
 = B_K[\partial_sF_{\mathrm{adj}}(s,\cdot)](r_0,B).
\]
Unlike the previous frozen spatial-shapes statement, the source,
both genuine modulation directions, and the unique real selected
Cramer pair all vary with \(s\). The strictly accepted Lean theorem
\texttt{ActualHartreeTrueMovingSelectedRegularGreenDerivative.lean}
(commit \texttt{cea8867}), consumes the existing, already verified
\texttt{ActualOriginGreenSourceTimeDerivative.lean} and the
real-source Cramer derivative module. The original source derivative
module's initial compile failed on a term-level let/minus parsing
ambiguity; this was corrected in commit \texttt{fc786e1}.
The Green derivative CI \texttt{42457157917} exposed a
function-level versus pointwise-function normalisation mismatch
in the imported source-derivative proof. After adding
\texttt{Pi.sub\_apply} and \texttt{Pi.mul\_apply}, the
same mathematical conclusion was resubmitted as commit
\texttt{c9a5989}. After an explicit real-versus-complex derivative
coercion correction (\texttt{6dbe26a}), the strict linter detected
two unused arguments in an auxiliary definition; these were repaired
by \texttt{2fdd446}. The resulting CI \texttt{42465956550}
reports \emph{Success} with pinned Lean and trust-zero axiom audit.
This accepted statement retains its explicit differentiation and
integrable-majorant assumptions.
The moving lower endpoint \(r_0(s)\), the moving Kummer basis,
and domination of the original full Hartree PDE time derivative
remain genuine separate analytic obligations.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The actual slow-inner moving endpoint.}
The physical Fredholm radius and normalized Kummer left interface are
\[
 L(s)=\ell(s)^{-1/16},\qquad
 r_0(s)=\frac{L(s)}{a(s)}.
\]
Without fixing any final Type-II blow-up rate, differentiation of these
\emph{actual} parameter expressions, provided
\(\ell(s),a(s)>0\), gives
\begin{align}
 L'(s)&=-\frac1{16}\ell(s)^{-17/16}\ell'(s),\\
 r_0'(s)&=\frac{L'(s)a(s)-L(s)a'(s)}{a(s)^2}.
 \label{eq:actual-hartree-moving-green-left-velocity}
\end{align}
At fixed \(s\), the genuinely selected original Hartree forcing
\(F_{\mathrm{adj}}(s,\cdot)\), when continuous on the relevant annulus,
has the original Green moving-left-endpoint contribution
\[
 \left.\frac{d}{dt}
 B_K\bigl[F_{\mathrm{adj}}(s,\cdot)\bigr](r_0(t),B)
 \right|_{t=s}
 =-r_0'(s)\,\frac{Y_K(r_0(s))
   F_{\mathrm{adj}}(s,r_0(s))}{W_K(r_0(s))}.
\]
This statement does not freeze the \emph{radius}; only the source and
basis are held at the reference slice to isolate that contribution.
The exact formula and the source-specific original Green FTC bridge
are now implemented as candidate
\texttt{ActualHartreeSlowInnerMovingGreenEndpoint.lean},
commit \texttt{bac3d6f}, with dedicated strict audit workflow
\texttt{hbl-true-hartree-moving-physical-endpoint.yml},
commit \texttt{9f54a71}. Strict pinned CI
\texttt{42466055866} reports \emph{Success}; no prescribed
Type-II exponent is used. This is a physical-parameter-derived
endpoint term, not the joint moving Green derivative.
Its combination with the evolving-source Green derivative and
the moving Kummer basis still requires additional analytic proofs.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{A quantitative no-\(a^{-1}\)-loss time budget for the
one real speed-selected Hartree source.}
The genuine two Cramer speed directions have
\(\|S_b(s,r)\|,\|S_\omega(s,r)\|\le C_s a(s)/r\) throughout
the actual normalized annulus \(L/a\le r\le B\). Combined with
the verified real Cramer derivative estimate, this gives
\begin{align}
 \bigl\|\partial_sF_{\rm adj}(s,r)\bigr\|
 &\le \bigl\|\partial_sF_0(s,r)\bigr\|
  +\frac{1024 C_s}{B^2r}
    \bigl\|Z'(s)+p_b(s)b'(s)+p_\omega(s)w'(s)\bigr\| \notag\\
 &\quad+|p_b(s)|\,\|\partial_sS_b(s,r)\|
  +|p_\omega(s)|\,\|\partial_sS_\omega(s,r)\|.
 \label{eq:true-hartree-selected-source-time-budget}
\end{align}
In particular, the Cramer inverse factor \(a^{-1}\) cancels against
the \emph{actual} \(a/r\) source envelope. The remaining factor
\(r^{-1}\) is explicitly retained and must be handled in the full
weighted source norms: the bound does \emph{not} assert that the
complete PDE residual is \(O(s^{-9/4})\).
The original-source pointwise time estimate has a strict Lean proof
in \texttt{ActualHartreeMovingSelectedSourceTimeBudget.lean},
initial commit \texttt{3b4f928}, repaired source commit \texttt{de54e97}.
The exact target theorem was re-audited under pinned Lean and Mathlib
in CI \texttt{42471590107} (\emph{Success}), with no new axioms.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Selected speed derivative in the ORIGINAL regular
Green coefficient.}
The already verified normalized Green speed-source estimates imply
\[
 |B_K[S_b](L/a,B)|,\ |B_K[S_\omega](L/a,B)|
 \le C_sG\,aB,
\]
where \(B_K\) is the original Wronskian-normalized coefficient,
rather than an artificial integral packet. Hence the real
two-column Cramer speed derivative satisfies
\begin{align}
 &\left|p_b'(s)\,B_K[S_b](L/a,B)
       +p_\omega'(s)\,B_K[S_\omega](L/a,B)\right|\notag\\
 &\quad\le \frac{1024C_sG}{B}
     \left|Z'(s)+p_b(s)b'(s)+p_\omega(s)w'(s)\right|.
 \label{eq:true-hartree-selected-speed-green-time}
\end{align}
The moving scale \(a\) cancels identically. This is a genuinely
Green-integrated estimate, stronger than the corresponding pointwise
control, and it does not require assuming a separate speed derivative
or a Type-II exponent. The direct strict proof is
\texttt{ActualHartreeSelectedSpeedGreenTimeNoScale.lean},
initial commit \texttt{eaaa182}, algebraic linter repair
\texttt{a615559}. The target theorem's pinned trust-zero
CI \texttt{42471592939} reports \emph{Success} after the
exact axiom audit was corrected in workflow commit \texttt{132efe7}.
The original-source, moving-endpoint and Kummer-basis time terms
must still be joined and bounded in the full physical field.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Exact source--moving-endpoint cross difference, with
the actual selected Hartree source.}
For one physical orbit let
\(
F_s(r)=F_{\mathrm{adj}}(s,r)
\)
and
\(
r_s=\ell(s)^{-1/16}/a(s)
\).
At a fixed origin Kummer basis \(K\), write
\(
G_K(u;F)=\int_u^B Y_K(r)F(r)/W_K(r)\,dr
\).
By exact interval-integral additivity and linearity, without
an asymptotic expansion,
\begin{align}
\mathfrak E_K(t,s)
 &:=
 G_K(r_t;F_t)-G_K(r_s;F_t)
 -G_K(r_t;F_s)+G_K(r_s;F_s)\notag\\
 &=\int_{r_t}^{r_s}
    \frac{Y_K(r)}{W_K(r)}
       [F_t(r)-F_s(r)]\,dr .
 \label{eq:true-hartree-green-source-endpoint-cross}
\end{align}
Under interval integrability of the original Green densities and the
\emph{explicit} local temporal Lipschitz assumption
\[
 \left|\frac{Y_K(r)}{W_K(r)}
       [F_t(r)-F_s(r)]\right|
 \le C|t-s|,\qquad
 r\in[\min(r_t,r_s),\max(r_t,r_s)],
\]
the exact physical remainder satisfies
\begin{align}
 |\mathfrak E_K(t,s)|
 &\le C|t-s|\,|r_t-r_s|.\label{eq:true-hartree-green-cross-bound}
\end{align}
These are formally checked by
\texttt{ActualHartreeMovingSelectedGreenMixedDifference.lean},
strict CI \texttt{42472592872} (\emph{Success}).
Since the endpoint derivative has already been derived from the genuine
\(\ell(s),a(s)\), \(r_t-r_s=O(|t-s|)\) locally. Therefore
\(\mathfrak E_K(t,s)=O(|t-s|^2)=o(|t-s|)\), and, in combination
with the separately verified source and endpoint derivatives,
the desired \emph{fixed-basis joint} formula is
\begin{align}
 \frac{d}{ds}G_K(r_s;F_s)
 &=G_K(r_s;\partial_sF_s)
   -r_s'\frac{Y_K(r_s)F_s(r_s)}{W_K(r_s)}.
 \label{eq:true-hartree-green-joint-source-endpoint-derivative}
\end{align}
The exact joint derivative has now passed pinned strict CI
\texttt{42473023783} (\emph{Success}) in
\texttt{ActualHartreeMovingGreenJointDerivative.lean}, corrected
commit \texttt{811ef15}. The same derivative is instantiated
for the \emph{one genuine original nonlocal Hartree source}, its
unique real Cramer speed and the actual slow-inner moving radius
in \texttt{ActualHartreeOnePhysicalMovingGreenJoint.lean},
strict CI \texttt{42473151794} (\emph{Success}).
The only remaining time regularity assumption of the mixed error
is a local Lipschitz bound on the ORIGINAL Green source density,
not an independent assumed vanishing mixed derivative.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Producing the local Green time Lipschitz estimate
from the genuine physical source time derivative.}
For each physical radius \(r\) on the moving interface interval,
suppose the full actual time-dependent selected source satisfies
\(\|\partial_\tau F_\tau(r)\|\le D\) for every \(\tau\) between
\(s\) and \(t\), and the original fixed-basis Wronskian multiplier
satisfies \(|Y_K(r)/W_K(r)|\le C_K\). The mean value theorem then
gives
\begin{align}
 \left|\frac{Y_K(r)}{W_K(r)}
          [F_t(r)-F_s(r)]\right|
 &\le C_KD\,|t-s|.
 \label{eq:true-hartree-green-density-time-mvt}
\end{align}
This \emph{derives} the Lipschitz input of
\eqref{eq:true-hartree-green-cross-bound}
from the actual source-time derivative, not from mere
measurability. The exact original-Hartree specialization is
\texttt{ActualHartreeSelectedGreenDensityTimeMVT.lean},
strict CI \texttt{42473401888} (\emph{Success}).
The further composition removing the independently stated
Green-density Lipschitz hypothesis is
\texttt{ActualHartreeOnePhysicalGreenJointFromDerivative.lean},
source commit \texttt{6242b8c}. Its exact-target, pinned
trust-zero CI \texttt{42473541248} has \emph{Success}.
Thus the source/endpoint joint derivative now follows from the
original physical Hartree source's quantitative time-derivative
assumptions and the genuine Wronskian multiplier, not from an
independently imposed Green-density Lipschitz property.

Even after this fixed-Kummer-basis time calculus, the original
3D nonlocal source's derivative bounds must be produced along
one PDE orbit; the \emph{moving Kummer basis} derivative,
full \(O(s^{-9/4})\) three-region nonlinear residual and direct
threshold Lipschitz remain open.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The complete compatibility-cancelled regular upper
Kummer coefficient from the moving Fredholm jet and joint Green term.}
Let \(r_0(s)=\ell(s)^{-1/16}/a(s)\), and let
\(U(s),D U(s)\) be the actual left Fredholm value and slope.
After the uniquely selected real speed cancels the singular
compatibility mode, the forced regular amplitude is
\begin{align}
 \gamma(s)
 &=
 \underbrace{
   \frac{Y_K(r_0(s))D U(s)-Y'_K(r_0(s))U(s)}
        {W_K(r_0(s))}}_{\beta_{\mathrm{reg}}(s)}
 +\underbrace{\int_{r_0(s)}^B
    \frac{Y_K(r)F_{\mathrm{adj}}(s,r)}{W_K(r)}\,dr}
  _{\mathcal B_K(s)}.
 \label{eq:true-hartree-moving-regular-gamma}
\end{align}
The already proved moving-jet derivative, together with the joint
original-source and moving-endpoint Green derivative, gives
\begin{align}
 \gamma'(s)
 &=\frac{N'(s)W(s)-N(s)W'(s)}{W(s)^2}
   +\int_{r_0(s)}^B
    \frac{Y_K(r)\partial_sF_{\mathrm{adj}}(s,r)}{W_K(r)}\,dr
   -r_0'(s)\frac{Y_K(r_0(s))F_{\mathrm{adj}}(s,r_0(s))}
       {W_K(r_0(s))},\notag\\
 N(s)&=Y_K(r_0(s))D U(s)-Y_K'(r_0(s))U(s),\qquad
 W(s)=W_K(r_0(s)).
 \label{eq:true-hartree-moving-regular-gamma-derivative}
\end{align}
At fixed basis parameters and fixed positive upper interface \(B\),
the regular upper Kummer jet has
\begin{align}
 \frac{d}{ds}
 \begin{pmatrix}
 X_K(B)\gamma(s)\\
 X_K'(B)\gamma(s)
 \end{pmatrix}
 &=
 \begin{pmatrix}
 X_K(B)\\
 X_K'(B)
 \end{pmatrix}\gamma'(s).
 \label{eq:true-hartree-moving-upper-regular-jet-derivative}
\end{align}
The direct composition of the moving Fredholm jet, the
strict joint Green time calculus and the regular upper-jet derivative
is \texttt{ActualHartreeMovingMatchedRegularUpperJet.lean},
original commit \texttt{9dd2ed4}, corrected commit \texttt{a6db90c},
dedicated pinned trust-zero CI \texttt{42474096247} (\emph{Success}).
The stronger instance using the \emph{single original quartic Hartree
Riesz source} corrected by the unique real Cramer speed and the actual
moving radius \(\ell(s)^{-1/16}/a(s)\) is strictly accepted in
\texttt{ActualHartreeOnePhysicalSelectedUpperJetTime.lean},
commit \texttt{1a3559c}, dedicated pinned trust-zero exact-target
CI \texttt{42474332557} (\emph{Success}).
The motion of the Fredholm left value and slope must still be
verified along the one original PDE orbit, and the spatial
Kummer fundamental pair remains fixed in this theorem.
The actual moving-\(K\) derivative and the full
\(O(s^{-9/4})\) original nonlocal Hartree PDE residual remain open.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Original nonlocal Hartree source: deriving its time
derivative from the same real core and complex radiation orbit.}
On one physical slice \(s\), put \(u=V+z\),
\(C(u)=|u|^2u\), and retain the three ORIGINAL radial
logarithmic Riesz potentials
\[
 P_{\rm m}=\mathcal I_2[|V+z|^4-|V|^4-|z|^4],
 \qquad
 P_z=\mathcal I_2[|z|^4],
 \qquad
 P_V=\mathcal I_2[|V|^4],
\]
with \(\mathcal I_2[f](r)=
 \frac{2\pi\kappa_{\rm H}}{r}
 \int_0^\infty \rho\,K_{\log}(r,\rho) f(\rho)\,d\rho\).
For the same actual fields \(V(s,r)\in\mathbb R\) and
\(z(s,r)\in\mathbb C\), the pointwise derivatives are
\begin{align}
 \partial_s|z|^4
 &=4|z|^2\Re(\bar z z_s),\notag\\
 \partial_s\bigl(|V+z|^4-|V|^4-|z|^4\bigr)
 &=4|V+z|^2\Re\bigl(\overline{V+z}(V_s+z_s)\bigr)
   -4V^3 V_s-4|z|^2\Re(\bar z z_s).
 \label{eq:true-hartree-three-quartic-densities-time}
\end{align}
Under explicitly stated integrability and a common majorant for
each ORIGINAL log-kernel weighted quartic velocity,
the dominated-parametric-integral theorem gives
\begin{align}
 \partial_sP_\alpha(s,r)
 &=
 \frac{2\pi\kappa_{\rm H}}{r}
 \int_0^\infty \rho\,K_{\log}(r,\rho)
       \partial_s f_\alpha(s,\rho)\,d\rho,
 \qquad \alpha\in\{{\rm m},z,V\},\quad r>0.
 \label{eq:true-hartree-three-riesz-potentials-time}
\end{align}
The \(z\) and mixed components are strictly verified by
\texttt{ActualHartreeRadialRieszTimeDerivative.lean}
(CI \texttt{42474757711}, \emph{Success}) and
\texttt{ActualHartreeMixedRieszTimeDerivative.lean}
(CI \texttt{42475283498}, \emph{Success}).
The real-core case is the same strictly verified quartic derivative
after embedding \(V\) into \(\mathbb C\), as made explicit in
\texttt{ActualHartreeFullInteractionTimeFromOrbit.lean}
(CI \texttt{42475826921}, \emph{Success}).

For the ACTUAL cubic multiplier, including at \(u=0\), the
calculated derivative is
\[
 \partial_s C(u)
 =2\Re(\bar u u_s)u+|u|^2u_s .
\]
This is checked in
\texttt{ActualHartreeLocalCubicTimeDerivative.lean},
CI \texttt{42475554508} (\emph{Success}).
The resulting COMPLETE three-potential interaction
\(
 \mathcal N_{\rm int}
 =P_{\rm m}C(u)
 +P_z[C(u)-C(z)]
 +P_V[C(u)-C(V)]
\)
has the exact time derivative
\begin{align}
 \partial_s\mathcal N_{\rm int}
 &=P_{\rm m}'C(u)+P_{\rm m}\partial_s C(u)\notag\\
 &\quad+P_z'[C(u)-C(z)]
       +P_z[\partial_sC(u)-\partial_sC(z)]\notag\\
 &\quad+P_V'[C(u)-C(V)]
       +P_V[\partial_sC(u)-\partial_sC(V)].
 \label{eq:true-hartree-complete-nonlocal-interaction-time}
\end{align}
The precise three-potential product theorem is accepted by
CI \texttt{42475714014} (\emph{Success}). More strongly,
\texttt{ActualHartreeFullInteractionTimeFromOrbit.lean},
CI \texttt{42475826921} (\emph{Success}), derives
\eqref{eq:true-hartree-complete-nonlocal-interaction-time}
from the primitive \(V_s,z_s\), actual quartic integrability,
and three genuine Riesz dominated-velocity bounds:
no assumed potential derivatives or independent full-source
time derivative remain.

\emph{Scope of this result:} the physical radial observation
point \(r\) is fixed. The same-orbit regularity assumptions and
integrable majorants are visible and must still be produced from
high-regularity Hartree dynamics. The moving observation
\(r=a(s)r_K\), moving Kummer fundamental pair and full four-piece
residual of order \(O(s^{-9/4})\), including the direct shooting
Lipschitz bound, are NOT asserted by
\eqref{eq:true-hartree-complete-nonlocal-interaction-time}.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Spatial derivative of the ORIGINAL three-dimensional
Riesz potential under the physical moving observation point.}
For the actual complex physical radiation at a fixed time \(s\),
set \(\rho_s(y)=|z(s,y)|^4\) and retain the original convolution
\[
 \mathcal P_3[\rho_s](x)
 =\int_{\mathbb R^3}\frac{\rho_s(y)}{|x-y|^2}\,dy .
\]
Instead of differentiating the singular kernel directly, the
pre-existing noncompact-convolution completion theorem transfers
the spatial derivative to the \emph{actual} quartic density:
\begin{align}
 D_x\mathcal P_3[\rho_s](x)[h]
 &=\int_{\mathbb R^3}
    \frac{D\rho_s(y)[h]}{|x-y|^2}\,dy ,
 \label{eq:true-hartree-threeD-spatial-gradient}
\end{align}
provided \(z(s)\in C^1\cap L^4\), \(Dz(s)\in L^2\) with
the displayed boundedness assumptions, and the genuine density
\(\rho_s\) together with \(D\rho_s\) admits compactly supported
smooth approximants in the explicitly required \(L^1\) and
uniform bounds. In this same setting, the physical observer
\(x(s)=a(s)x_0\) obeys
\begin{align}
 \left.\frac{d}{dt}
   \mathcal P_3[\rho_s](a(t)x_0)\right|_{t=s}
 &=D_x\mathcal P_3[\rho_s](a(s)x_0)
   [a'(s)x_0].
 \label{eq:true-hartree-threeD-moving-observer}
\end{align}
The original source density is used in
\texttt{ActualHartreeThreeDMovingObserverDerivative.lean}.
Its initial version (an independently specified density gradient)
passed CI \texttt{42476562067}; the strengthened version
\emph{derives} the quartic density gradient and its budgets
from the original field, using the previously verified
\texttt{HartreeQuarticGradient.lean}.
The exact strengthened theorem
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_threeD\_quartic\_moving\_observer\_from\_field}
is independently strict by CI \texttt{42476734964} (\emph{Success}),
source commit \texttt{3218ca9}, workflow commit \texttt{a962e99}.

The conclusion of \eqref{eq:true-hartree-threeD-moving-observer}
is the spatial observer term with the \emph{physical density time
frozen at \(s\)}. It is not asserted that the original half-line
radial logarithmic representation has already been identified with
this three-dimensional convolution in the formal proof chain.
That radialization identity, the simultaneous \(s\)-dependent
density and moving observer derivative, the moving Kummer basis,
and the full physical \(O(s^{-9/4})\) residual remain genuine
separate obligations.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Exact critical transport of the original Hartree
interaction from the moving observer to fixed Kummer radius.}
Write the full nonlocal interaction of one real radial core \(V\)
and one complex radiation \(Z\) as
\[
 {\mathfrak I}(V,Z)
 =\mathcal N_{\HH}(V+Z)-\mathcal N_{\HH}(V)
   -\mathcal N_{\HH}(Z),\qquad
 \mathcal N_{\HH}(U)=\bigl(|x|^{-2}*|U|^4\bigr)|U|^2U,
\]
where its original radial expression contains all three genuine
inverse-square quartic Riesz contributions. For each \(a>0\)
put
\[
 V_a(r)=a^{1/2}V(ar),\qquad Z_a(r)=a^{1/2}Z(ar).
\]
The exact nonlocal critical covariance gives
\begin{align}
 {\mathfrak I}(V_a,Z_a)(r)
 &=a^{5/2}{\mathfrak I}(V,Z)(ar),\label{eq:hbl-critical-full-mixed-scaling}\\
 \sqrt a\,\mathcal S_K(r)
 &={\mathfrak I}\bigl(V_{a,\ell,c},Z_{a,\ell}\bigr)(r),
 \qquad
 \mathcal S_K(r)=a^2 {\mathfrak I}(V_{\ell,c},Z_\ell)(ar).
 \label{eq:hbl-critical-original-source-fixed-radius}
\end{align}
Here \(\mathcal S_K\) denotes the unsigned scaled Kummer source
\texttt{actualHartreeLaplacianScaledKummerSource}; the forcing
used in the selected four-piece Green construction has the
\emph{opposite sign}, \(F_0=-\mathcal S_K\).
In particular, the moving observation point \(ar\) is absent
from the right-hand side of the second identity; \emph{all}
time variation is transferred into the actual rescaled core,
radiation and explicit prefactor. Both equalities, including the
original logarithmic radial Riesz kernel, are proved in
\texttt{ActualHartreeCriticalScaledInteraction.lean};
dedicated strict CI \texttt{42478335181} reports
\emph{Success} under pinned Lean 4.35.0-rc3.

For differentiable \(a(s)>0\) and differentiable original
fixed-radius scaled interaction
\(\widetilde F(s,r):={\mathfrak I}(V_{a,\ell,c}(s),
Z_{a,\ell}(s))(r)\), the exact product rule is
\begin{align}
 \partial_s\mathcal S_K(s,r)
  &=a(s)^{-1/2}\partial_s\widetilde F(s,r)
     -\frac{a'(s)}{2a(s)^{3/2}}\widetilde F(s,r),\qquad
 \partial_sF_0=-\partial_s\mathcal S_K.
 \label{eq:hbl-original-hartree-source-time-via-critical}
\end{align}
This does not assume a terminal Type-II power law.
The Lean producer of the unsigned \(\partial_s\mathcal S_K\)
(the actual forcing derivative is its negative)
\texttt{ActualHartreeTrueMovingOriginalSourceFromScaledOrbit.lean}
has strict CI \texttt{42478989692} (\emph{Success}),
which derives \(\partial_sF_0\), rather than taking it as
a source-derivative hypothesis. The next full physical-orbit
producer \texttt{ActualHartreeTrueSourceTimeFromPhysicalScaledOrbit.lean}
attempts to obtain \(\partial_s\widetilde F\) from the previously
verified full three-Riesz time differentiation, with only primitive
core/radiation \(C^1\) and original integrable velocity-envelope
inputs. Its independent strict CI must be checked before acceptance.

The concrete two-stage real core has
\[
 V_{a,c}(s,r)=a(s)^{1/2}V_{c(s)}(a(s)r),
\]
and its derivative, valid also across \(c(s)=0\), is
\begin{align}
 \partial_s V_{a,c}
 &=\frac{a'}{2\sqrt a}V_c(ar)
 +\sqrt a\Bigl[
       c'\,\partial_cV_c(ar)+a'r\,\partial_RV_c(ar)
       \Bigr].
 \label{eq:hbl-two-stage-core-time-tangent}
\end{align}
The expression uses the exact moment-locked cutoff parameter
tangent and spatial tangent, not a formal derivative of
\(|c|\) at zero. This has independent strict CI
\texttt{42479257326} (\emph{Success}) in
\texttt{ActualHartreeTwoStageCoreTimeTangent.lean}.
The true two-stage radiation
\(\sqrt{a(s)\ell(s)}\,z(s,a(s)\ell(s)r)\)
has a corresponding complete chain-rule module,
\texttt{ActualHartreeTwoStageRadiationTimeTangent.lean},
strict CI \texttt{42479553379} (\emph{Success}). All original time/spatial radiation
regularity and nonlocal quartic velocity majorants must still
come from the final physical PDE trajectory.
The moving Kummer basis and the full weighted nonlinear
\(O(s^{-9/4})\) residual are not inferred from these identities.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The actual real-compatible Hartree source under one
fixed-basis spectral Kummer correction.}
Let \(K_0\) denote the original origin-normalized Kummer pair at
\((\mu_0,\theta_0)\), and let
\(r_0=L/a\), \(F_0(r)=-a^2\mathcal S_{\rm H}(ar)\).
The genuine Fredholm two-channel compatibility condition determines
one unique real pair \(p=(p_b,p_\omega)\), giving
\[
 F_{\rm adj}(r)=F_0(r)-p_bS_b(r)-p_\omega S_\omega(r).
\]
The accepted original-source estimate yields a nonnegative \(Q_a\)
such that
\[
 \|F_{\rm adj}(r)\|\le Q_a/r^2,\qquad
 0\le Q_a\le C_{\rm sel}
   \bigl(N_{\rm F}+C_{\rm phys}\ell^{1/8}\bigr).
\]
Writing
\(
 \delta=b(\mu,\theta)-b(\mu_0,\theta_0)
\)
and using the \emph{same} original normalized Green operator \(G_0\),
set
\[
 u_0=G_0[F_{\rm adj}],\qquad
 u_1=G_0[\delta u_0].
\]
The genuine frozen Kummer differential equation implies the
\emph{exact} cancellation
\[
 {\cal L}_{\mu,\theta}(u_0-u_1)
      -F_{\rm adj}=-\delta u_1.
\]
The original physical source, with no separately prescribed
corrected-forcing supremum \(M\), first yields a valid but
suboptimal estimate with factor \(r_0^{-2}\);
this earlier bound was strictly checked by
\texttt{ActualHartreeOriginalSelectedSpectralSourceNormGain.lean},
CI \texttt{42485891640} (\emph{Success}).
Taking the supremum \(\|F_{\rm adj}(r)\|\le Q_a/r_0^2\)
\emph{before} the Green integral, however, discards an exact
physical cancellation.

Indeed, the original normalized Wronskian obeys
\(\|W_K(r)^{-1}\|=r^2\), so on the actual annulus
\[
 \left\|\frac{X_K(r)F_{\rm adj}(r)}{W_K(r)}\right\|,
 \quad
 \left\|\frac{Y_K(r)F_{\rm adj}(r)}{W_K(r)}\right\|
 \le C_KQ_a.
\]
Consequently the genuine original Green operator satisfies
\[
 \|G_0[F_{\rm adj}](r)\|+
 \|\partial_rG_0[F_{\rm adj}](r)\|
 \le 2C_K^2(B-r_0)Q_a
\]
with \emph{no explicit inverse left-radius factor}.
This sharpened Kummer result has strict CI
\texttt{42486794147} (\emph{Success}).
The second original Green integral
\(u_1=G_0[\delta G_0[F_{\rm adj}]]\)
then satisfies
\[
 \|\mathcal L_{\mu,\theta}(u_0-u_1)-F_{\rm adj}\|
 \le |\delta|^2
 [2C_K^2(B-r_0)][2C_K^3(B-r_0)]Q_a.
\]
This is the exact linear Kummer spectral correction,
strictly checked in
\texttt{ActualHartreeFixedKummerSpectralNoLeftRadiusLoss.lean},
CI \texttt{42486794135} (\emph{Success}).

Finally, the full physical Cramer/Fredholm source construction
supplies \(Q_a\le C_{\rm sel}(N_{\rm F}
+C_{\rm phys}\ell^{1/8})\), while
\(|\delta|\le|\mu-\mu_0|+|\theta-\theta_0|\).
For the \emph{same} original quartic Hartree source,
\emph{same} unique selected real speed and \emph{same}
normalized basis, the main quantitative result is therefore
\[
 \boxed{\begin{aligned}
 &\bigl\|\mathcal L_{\mu,\theta}(u_0-u_1)
             -F_{\rm adj}\bigr\|\\
 &\qquad\le
 (|\mu-\mu_0|+|\theta-\theta_0|)^2
 [2C_K^2(B-r_0)][2C_K^3(B-r_0)]\\
 &\hspace{52mm}\cdot C_{\rm sel}
 (N_{\rm F}+C_{\rm phys}\ell^{1/8}).
 \end{aligned}}
\]
The exact original-input, source-selected composition is
\texttt{ActualHartreeOriginalSelectedSpectralNoRadiusLoss.lean},
strict CI \texttt{42486875347} (\emph{Success}).
All three new independent audits used the pinned Lean compiler,
\texttt{--trust=0}, warnings as errors and the
\emph{exact target theorem} in both \texttt{\#check}
and \texttt{\#print axioms}, without additional axioms.

\emph{Scope:} the cancellation removes the explicit
\(r_0^{-2}\) originating from the old source supremum.
It does \emph{not} prove a uniform \(C_K\) as \(r_0\to0\);
the true Kummer basis may itself carry radius dependence.
Nor does this statement establish the physical modulation
decay of \(\delta\), restore the altered physical Cauchy
traces after the second Green correction, or estimate
the complete inner--Kummer--repair--remote nonlocal PDE
residual. These remain necessary to prove a positive
time-order step and the revised manuscript §2.13.
No Type-II concentration exponent has been assumed.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The original Wronskian spectral correction also
controls the complete two-coordinate boundary jet.}
The preceding quadratic PDE estimate is insufficient by itself
for a physical splice, since the second Green response changes
both the value and the radial slope. Write
\[
 E_1=2C_K^2(B-r_0),\qquad
 E_2=2C_K^3(B-r_0),\qquad
 Q_1=\delta G_0[F_{\rm adj}].
\]
The original inverse-square source estimate, applied \emph{before}
the first Green integration, gives
\[
 \|G_0[F_{\rm adj}](r)\|+
 \|S_0[F_{\rm adj}](r)\|
 \le E_1Q_a.
\]
Consequently \(\|Q_1(r)\|\le |\delta|E_1Q_a\).
A second application of the \emph{same} normalized Green operator
in its original value--slope form gives
\[
 \|G_0[Q_1](r)\|+\|S_0[Q_1](r)\|
 \le E_2|\delta|E_1Q_a,\qquad r_0\le r\le B.
\]
In particular, with the literal corrected jet
\(
 (u_{\rm corr},S_{\rm corr})=
 (G_0[F_{\rm adj}]-G_0[Q_1],
  S_0[F_{\rm adj}]-S_0[Q_1])
\), both estimates hold \emph{simultaneously}:
\[
 \boxed{\begin{aligned}
 &\|u_{\rm corr}(r)-G_0[F_{\rm adj}](r)\|
   +\|S_{\rm corr}(r)-S_0[F_{\rm adj}](r)\|
     \le E_2|\delta|E_1Q_a,\\
 &\|\mathcal L_{\mu,\theta}u_{\rm corr}(r)
      -F_{\rm adj}(r)\|
     \le |\delta|^2E_1E_2Q_a.
 \end{aligned}}
\]
The general source-class statement and its joint proof are implemented
in \texttt{ActualHartreeOriginalSpectralC1TraceNoRadiusLoss.lean}.
The dedicated strict GitHub CI
\texttt{42500440029} completed successfully with Lean
4.35.0-rc3, \texttt{--trust=0}, warning-as-error,
and a verified \texttt{\#print axioms} report containing only
\texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, and
\texttt{Quot.sound}, with no \texttt{sorryAx}.
The exact input assumption remains \(\|F(r)\|\le Q/r^2\);
it is supplied by the preceding selected original Hartree source.

\emph{Boundary condition caveat:}
this estimate bounds the changed jet but does not \emph{restore}
the original Fredholm left interface or outgoing right compatibility.
Moreover, \(C_K\) still depends potentially on the moving left
radius. The genuinely matched zero-left-Cauchy-jet correction
and the regularized-origin weighted Volterra bounds must be
linked before any time-positive full Hartree PDE claim.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{A radius-uniform source-density estimate in the
original regularized Kummer gauge.}
The physical singular mode \(Y(r)=y(r)/r\) cannot be bounded uniformly
as the moving left interface tends to zero. Its \emph{regularized}
counterpart \(y(r)\), together with \(x(r)=rX(r)\), is bounded on
the fixed origin interval by the original IVP estimate
\(
\|y(r)\|+\|x(r)\|\le2G
\).
For a source in the natural inverse-linear class,
\[
 0<r\le B,\qquad \|F(r)\|\le M/r,
\]
the exact normalized Wronskian identity gives directly
\[
 \boxed{
 \left\|\frac{X(r)F(r)}{W_K(r)}\right\|,
 \left\|\frac{Y(r)F(r)}{W_K(r)}\right\|\le GM.
 }
\]
Integration therefore yields
\[
 \|A_F(r)\|\le GM(B-r),\qquad
 \|B_F(r)\|\le GM(r-r_0),
 \quad r_0\le r\le B.
\]
The theorem is about the \emph{original} Wronskian densities and
integral coefficients, with no \(r_0^{-1}\), \(r_0^{-2}\), or
unweighted \(C_K\) premise.
It was strictly verified as
\texttt{originGreen\_inverse\_linear\_source\_regularized\_uniform}
and
\texttt{originGreen\_inverse\_linear\_coefficients\_regularized\_uniform}
in
\texttt{InverseLinearSourceRegularizedNoRadiusLoss.lean};
CI \texttt{42502314380} completed successfully. The archived
exact-axiom audit again contains only
\texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, and
\texttt{Quot.sound}, no \texttt{sorryAx}.
For a spectral correction \(F=\delta u\) this source class follows
from a genuine weighted field estimate
\(\sup_{r\in[r_0,B]}r\|u(r)\|<\infty\).
That \emph{field estimate for the real selected Hartree orbit},
and the corresponding zero-left-jet matched Volterra correction,
remain to be proved uniformly; they are not assumptions on
the main Type-II theorem.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The exact left-jet-preserving matched spectral response.}
The preceding isolated Green correction need not preserve the
original Fredholm Cauchy jet. With the \emph{complete} original
Cramer-matched field $w_0$ and $q=\delta w_0$, instead set
\[
 w_1(r)=w_{\rm match}[q,0,0](r),
 \qquad
 v(r)=w_0(r)-w_1(r).
\]
The zero left value and slope are imposed on the \emph{actual}
Cramer solution, not on a free homogeneous repair. Direct calculation
with the original Kummer operator gives
\[
 v(r_0)=w_0(r_0),\qquad
 \partial_rv(r_0)=\partial_rw_0(r_0),\qquad
 {\cal L}_{\mu,\theta}v-F_{\rm adj}=-\delta w_1 .
\]
The right outgoing defect is \emph{not} automatically zero:
\[
 {\cal T}_{B}[v]
   =-\mathfrak O_{[r_0,B]}[q,0,0]
   \quad\text{when}\quad
   \mathfrak O_{[r_0,B]}[F_{\rm adj},U,DU]=0.
\]
These exact identities are verified in
\texttt{ActualHartreeMatchedSpectralLeftC1Exact.lean};
strict CI \texttt{42502178507} returned \emph{Success}.

For the original regularized Kummer modes
$y(r)=rY(r)$ and $x(r)=rX(r)$,
the \emph{actual} zero-left Cramer coefficient satisfies the
cancellation
\[
 r\,w_1(r)
 =y(r)\{A_q(r)-A_q(r_0)\}+x(r)B_q(r).
\]
Thus no independent singular homogeneous coefficient is inserted.
If the first \emph{same} matched profile is controlled by
$\sup_{[r_0,B]}r\|w_0(r)\|\le H$ and
$\|x(r)\|,\|y(r)\|\le G$, then the exact Wronskian integral gives
\[
 \|r w_1(r)\|
  \le 2G^2(B-r_0)|\delta|H,\qquad
 \|\delta w_1(r)\|
  \le \frac{2G^2(B-r_0)|\delta|^2H}{r}.
\]
The first bound and the Cramer cancellation are strictly checked in
\texttt{ActualHartreeZeroLeftRegularizedVolterra.lean},
CI \texttt{42503893981} (\emph{Success}).
The corrected outgoing flux independently obeys
\[
 \left\|{\cal T}_{B}[v]\right\|
 \le G|\delta|H(B-r_0),
\]
with original regularized IVP bounds rather than a potentially
divergent unweighted singular Kummer basis.
Its strict audit is
\texttt{ActualHartreeMatchedSpectralUpperFluxWeighted.lean},
CI \texttt{42503498370} (\emph{Success}).
The three accepted strict audits list precisely
\texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, and
\texttt{Quot.sound}; they contain no \texttt{sorryAx}.

\emph{Remaining physical input:} the displayed $H$ must be
derived from the \emph{same} genuine source-selected Hartree trajectory
and its Fredholm Cauchy coordinates. The complete moving-basis estimates,
nonlocal four-region PDE error and true time-positive finite recurrence
also remain unproved; no physical Type-II exponent is assumed here.
The original §2.13 environment and final theorem are therefore
\emph{not} accepted by these separate strict Cramer lemmas.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Joint physical-source selection and spectral interface jet
estimate.}
The genuine nonlocal Hartree source and the single unique real
compatibility speed now produce, \emph{in one theorem}, both the
first-order \emph{isolated} Green--slope perturbation and the
second-order frozen-Kummer PDE residual. Writing
\[
  \Delta_{\mu,\theta}
    =|\mu-\mu_0|+|\theta-\theta_0|,
  \quad E_1=2C_K^2(B-r_0),\quad E_2=2C_K^3(B-r_0),
  \quad M_{\rm phys}
    =C_{\rm sel}(N_{\rm F}+C_{\rm phys}\ell^{1/8}),
\]
their simultaneously valid estimates are
\[
 \begin{aligned}
 &\|u_{\rm corr}-u_0\|
   +\|S_{\rm corr}-S_0\|
     \le E_2\Delta_{\mu,\theta}E_1M_{\rm phys},\\
 &\|\mathcal L_{\mu,\theta}u_{\rm corr}
       -F_{\rm adj}\|
     \le\Delta_{\mu,\theta}^{2}E_1E_2M_{\rm phys}.
 \end{aligned}
\]
No independent selected speed, adjusted source envelope or expected
time-decay rate is posited. This is precisely
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_original\_selected\_spectral\_C1\_and\_quadratic\_residual}
in \texttt{ActualHartreeOriginalSelectedSpectralC1Joint.lean},
strict CI \texttt{42503807361} (\emph{Success});
the pinned \texttt{--trust=0} audit reports only
\texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, and
\texttt{Quot.sound}, no \texttt{sorryAx}.
The fixed-annulus basis constant still cannot be assumed uniformly
bounded on shrinking moving annuli; the distinct \emph{matched}
left-jet-preserving construction and its outgoing flux must be
controlled as above before global four-region recursion.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Radius-uniform Cramer regular coefficient from the original left jet.}
The regular homogeneous component in the matched field is not a free
parameter: its coefficient is given by the original left Cauchy data
$(U,DU)$ and the same origin-normalized Kummer pair.  Writing
$Y(r)=y(r)/r$, the original Wronskian formula yields
\[
 \gamma_{\mathrm{reg}}
  =\frac{Y(r_0)DU-Y'(r_0)U}{W_K(r_0)},\qquad
 |W_K(r_0)^{-1}|=r_0^2.
\]
Consequently, \emph{before} applying a norm estimate, the factors
$r_0^{-1}$ and $r_0^{-2}$ cancel:
\[
 \begin{aligned}
 |\gamma_{\mathrm{reg}}|
 &=\left|r_0y(r_0)DU-
    \bigl(r_0y'(r_0)-y(r_0)\bigr)U\right|\\
 &\le G\{r_0|DU|+(r_0+1)|U|\}.
 \end{aligned}
\]
The equality of absolute values uses
$|W_K(r_0)^{-1}|=r_0^2$; it does \emph{not} assert equality of
the complex phases.  In particular, since
$rX(r)=x(r)$ for the regular physical mode,
\[
 \boxed{\;
 \sup_{r_0\le r\le B}
  |r\,\gamma_{\mathrm{reg}}X(r)|
 \le G^2\{r_0|DU|+(r_0+1)|U|\}. \;}
\]
There is no explicit inverse matching radius or
$\sup|Y|,\sup|Y'|$ on the right.  Here the multiplication
of the coefficient bound by $|x(r)|\le G$ is justified by
$G\ge0$ and
$r_0|DU|+(r_0+1)|U|\ge0$, since $r_0>0$;
thus the full coefficient upper bound is nonnegative, not merely $G$.
This estimate is implemented in
\texttt{ActualHartreeMatchedRegularJetNoRadiusLoss.lean}
with its accepted exact strict kernel/type/axiom audit
\texttt{42511347313} (\emph{Success}, commit \texttt{2d94b7ce}).
Both declarations depend on precisely the standard Lean logical axioms
\texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, and \texttt{Quot.sound};
no \texttt{sorryAx} or additional premise is present.  The two left jets
must still be controlled by the \emph{same} physically selected Fredholm
trajectory, and the source-driven nonlocal Green part, moving basis,
outgoing obstruction, three physical $C^1$ joins, and the four-region
positive-time-order residual are separate requirements.  In particular,
neither original \S2.13 nor the final Type-II blowup theorem is promoted
on the basis of this fixed-Kummer cancellation.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Derived first-profile envelope and corrected spectral bounds.}
Set $a=r_0$, $b=B$, and suppose the \emph{same compatible adjusted}
Hartree forcing $F_{\rm adj}$ satisfies
$\|F_{\rm adj}(r)\|\le Q/r^2$ on $[a,b]$, with its actual
Fredholm--Cramer left jet $(U,DU)$.  Let $w_0$ be the uniquely
Cramer-matched Kummer field.  Assuming the explicitly stated
normalized-basis budgets
$\operatorname{OriginBasisOn}(K,a,b,C)$ and
$\|y\|,\|y'\|,\|x\|\le G$ on $[0,R_K]$, the two terms of the
\emph{same} matched field obey
\[
 \|r\,\mathcal G_K[F_{\rm adj}](r)\|
 \le 2bC^2(b-a)Q,\qquad
 \|r\gamma_{\rm reg}X(r)\|
 \le G^2\bigl(a|DU|+(a+1)|U|\bigr).
\]
The singular homogeneous Cramer coefficient vanishes by the
\emph{selected} compatibility equation.  Hence the first-field
envelope is an \emph{output},
\[
 \boxed{\quad
 \sup_{a\le r\le b}\|r\,w_0(r)\|
 \le H_{\rm phys}:=
  2bC^2(b-a)Q+
  G^2\bigl(a|DU|+(a+1)|U|\bigr).\quad}
\]
The precise Lean correspondence is
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_matched\_source\_and\_uniform\_regular\_jet\_weighted}.
For $q=\delta w_0$, the \emph{same} zero-left-jet correction
$w_1=w_{\rm match}[q,0,0]$ therefore satisfies
\[
 \|r\,w_1(r)\|\le 2G^2(b-a)|\delta|H_{\rm phys},
 \qquad
 \|\delta w_1(r)\|\le
  \frac{2G^2(b-a)|\delta|^2H_{\rm phys}}r,
\]
and its outgoing mismatch is bounded, \emph{not} set to zero,
\[
 \left|\mathfrak O_{[a,b]}[q,0,0]\right|
 \le G|\delta|(b-a)H_{\rm phys}.
\]
These are the source-derived specializations in
\texttt{ActualHartreeMatchedSourceUniformRegularJet.lean}.
These three source-derived matched-profile, quadratic-defect and
outgoing-obstruction estimates now have their own strict pinned
kernel/type/axiom acceptance, CI \texttt{42511347298}
(\emph{Success}, commit \texttt{2d94b7ce}), with precisely
\texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice} and \texttt{Quot.sound}.
This acceptance is separate from the preceding general
zero-left Cramer identities and does not yet include the genuinely
time-dependent Hartree source-selected Fredholm orbit.
The original interval estimate uses the explicit $C,G,Q,U,DU$;
it does not assert uniform control of $C$ along a moving Kummer basis,
a differentiated-in-time matched field, or a four-region nonlinear
relative residual order.  None of those unproved requirements is
inserted as a hypothesis into the manuscript's Type--II claim.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Exact source-moment replacement for the hidden basis-budget loss
(November 8 continuation; new strict audit pending).}
Let $r_0=L/a>0$, let the frozen \emph{regularized} Kummer pair be
$x(r),y(r)$, and write its exact physical modes
$X(r)=x(r)/r$, $Y(r)=y(r)/r$.  For the same true adjusted source
$F_{\rm adj}$ and original, unswapped Green moments
\[
 A(r)=\int_r^B\frac{X(\rho)F_{\rm adj}(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho,
 \qquad
 B_-(r)=\int_{r_0}^r\frac{Y(\rho)F_{\rm adj}(\rho)}{W(\rho)}\,d\rho,
\]
the genuine Green response obeys the \emph{exact} identity
\begin{equation}
 r\,\mathcal G_{K_0}[F_{\rm adj}](r)
       =y(r)A(r)+x(r)B_-(r).
 \label{eq:exact-original-green-weighted-moments}
\end{equation}
Thus the globally bounded regularized origin modes
$\lvert y(r)\rvert,\lvert x(r)\rvert\le G$ give
\begin{equation}
 \lvert r\mathcal G_{K_0}[F_{\rm adj}](r)\rvert
 \le G\bigl(\lvert A(r)\rvert+\lvert B_-(r)\rvert\bigr).
 \label{eq:original-green-moment-no-hidden-C}
\end{equation}
The crucial point is the algebraic multiplication by $r$
\emph{before} taking the modulus.  No fixed-annulus
$\operatorname{OriginBasisOn}(K_0,r_0,B,C)$ appears, so no hidden
$C\sim r_0^{-2}$ has been smuggled into
\eqref{eq:original-green-moment-no-hidden-C}.

For the actual time-dependent Hartree-adjusted source
$F_s$ and frozen spectral change
\[
 \delta_s=b(\mu(s),\theta(s))-b(\mu_0,\theta_0),
\]
the exact ODE defect is
\[
 (\mathcal L_{\mu(s),\theta(s)}-\mathcal L_{\mu_0,\theta_0})
 \mathcal G_{K_0}[F_s]
      =\delta_s\mathcal G_{K_0}[F_s].
\]
Consequently the original PDE forcing has the rigorously reduced
pointwise spectral defect
\begin{equation}
 \bigl|r\delta_s\mathcal G_{K_0}[F_s](r)\bigr|
 \le
 \bigl(|\mu(s)-\mu_0|+|\theta(s)-\theta_0|\bigr)
 G\bigl(|A_s(r)|+|B_{-,s}(r)|\bigr).
 \label{eq:original-hartree-moving-moment-defect}
\end{equation}
The three exact-formula Lean statements, including specialization
to the \emph{literal} time-dependent nonlocal Hartree source,
are in \texttt{ActualHartreeExactGreenMomentsMovingSpectral.lean}.
Their pinned \texttt{--trust=0}, \texttt{warningAsError} and exhaustive
three-declaration axiom/type audit are launched in
\texttt{hbl-213-exact-green-moments-spectral.yml}; they remain
\emph{candidates} until the workflow reports strict success.

Equations \eqref{eq:original-green-moment-no-hidden-C} and
\eqref{eq:original-hartree-moving-moment-defect} are not a numerical
upper bound for $A_s,B_{-,s}$: those two \emph{true} integrals must
still be estimated from the same Cramer-selected physical source.
In particular, the previously accepted outgoing $A_s$ bound is not
automatically a bound for the distinct lower-endpoint $B_{-,s}$.
No expected temporal decay, fixed scaling law, four-region nonlinear
residual, or Type--II exponent is assumed here.  The revised original
manuscript count remains $11/13$.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Original real-speed regular Green moments at every intermediate
radius (new Lean theorem; strict replay pending).}
The original two modulation source directions satisfy the
verified sharper physical bound
\[
 |P_{\rm sc}(r)|+|P_{\rm ph}(r)|\lesssim a/r,
 \qquad L/a\le r\le B,
\]
not merely the coarse $r^{-2}$ envelope.  Inserting the exact original
Wronskian $\lvert W(r)^{-1}\rvert=r^2$ and
$\lvert Y(r)\rvert\le G/r$ into the \emph{lower-endpoint regular Green
moment} gives a bounded density, uniformly in the moving left endpoint.
The existing source theorem at a general upper endpoint $b$ proves
\[
 \left|\int_{L/a}^{b}\frac{Y(\rho)P_j(\rho)}
 {W(\rho)}\,d\rho\right|
 \le C_{\rm sp}G\,a\,b
 \qquad(j\in\{\mathrm{sc},\mathrm{ph}\}).
\]
Every intermediate radius $r$ satisfies $r\le B\le1$ and $r<R_K$;
hence applying this \emph{same} original theorem with $b=r$
yields the single-constant uniform estimate
\begin{equation}
 \sup_{L/a\le r\le B}
 \left|\int_{L/a}^{r}\frac{Y(\rho)P_j(\rho)}
 {W(\rho)}\,d\rho\right|
 \le C_{\rm sp}G\,a B,
 \qquad j\in\{\mathrm{sc},\mathrm{ph}\}.
 \label{eq:original-Hartree-speed-regular-all-radii}
\end{equation}
No $r_0^{-1}$ or artificial $\log(B/r_0)$ is generated.
The proof is formalized in

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Full original source-driven Green field: no hidden matching

 \sup_{L/a\le r\le B}

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Linear two-speed regular moment as the original Green integral

 F_{\rm sp}=-p_1P_{\rm sc}-p_2P_{\rm ph}.

 B_{F_{\rm sp}}(r)

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Actual Riesz source to frozen-moving spectral drift
without a shrinking-origin basis constant (Lean extension submitted).}
Fix the origin-normalized Kummer pair at $(\mu_0,\theta_0)$.
At a fixed physical epoch $s$ let $\mu(s),\theta(s)$ be the actual
spectral coefficients.  Set
\[
 \delta(s):=|\mu(s)-\mu_0|+|\theta(s)-\theta_0|,\qquad
 E_0:=\frac{16}{3}G^2 Q_{\rm H}L^{-3/8}.
\]
By the exact scalar coefficient identity and the literal source-driven
whole-Green estimate
\eqref{eq:original-hartree-full-physical-green-no-radius-loss},
\begin{align}
 &r\bigl(\mathcal L_{\mu(s),\theta(s)}
      \mathcal G_{K_0}[F_0](r)-F_0(r)\bigr)\notag\\
 &\quad=\bigl(q_0(\mu(s),\theta(s))
              -q_0(\mu_0,\theta_0)\bigr)
          r\mathcal G_{K_0}[F_0](r),\notag\\
 &\sup_{L/a\le r\le B}
 \left|r\bigl(\mathcal L_{\mu(s),\theta(s)}
      \mathcal G_{K_0}[F_0](r)-F_0(r)\bigr)\right|
 \le \delta(s)E_0.
 \label{eq:true-physical-frozen-spectral-drift}
\end{align}
The estimate is derived from the original nonlocal physical source,
not from an assumed norm of $\mathcal G_{K_0}[F_0]$; it is independent

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Same-source complete inhomogeneous Green field
without moving-radius loss (strict Lean replay submitted).}
Write
\[
 S=(G C_r C_f C_n+G C_p+G^2C_sC)(N_{\rm F}+Q_{\rm H}),
 \qquad
 T=\frac83 GQ_{\rm H}L^{-3/8}
   +C_{\rm sp}GBC_{\rm sel}(N_{\rm F}+Q_{\rm H}),
\]
where $C_r,C_p,C_f,C_n,C_s,C$ are the genuine source-derived
coefficients of the original outgoing singular Green theorem.
Uniqueness in the original two-real-channel compatibility system
identifies the selected velocities in the singular and regular
estimates; thus they refer to a single physical $F_{\rm adj}$.
Using the exact Kummer decomposition
$\mathcal G_{K_0}[F_{\rm adj}]=
Y A_{F_{\rm adj}}+X B_{F_{\rm adj}}$,
the origin-normalized identity $rX(r)=x(r)$ and $0<r\le B$,
\begin{align}
 \left|r\mathcal G_{K_0}[F_{\rm adj}](r)\right|
 &\le r\left|Y(r)A_{F_{\rm adj}}(r)\right|
       +|x(r)|\,\left|B_{F_{\rm adj}}(r)\right|
       \notag\\
 &\le B S+G T,\qquad L/a\le r\le B.
 \label{eq:original-true-selected-whole-green-no-loss}
\end{align}
This estimate has no independently assumed selected speed,

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Compatibility of the full Green bound with all three

  \mathcal C_{\rm physical}(p;F_0,F_+,F_-)=0,

 \begin{aligned}
  \partial_\rho u_{\rm four}(L)

 \end{aligned}

These are full differentiability statements at the three points, not

composition uses the original source continuity on the positive

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Original weighted Cramer matching, the zero-left
Volterra spectral correction, and the quadratic PDE defect.}
The source-selected complete \emph{inhomogeneous} Green estimate
and the forced regular Cramer homogeneous component combine as
follows. Given the original compatible Fredholm jet \((U,DU)\)
and the actual adjusted Hartree forcing \(F_{\rm adj}\), let
\[
 u_0(r)=\mathcal G^{\rm match}_{K_0}
       [F_{\rm adj};U,DU](r),\qquad
 H_G=\sup_{r_0\le r\le B}
     r\,|\mathcal G_{K_0}[F_{\rm adj}](r)|.
\]
The original Cramer obstruction equation makes its singular
homogeneous coefficient exactly zero, and the normalized Wronskian
cancels all explicit negative powers of \(r_0\) in its
regular homogeneous coefficient. Therefore
\begin{equation}
 \sup_{r_0\le r\le B}r|u_0(r)|
 \le H_G+G^2\left(r_0|DU|+(r_0+1)|U|\right)
 =:H_{\rm match}.
 \label{eq:hbl-selected-weighted-original-matched}
\end{equation}
This is the \emph{original} Cramer and Fredholm solution, not an
independently supplied regular homogeneous amplitude.
The abstract true-Green-to-matched proof is strictly checked in
\texttt{ActualHartreeMatchedFromWeightedOriginalGreenCramer.lean},
CI \texttt{42527710745} (\emph{Success}), with pinned
\texttt{--trust=0}, a zero-length \texttt{strict.log}, and only
the three standard Lean axioms. Its specialization to the literal
source-selected Hartree field is an additional formal composite,
not automatically certified by that single CI.

Write the original Wronskian coefficient integrals for an
inverse-linear source \(q\) as
\[
 A_q(r)=\int_r^B \frac{X(s)q(s)}{W_K(s)}\,ds,\qquad
 B_q(r)=\int_{r_0}^r\frac{Y(s)q(s)}{W_K(s)}\,ds .
\]
The exact zero-left-Cauchy-data matched solution
\(u_1=\mathcal G^{\rm match}_{K_0}[q;0,0]\)
has singular coefficient \(-A_q(r_0)\) and vanishing
regular homogeneous coefficient. For the normalized origin
IVP basis \(y=rY\), \(x=rX\), this gives the \emph{identity}
\[
 \boxed{r u_1(r)=
  y(r)\bigl(A_q(r)-A_q(r_0)\bigr)+x(r) B_q(r).}
\]
Since the exact physical Wronskian satisfies
\(\lvert W_K^{-1}(r)\rvert=r^2\), if the two
regularized origin IVP modes satisfy the \emph{individual} bounds
\(\sup_{0\le r\le B}|x(r)|\le G\) and
\(\sup_{0\le r\le B}|y(r)|\le G\), and
\(|q(r)|\le M/r\), both coefficient densities are bounded by
\(GM\). Hence
\begin{equation}
 \sup_{r_0\le r\le B}r|u_1(r)|
 \le 2G^2(B-r_0)M .
 \label{eq:hbl-original-zero-left-volterra-weighted}
\end{equation}
Unlike a direct physical-basis estimate, this bound has no
\(r_0^{-1}\) or \(r_0^{-2}\) loss. The actual spectral-response
source \(q=\delta u_0\) has \(M=|\delta|H_{\rm match}\).
The matched correction \(u_{\rm corr}=u_0-u_1\)
thus preserves its \emph{literal} left Cauchy data and obeys
\[
 \begin{aligned}
 u_{\rm corr}(r_0)&=U,&
 \partial_r u_{\rm corr}(r_0)&=DU,\\
 \mathcal L_\delta u_{\rm corr}-F_{\rm adj}
   &=-\delta u_1,&
 \sup_{r_0\le r\le B}
 r|\mathcal L_\delta u_{\rm corr}-F_{\rm adj}|
   &\le 2G^2(B-r_0)|\delta|^2H_{\rm match}.
 \end{aligned}
\]
For the physical upper join one must \emph{recompute} the repair
using
\[
 (U_B^{\rm corr},D_B^{\rm corr})
  :=\bigl(u_{\rm corr}(B),\partial_r u_{\rm corr}(B)\bigr),
\]
not the old uncorrected jet. The corrected repair field then has
the candidate exact derivatives
\[
 \partial_yu_{\rm splice}(aB)
  =a^{-1}D_B^{\rm corr},\qquad
 \partial_yu_{\rm splice}(2aB)=0.
\]
The zero-left Volterra identity, its weighted estimate, the
left-preserving quadratic residual, and the actual re-matched
outer \(C^1\) statements have separate Lean modules and
strict workflow replays. A full main-chain acceptance requires
all these replays to succeed and to be composed with the
\emph{same source-selected} \(p\) and Fredholm jet; the
time-dependent four-region quartic Hartree residual, threshold
Lipschitz and dynamically selected Type-II rate remain open.




\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Same source-selected spectral defect and corrected
three-interface field.}
The last fixed-time matching step is to use \emph{one and the same}

original two-component Fredholm compatibility equation produced by

\[
 F_0=-\mathcal S_{\mathrm H}^{\mathrm{phys}},
 \qquad
 F_{\mathrm{adj}}
    =F_0-p_1\mathcal P_{\mathrm{scale}}
          -p_2\mathcal P_{\mathrm{phase}}.
\]
With the genuine adjusted inner Cauchy jet

\[
 H_{\mathrm{phys}}
  = H_G+G^2\bigl(r_0|D U|+(r_0+1)|U|\bigr),
 \qquad
 v_d=u_0-\mathcal G_{K_0}^{\mathrm{match}}[d u_0;0,0].
\]
Here $H_G$ is the explicitly derived source-selected whole
inhomogeneous Green bound above, not an additional hypothesis.
The shortest proposed composite has the simultaneous conclusions
\[
 \begin{aligned}
 v_d(r_0)&=U,&
 \partial_r v_d(r_0)&=D U,\\
 \sup_{r_0\le r\le B}
  r\,\bigl|\mathcal L_d v_d-F_{\mathrm{adj}}\bigr|
   &\le 2G^2(B-r_0)|d|^2H_{\mathrm{phys}},&
 \partial_\rho U_d^{\mathrm{four}}(L)
   &=\partial_\rho u_{\mathrm{inner}}(L),\\
 \partial_\rho U_d^{\mathrm{four}}(aB)
   &=a^{-1}\partial_r v_d(B),&
 \partial_\rho U_d^{\mathrm{four}}(2aB)&=0.
 \end{aligned}
\]
The crucial change at the outer transmission radius is that the
repair uses the \emph{spectrally corrected} value and derivative at

in
\texttt{ActualHartreeTrueSelectedSpectralQuadraticThreeC1.lean};
the dedicated pinned CI workflow is

Neither $d(s)$ nor its desired decay, the moving Kummer transport,

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Strictly verified right-hand $C^1$ repair of the
genuine spectral-corrected original Kummer profile.}
Fix the original Cramer matched field
\(u_0=\mathcal G^{\mathrm{match}}_{K_0}[F;U,DU]\) and its
zero-left-Cauchy-data first spectral correction
\(u_1=\mathcal G^{\mathrm{match}}_{K_0}[d u_0;0,0]\).
The physical continuation and the actual new endpoint jets are
\[
  w=u_0-u_1,\qquad
  v(y)=w(y/a),\qquad
  U_B=w(B),\quad D_B=w'(B).
\]
Let \(\operatorname{Rep}_{a,B}[U_B,D_B]\) denote the original smooth
physical Kummer interface repair, built from precisely these new
jet values. The literal corrected right-hand splice is
\[
 \mathcal U(y)=
 \begin{cases}
     v(y),&y\le aB,\\
     \operatorname{Rep}_{a,B}[U_B,D_B](y),
       &aB<y\le 2aB,\\
     0,&y>2aB.
 \end{cases}
\]
For \(a>0\), \(0<r_0\le B<\mathrm{scale}\), and the original
continuous physical source in the positive Kummer domain,
the genuine two-sided derivative is proved at both interfaces:
\[
  \boxed{
    \mathcal U'(aB)=a^{-1}D_B,\qquad
    \mathcal U'(2aB)=0.}
\]
The exact original source and its zero-left Green correction determine
\(U_B,D_B\); they are not freely prescribed independent data.
This isolated outer-pair theorem is
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_spectral\_corrected\_physical\_two\_outer\_C1\_joins},
