source-selected first Fredholm interface on one dynamic orbit.
It does not give a positive time-order full-Hartree PDE bound or
the dynamically derived Type-II exponent.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Cramer--Volterra derivative cancellation at the moving left interface (strict replay pending).}
The previous zero-left data matching yields an identity for the radial
\emph{derivative}, not merely a bound on the value. For the same genuine
original Green source \(q\), let
\[
 A_q(r)=\int_r^B \frac{X(t)q(t)}{W_K(t)}\,dt,\qquad
 B_q(r)=\int_{r_0}^r \frac{Y(t)q(t)}{W_K(t)}\,dt.
\]
Writing \(y=rY,\ x=rX\) for the original regularized IVP modes,
the exact Cramer coefficients give the two algebraic identities
\[
 \begin{aligned}
 r u_1(r)&=y(r)\,[A_q(r)-A_q(r_0)]+x(r)B_q(r),\\
 r^2 u_1'(r)&=
 [r y'(r)-y(r)]\,[A_q(r)-A_q(r_0)]\\
 &\quad+[r x'(r)-x(r)]\,B_q(r).
 \end{aligned}
\]
If \(\|q(r)\|\le M/r\) and the true IVP bounds are
\(
\|y(r)\|,\|y'(r)\|,\|x(r)\|,\|x'(r)\|\le G
\)
on the common fixed regularized interval, the same original Wronskian
estimates give
\[
 \boxed{
 \|r u_1(r)\|+\|r^2 u_1'(r)\|
 \le 2(B+2)G^2(B-r_0)M,\quad r_0\le r\le B.}
\]
For the \emph{literal} spectral correction \(q=\delta u_0\),
the weighted first-profile estimate yields
\(M=|\delta|H_{\mathrm{match}}\), not a separately introduced
correction-source constant. Under the actual positive-radius source
continuity, the second bound concerns the true classical derivative
of the original matched Green solution. In the physical variable
\(y=ar\), the derivative is \(a^{-1}\partial_r u_1(y/a)\);
no dynamical bound on this rescaling factor or time order is claimed.

The exact candidate Lean modules are
\texttt{ActualHartreeZeroLeftRegularizedVolterraSlope.lean}
and \texttt{ActualHartreeMatchedZeroLeftSpectralWeightedC1.lean}.
Their independent strict replays are GitHub Actions
\texttt{42530358097} and \texttt{42530472494}.
\emph{These statements must remain unaccepted in the original
manuscript ledger until successful exact-target \texttt{--trust=0}
compilation and transitive axiom audit.}

 \left(U_{\mathrm{corr}}^{\mathrm{four}}\right)'(L)

\]

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{From an actual selected Hartree source time derivative
to the moving Green cross term.}
Write
\[
 F(t,r)=-a(t)^2\mathcal S_{\HH}
    (\ell(t),c(t),z(t);a(t)r)
       -p_1(t)\mathcal P_1(t,r)-p_2(t)\mathcal P_2(t,r),
 \quad
 R(t)=\frac{\ell(t)^{-1/16}}{a(t)},
\]
where \(p(t)\in\R^2\) is the unique real compatibility selector.
For the original normalized Wronskian density
\(g(t,r)=Y(r)F(t,r)/W_K(r)\), the genuinely mixed
source--endpoint increment is, exactly,
\[
 \begin{aligned}
  \mathcal C(s,t)
  :={}&G(R(t),F(t))-G(R(s),F(t))\\
     &-G(R(t),F(s))+G(R(s),F(s))\\
   ={}&\int_{R(t)}^{R(s)}
             \bigl(g(t,r)-g(s,r)\bigr)\,dr .
 \end{aligned}
\]
On a common admissible time interval \(s\le t\), a derived uniform
bound on the \emph{actual selected density derivative},
\[
  \bigl|\partial_\tau g(\tau,r)\bigr|\le C_{\rm dyn},
 \qquad
 \tau\in[s,t],\quad
 r\in[R(t),R(s)]_{\rm unoriented},
\]
implies by the vector-valued fundamental theorem of calculus and
the same original interval integral inequality
\begin{equation}
  |\mathcal C(s,t)|
   \le C_{\rm dyn}|t-s|\,|R(t)-R(s)|.
 \label{eq:actual-hartree-moving-green-derived-cross}
\end{equation}
The new Lean proof candidate
\texttt{ActualHartreeMovingSelectedGreenCrossFromPhysicalTimeDerivative.lean}
(revisions \texttt{00dad2b9}, \texttt{74c06cfd})
replaces a \emph{freestanding time-Lipschitz assumption} with
this transparent sufficient derivative obligation for the actual
selected Hartree source; its pinned audit was submitted as
GitHub Actions \texttt{42532008498}. A bound on
\(\partial_\tau g\) derived from the complete original Hartree
orbit, moving Kummer basis and modulation equations is
\emph{not yet proved}; accordingly
\eqref{eq:actual-hartree-moving-green-derived-cross}
does not assert the required positive-time-order estimate
for the four-region PDE remainder.

The original verified generic corrected Fredholm--Kummer--repair
three-interface \(C^1\) proof passed in \texttt{42530313725}.
A later attempted simplification of its pullback-derivative
elaboration regressed at line 98 in \texttt{42531033761};
the exact earlier strictly successful proof step was restored
in commit \texttt{cd569084}. This is a proof elaboration
repair, not a new PDE estimate. Until the whole source-selected
time-dependent residual and finite recursion are proved,
the strict original-environment count is still \(11/13\).

\noindent\textbf{Strict source-selected whole Green and matched weighted

For the \emph{actual} scaled inverse-square quartic Hartree forcing

\[
 F_{\mathrm{adj}}=F_0-p_1S_{\mathrm{scale}}
                       -p_2S_{\mathrm{phase}},\qquad
 r_0=L/a.
\]
The complete original Green coefficient decomposition, including both

\[
 \|r\,G_{K_0}[F_{\mathrm{adj}}](r)\|
   \le H_G,\qquad r_0\le r\le B.
\]
Here \(H_G\) is the \emph{explicit source-derived expression} in

 \sup_{r_0\le r\le B}r\,

 \le H_G+G^2\bigl(r_0|DU|+(r_0+1)|U|\bigr).\;}

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Original critically scaled Hartree source and actual
Cramer speed: a joint time-derivative composition.}
The moving baseline source is not a freely differentiated
forcing. The exact critical Riesz scaling already established
for the original orbit is
\[
 F_{\rm old}(t,r)=a(t)^{-1/2}\,\mathcal I_{\rm crit}(t,r).
\]
For a differentiable physical scale \(a(t)>0\) and the
\emph{original} fixed-observer nonlocal Hartree interaction,
\[
 \begin{aligned}
 \partial_tF_{\rm old}
 &= -\frac{a'}{2a^{3/2}}\mathcal I_{\rm crit}
       +a^{-1/2}\partial_t\mathcal I_{\rm crit},\\
 F_{\rm adj}
 &= -F_{\rm old}-p_1 S_1-p_2 S_2,\\
 \partial_tF_{\rm adj}
 &= -\partial_tF_{\rm old}
    -p_1'S_1-p_1\partial_tS_1
    -p_2'S_2-p_2\partial_tS_2 .
 \end{aligned}
\]
The derivatives \(p_1'\), \(p_2'\) are \emph{outputs} of the
real Cramer-determinant differentiation, rather than independent
inputs. The new compositional Lean target
\texttt{actual_Hartree_true_selected_source_derivative_from_scaled_orbit}
in
\texttt{ActualHartreeSelectedSourceDerivativeFromScaledOrbit.lean}
was submitted at commit \texttt{a8cf1827} with a separate
fixed-dependency, exact-type and transitive-axiom workflow
\texttt{42532639028}, initially pending.
It removes the need to assume the derivative of \(F_{\rm old}\)
as a separate auxiliary input, but still requires producing
\(\partial_t\mathcal I_{\rm crit}\) and its weighted bounds from
the \emph{actual nonlocal PDE orbit}. Thus it does not
assert any time-positive residual rate or the Type--II exponent.

\[
 \mathfrak R[\Phi_n]-\mathfrak R[\Phi_0]
 =\sum_{j=1}^{n}

\]

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{A source-specific radial energy slope, without upgrading
the original source to an inverse-linear envelope (strict replay pending).}
The preceding unweighted Volterra lemma applies only to the
\emph{inverse-linear} class $|q(r)|\le M/r$.
It cannot be substituted, with a matching-radius-independent $M$, for
the \emph{complete original} quartic Hartree source
\[
 F_0(r)=-a^2\mathcal S_{\HH}(ar),\qquad
 |F_0(r)|\le Q/r^2,\qquad
 Q=C_{\mathrm{phys}}(B_z,A_z)\ell^{1/8}.
\]
For this original Riesz forcing the appropriate coordinate for radial
three-dimensional energy is instead $r|\partial_r w_0(r)|$,
since $r^2|\partial_r w_0|^2$ is the radial energy density.
Let $w_0=\G_{r_0,B}^{\,\mathrm{zero-left}}[F_0]$,
$r_0=L/a$, and use the actual physical origin-normalized Kummer
modes $X=x/r$ and $Y=y/r$.  Set
\[
 J=1+(D+1)GB^2,\qquad
 T=\frac83\,GQL^{-3/8}.
\]
The true original source and the exact physical Wronskian yield
\begin{align*}
  \left|\frac{X(\rho)F_0(\rho)}{W_K(\rho)}\right|
  &\le JQ,&
  |A_{F_0}(r)-A_{F_0}(r_0)|
  &\le JQ(r-r_0),\\
  |B_{F_0}(r)|
  &\le T,&
  |r y'(r)-y(r)|
  &\le (B+1)G,\\
  |r x'(r)-x(r)|
  &\le 2(D+1)Gr^3.
\end{align*}
The $B$-moment bound here is the \emph{actual}
$L^{-3/8}$ Riesz tail estimate, not an independently imposed bound.
The exact, zero-left Wronskian identity then gives
\begin{align*}
 r^2 w_0'(r)
 &=\bigl(ry'(r)-y(r)\bigr)
     \bigl(A_{F_0}(r)-A_{F_0}(r_0)\bigr)
    +\bigl(rx'(r)-x(r)\bigr)B_{F_0}(r),\\
 \boxed{\quad
 r|w_0'(r)|
 &\le (B+1)GJQ+2(D+1)GB^2T,\qquad r_0\le r\le B.
 \quad}
\end{align*}
In particular no inverse power of $r_0$ is introduced.
This addresses the \emph{original Riesz} part of the zero-left
radial energy estimate. The selected modulation-generator part and
the homogeneous inner-jet component still require the same-speed
composition; the complete four-domain time-dependent nonlinear PDE
residual and genuine finite induction remain open.

The source-specific Lean implementation is
\texttt{ActualHartreeOriginalRieszZeroLeftEnergySlope.lean},
submitted at commit \texttt{95fb3ec6} and strengthened at
\texttt{5bc59e00} by deriving interval moment integrability
directly from the original Hartree forcing, without an
additional source-continuity premise, with a separate locked
\texttt{--trust=0} and exact transitive-axiom workflow.
Until the dedicated CI is complete and its original evidence is
audited, the displayed theorem is a \emph{proof candidate}, not a
newly certified assertion, and cannot increase the original count
above $11/13$.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Full compatible Kummer radial slope: the indispensable
outgoing Fredholm trace (new exact proof candidate).}
The zero-left source bound alone does not control the physical matched
slope: compatibility cancels the additional homogeneous singular
coefficient, but the outgoing source integral at $r_0$ remains equal
to the original Fredholm trace $J_-(r_0)$.
Let $F=F_{\rm adj}$ and define
\[
 J_-:=\frac{U X'(r_0)-DU X(r_0)}{W_K(r_0)},
 \qquad
 \gamma:=\frac{Y(r_0)DU-Y'(r_0)U}{W_K(r_0)}.
\]
Here $X,Y$ are the \emph{physical} Kummer basis; in the original
normalization $J_-=\operatorname{outgoingTrace}(K,r_0,U,DU)$.
With $\mathcal S_K[F;0,0]$ the zero-left Cramer slope,
the correct exact algebraic identity is
\[
 \boxed{\quad
 \mathcal S_K[F;U,DU](r)=
   \mathcal S_K[F;0,0](r)
   +J_-\,Y'(r)+\gamma\,X'(r).
 \quad}
\]
The term $J_-Y'(r)$ must \emph{not} be omitted. Since
\[
 |J_-|\le C_{\rm tr}r_0 N_{\rm adj},\quad
 |r^2Y'(r)|\le(B+1)G,\quad
 |X'(r)|\le2(D+1)Gr,\quad
 |\gamma|\le G\{r_0|DU|+(r_0+1)|U|\},
\]
the ratio $r_0/r\le1$ supplies the crucial cancellation:
\begin{align*}
 r\,|\mathcal S_K[F;U,DU](r)|
 &\le r\,|\mathcal S_K[F;0,0](r)|
    +(B+1)G C_{\rm tr}N_{\rm adj}\\
 &\quad+2(D+1)G^2 B^2
         \{r_0|DU|+(r_0+1)|U|\}.
\end{align*}
No inverse moving-left-radius constant appears.
The first identity is formalized as the candidate
\texttt{ActualHartreeMatchedSlopeFromZeroLeftAndPhysicalJet.lean}
with strict CI \texttt{42539730025}; the general bound is
\texttt{ActualHartreeMatchedSlopeFromTraceEnergyBound.lean}
with CI \texttt{42540031173}.
These new proofs have \emph{not yet passed} their own strict
build and axiom checks. The full physical conclusion will require
the already established original Fredholm source/trace producer
and the same Cramer speed, not a fresh independently assumed trace.
It also requires the a.e./classical identification of the algebraic
Green slope with a derivative of the actual field before asserting
a radial $\dot H^1$ theorem.
The original revised environment count is still $11/13$.

four-region error or the rate-defining modulation ODE.

The earlier correction residual class currently uses two
core-parameter paths satisfying
\[
 c_0'=-bc_0+m,\qquad
 c_1'=-(b+\Delta b)c_1+m
\]
and assumes $c_1(s)=c_0(s)$ \emph{for every} $s\ge1$.
Differentiating this exact equality at $s>1$ forces
\[
 \boxed{\qquad \Delta b(s)c_0(s)=0.\qquad}
\]
Thus the existing source class covers the degenerate
same-core case, not an arbitrary nonzero physical scale
correction.  To construct a legitimate nonlinear Hartree
iteration for the final theorem, the intermediate correction
identity must be revised to retain the genuine
$c_1-c_0$ and core-tangent contributions and to bound
their complete quartic nonlocal residuals.  Prescribing
$\Delta b=0$ or postulating the missing positive order
would change the theorem's mathematical content.
This issue does not invalidate the already strictly
proved fixed-time Green, spectral and $C^1$ statements,
but it prevents accepting the full finite-order
residual ledger from the old atom alone.



\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Nonlinear next-layer correction on one global Hartree background:
strict proof without incompatible core paths.}
The former finite correction atom assumed the global identity
$c_1\equiv c_0$ despite the distinct equations
$c_0'=-bc_0+m$ and $c_1'=-(b+\Delta b)c_1+m$.
These three conditions imply $\Delta b\,c_0=0$ on the open
time interval; hence that atom does not represent generic
nonzero-speed profile corrections.

A revised construction takes a \emph{single} evolving complex
background $U(s,r)$ and its actual correction $H(s,r)$, and
retains every term in the original quartic nonlocal Hartree PDE.
Let $\mathcal P_U(H;\Delta b,\Delta\omega)$ denote the full
linearized Hartree evolution around $U$, including
$i\Delta b\Lambda U+\Delta\omega U$ but excluding only the
quadratic cross terms on $H$.  Define the \emph{literal} residual
\[
 \mathcal E_{U,H}:=
 \mathcal R(U+H;b+\Delta b,\omega+\Delta\omega)
 -\mathcal R(U;b,\omega)-\mathcal P_U(H;\Delta b,\Delta\omega).
\]
The original Hartree PDE correction identity then gives
\begin{equation}
 \mathcal E_{U,H}
 =i\Delta b\,\Lambda H+\Delta\omega\,H
   +\mathcal Q_{\HH}(U,H),
 \label{eq:hbl-genuine-single-core-nonlinear-remainder}
\end{equation}
where $\mathcal Q_{\HH}(U,H)$ is the exact nonlocal
quartic Hartree Taylor remainder, not an independent local
source or an assigned order label.

Assume the explicitly stated regularity of $U,H$ and,
for $\rho\ge\kappa_{\rm phys}>0$, the actual bounds
\[
 |U(s,r)|\le M\,W(r),\qquad
 |H(s,r)|\le C_Hs^{-\rho}W(r),\qquad
 |\Lambda H(s,r)|\le C_{Hd}s^{-\rho},
\]
\[
 |\Delta b(s)|+|\Delta\omega(s)|
 \le C_{\rm speed}s^{-\rho}.
\]
Using the original nonlocal Hartree Taylor estimate and
the proved speed cross bound, without prescribing a new
residual order, gives
\[
 \boxed{\quad
 |\mathcal E_{U,H}(s,r)|
 \le\left\{
   C_{\rm speed}(C_{Hd}+C_H)
   +63(M+C_H)^5C_H^2W(r)^5
 \right\}s^{-(\rho+\kappa_{\rm phys})}.
 \quad}
\]
The exact identity and the quantitative next-layer estimate
are \emph{strictly accepted} in
\texttt{ActualHartreeOneGlobalCoreCorrectionNextLayer.lean},
[CI \texttt{42547533614}], original artifact
\texttt{13644785150}; the two exact
\texttt{\#check}/\texttt{\#print axioms} audits under
\texttt{--trust=0} yield only
\texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice},
\texttt{Quot.sound} (universe annotations allowed).
The downstream genuinely realized residual-atom wrapper,
\texttt{ActualHartreeOneGlobalCoreCorrectionResidualAtom.lean},
had an initial namespace-resolution failure in
CI \texttt{42547935578} (original \texttt{bubble}
was not opened); repaired source commit \texttt{416022b0}
replaces it with strict CI \texttt{42548637148},
\emph{strictly accepted} at original source commit \texttt{416022b0},
with archived original evidence \texttt{13645185877}.  The
\texttt{--trust=0}, \texttt{warningAsError}, exact target
\texttt{\#check} and \texttt{\#print axioms} audit reports exactly
\texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, and
\texttt{Quot.sound}, without \texttt{sorryAx}.

The remaining non-circular step is to produce the relevant
full four-region $U,H$ from the \emph{same actual orbit} and
solve their corresponding principal Hartree equations, with
time-dependent Green/Kummer interfaces, original weighted
Fredholm norms, parameter Lipschitz and finite recursion.
The verified estimate alone neither constructs those fields
nor proves the final dynamical blowup rate. Revised original
count remains $11/13$.



\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Strict verification scope: the realized atom is
temporal at one fixed radius, not the original weighted spatial norm.}
The now accepted
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_one\_global\_core\_correction\_realized\_atom}
constructs, for each fixed \(r>0\), an actual
\texttt{WeightedResidualAtom} with
\[
 E_r(s)=\mathcal R[U+H](s,r)-\mathcal R[U](s,r)
       -\mathcal P_U(H)(s,r),\qquad
 |E_r(s)|\le C_0s^{-\rho-\kappa_{\rm phys}}.
\]
Its displayed coefficient is uniform in \(r\) only after
using \(W(r)^5\le1\).  The data type
\texttt{WeightedResidualAtom} controls \emph{time order of a
scalar complex-valued field}, not
\(\|\langle r\rangle^{7/4}E(s,\cdot)\|_{L^2_r}\).
Indeed a spatially constant upper envelope is not integrable
in the weighted half-line.  The quartic Taylor summand retains
\(W(r)^5\), but the speed-cross summands require their own
spatially integrable, same-field bound and moving-domain
control.  Neither the exact nonlinear telescope nor the
now certified residual atom proves the global
\(O(s^{-9/4})\) source in the original Fredholm/threshold
space.  This is an essential type-and-norm distinction for
the manuscript's \S2.13 acceptance.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The spatially resolved nonlinear Hartree correction and its
conjugated Fredholm norm gate (9 November 2026).}
For the same global evolving background $U(s,r)$ and correction $H(s,r)$,
the exact original quartic nonlocal Hartree residual identity reads
\[
 \mathcal E_{U,H}
 =i\,\Delta b\,\Lambda H+\Delta\omega\,H+\mathcal Q_{\HH}(U,H).
\]
The previously accepted scalar \texttt{WeightedResidualAtom} used a
radius-independent majorant on the modulation speed--correction products.
To retain their genuine spatial shapes, suppose the visible actual
regularity and source hypotheses hold, with
$|\Delta b|+|\Delta\omega|\le C_v s^{-\rho}$,
$|U(s,r)|\le M W(r)$, $|H(s,r)|\le C_Hs^{-\rho}W(r)$,
and $W(r)=(1+r^2)^{-1/2}$. Then the pointwise consequence is
\begin{equation}
 |\mathcal E_{U,H}(s,r)|
 \le C_v s^{-\rho}\bigl(|\Lambda H(s,r)|+|H(s,r)|\bigr)
 +63(M+C_Hs^{-\rho})^5C_H^2s^{-2\rho}W(r)^5 .
 \label{eq:hbl-global-core-spatial-remainder}
\end{equation}
This is the precise new Lean candidate
\texttt{ActualHartreeOneGlobalCoreSpatialRemainderBridge.lean},
source revision \texttt{ec8efcee}, now \emph{strictly accepted}
by original CI \texttt{42552019761} (job \texttt{128949475926},
artifact \texttt{13645594881}).  The source is checked with
Lean \texttt{4.35.0-rc3}, Mathlib
\texttt{a98628e16c11f5167f16124105ddce53efa9bfe5},
\texttt{--trust=0}, \texttt{warningAsError}, exact
\texttt{\#check} and \texttt{\#print axioms}; the original job prints
only \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice},
\texttt{Quot.sound}, without \texttt{sorryAx}. Initial CI
\texttt{42551656354} failed on complex norm simplifications,
repaired in \texttt{ec8efcee}. This is one verified
\emph{auxiliary} spatial-shape theorem, not an accepted
original \S2.13 environment.

For the inner radial conjugation $q=rH$ and
$w(r)=(1+r^2)^{7/8}$, the actual Fredholm forcing is
$r\mathcal E_{U,H}$. Provided the spatial speed-cross norms are finite,
the Minkowski inequality applied to
\eqref{eq:hbl-global-core-spatial-remainder} yields
\[
 \|wr\mathcal E_{U,H}(s,\cdot)\|_{L^2(0,\infty)}
 \le C_v s^{-\rho}
 \bigl(\|wr\Lambda H_s\|_2+\|wrH_s\|_2\bigr)
 +63(M+C_Hs^{-\rho})^5 C_H^2s^{-2\rho}C_*,
\]
where
\[
 C_*^2=\int_0^\infty r^2(1+r^2)^{-13/4}\,dr
 =\frac12\operatorname{B}\!\left(\frac32,\frac74\right)<\infty.
\]
This integral inequality is an analytical bridge, \emph{not}
a separate claim of Lean acceptance. The mere bubble bound on $H$
does not control $\|wrH\|_2$ over the full half-line.
The original source decomposition instead uses a strongly weighted
inner source and a weaker outer source, with moving matching radii;
the present global bound can serve as a sufficient estimate only where
those norms are actually produced. The actual four-piece field,
its dilation, moving interfaces and selected-speed Lipschitz increments
must provide these norms and strict time gain. Neither a global
$O(s^{-9/4})$ bound nor a dynamically selected Type--II rate follows
from the displayed algebra alone. The original revised count is
$11/13$.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{A precise shell-volume obstruction to using the pointwise
physical clock as a Fredholm source gain.}
The actual Hartree PDE moving-shell estimate is pointwise
$|E_{\rm sh}(s,r)|\le C\ell(s)^{5/2}$ for
$R(s)\le r\le2R(s)$, before relaxing to the physical
clock density $C\ell(s)^2$.  For the inner conjugated
Fredholm forcing $rE_{\rm sh}$, the original shell-weight calculation
yields, for $R\ge1$,
\[
 \|w(r)\,r\,\mathbf 1_{(R,2R)}(r)E_{\rm sh}(s,r)\|_{L^2(dr)}
 \le20C R^{13/4}\ell(s)^{5/2}.
\]
If the genuine moment-based radius merely satisfies
$R(s)\le Q/\ell(s)$, this yields at most
\[
 \|w\,r\,\mathbf 1_{(R,2R)}E_{\rm sh}\|_2
 \le20 C Q^{13/4}\ell(s)^{-3/4},
\]
which has no positive power of $\ell$.  This is a limitation of the
\emph{coarse estimate}, not a lower bound on the true error:
additional actual spatial decay, cancellation or a region-specific
norm is indispensable. The fixed-trajectory physical-clock integral
cannot substitute for this spatial source estimate, and the source
exponent cannot be declared before the same-orbit PDE produces it.
The original revised environment count remains $11/13$.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Genuine nonlinear Hartree correction as a radial-conjugated
shell source: exact moving-radius cost.}
The true same-core remainder from
\eqref{eq:hbl-global-core-spatial-remainder} is
\(\mathcal E_{U,H}=\mathcal R[U+H]-\mathcal R[U]-\mathcal P_U(H)\).
For \(R(s)\ge1\), \(1\le s\), and the visible same-field
regularity, amplitude, dilation and speed hypotheses, the established
original shell norm geometry converts its \emph{actual} nonlinear
PDE residual to the radial-conjugated source
\[
 G_{s,R}(r):=
 \mathbf1_{(R,2R)}(r)\,r\,|\mathcal E_{U,H}(s,r)|.
\]
Writing \(\kappa=\kappa_{\rm phys}>0\) and
\(C_0=C_v(C_{\Lambda H}+C_H)+63(M+C_H)^5C_H^2\), the new
strict Lean target proves (subject to its independent CI)
\[
 \boxed{\quad
  G_{s,R}\in L^{2,7/4}(dr),\qquad
  \|G_{s,R}\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dr)}
  \le20 R^{13/4}C_0\,s^{-(\rho+\kappa)}.
 \quad}
\]
The original source is conjugated by \(r\); the factor
\(R^{13/4}=R^{1+7/4+1/2}\) includes the radial
conjugation, weight and square-root shell volume, respectively.
These cannot be erased by a pointwise temporal residual atom.
The source file

For an additional \emph{derived} upper radius bound

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Matched-shell replacement for the crude $R^{13/4}$ cost
(precise conditional route, not a closed PDE estimate).}
The factor $R^{13/4}$ in the previous pointwise-to-weighted
argument is genuine, but it need not be the final cost for an
\emph{exactly matched} inner--Kummer difference.
The already verified
\texttt{ActualHartreeMatchedShellWeightedGain.lean} gives, for
$u$ the original normalized two-channel Fredholm response,
$v$ a regular exterior solution of the \emph{same scaled Kummer
source equation} on $(R,2R)$, and at a common interface $r_0$
\[
 u(r_0)=v(r_0),\qquad u_r(r_0)=v_r(r_0),
\]
the genuine Kummer cutoff commutator of $d=u-v$ obeys
\[
 \bigl\|\mathbf1_{(R,2R)}r\,
 [\,\mathcal L_K(\chi_Rd)-\chi_R\mathcal L_Kd\,]\bigr\|_{L^{2,7/4}}
 \le
 20C\left(C_{\rm in}R^{-3/4}
   +C_{\rm K}(\mu,\theta)\frac{R^{13/4}}{a^2}\right)
 (N_++N_-).
\]
This conclusion requires \emph{all} of the genuine theorem's
regularity, common-source, exact matching, $R>2$, and
$16R^2(1+\|b(\mu,\theta)\|)/a^2\le1$ hypotheses.
For the \emph{original} canonical inner overlap,
the existing Hartree construction uses
$R=L/2$, $L=\ell^{-1/16}$, $L\ge6$, and the genuine
source-selector condition $a\ge L^3$.
These already imply
\[
 \frac{R^{13/4}}{a^2}
 \le \frac1{64}R^{-11/4}
 \le \frac1{64}R^{-3/4},\qquad
 R^{-3/4}=2^{3/4}\ell^{3/64}.
\]
Thus this geometric factor can be bounded by
$R^{-3/4}$ \emph{without assuming an external rate};
uniform spectral coefficients and the original shell
smallness condition must still be checked against the selected orbit.
The power $\ell^{3/64}$ is a positive \emph{scale} gain,
not automatically a positive $s$-time order.
This is a \emph{different} mechanism from applying
the unconditional $R^{13/4}$ shell-volume estimate
to a radius-independent scalar residual bound:
the matching and common differential equation first
produce the additional $R^{-4}$ factor.

For the actual \emph{spectrally corrected} four-piece field,
the exterior equation contains its true quadratic spectral defect,
so the common-source hypothesis above cannot simply be asserted.
The required next identity is a decomposition of the \emph{literal
original nonlocal PDE residual} into this matched commutator,
the quantitatively controlled spectral defect, the moving
basis/interface/selected-speed derivatives, and all nonlocal
cross-region interactions. Every term must be controlled in its
actual region-appropriate source norm and then in two-parameter
Lipschitz differences. No such decomposition-and-gain theorem
has yet been strictly accepted; neither original Section~2.13 nor
the terminal dynamical blowup result follows from the present
matched-shell estimate. The revised original count remains $11/13$.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Original-operator spectral source mismatch:
the exact term that the matched-shell theorem cannot discard
(9 November 2026).}
Write \(u_0=G_{\rm match}[F_{\rm adj};J]\),
\(u_1=G_{\rm left}[\delta u_0;0,0]\),
\(w_\delta=u_0-u_1\), and \(v_\delta(y)=w_\delta(y/a)\).
The already checked classical Green equations give, on the genuine
Kummer interval,
\[
 \mathcal L_Ku_0=F_{\rm adj},\qquad
 \mathcal L_Ku_1=\delta u_0,\qquad
 (\mathcal L_K+\delta)w_\delta-F_{\rm adj}
   =-\delta u_1.
\]
The last member is quadratic in \(\delta\) under the previously
proved Volterra bound.  Crucially, the \emph{original} (unshifted)
PDE operator instead obeys
\[
 \mathcal L_K^a v_\delta(y)
  =a^{-2}\bigl(F_{\rm adj}(y/a)
                  -\delta u_0(y/a)\bigr).
\]
For the physical inner Fredholm field
\(u=\mathcal G_{\rm in}(F_+,F_-)\), let
\[
 S_{\rm in}(y)=\frac{F_+(y)+iF_-(y)}{y},\qquad
 E_{\rm in}(y)=\mathcal L_K^a u(y)+S_{\rm in}(y).
\]
Consequently the \emph{actual source discrepancy} on the
backward overlap is
\[
 \boxed{\quad
 \mathcal L_K^a(u-v_\delta)
  =E_{\rm in}-\mathfrak M_\delta,\qquad
 \mathfrak M_\delta(y)
   =S_{\rm in}(y)+a^{-2}
     \bigl(F_{\rm adj}(y/a)-\delta u_0(y/a)\bigr).
 \quad}
\]
There is also a \emph{quantifier-level source-consistency gap}.
The strictly checked selected-source/Green theorems quantify over
arbitrary independent real functions \(F_+,F_-\) in the Fredholm
space and an arbitrary radiation \(z\) satisfying the visible
envelope conditions; the genuine exterior \(F_0\) is generated from
\(z\), but the theorem statements do not derive
\(F_\pm\) from the \emph{same} Hartree PDE trajectory.
The two real Cramer compatibility equations determine the
modulation pair; they do not imply the function-valued
identity \(S_{\rm in}=-a^{-2}F_{\rm adj}\).
The \texttt{\detokenize{ActualHartreeSinglePhysicalSourceTwoChannelSelection.lean}}
producer already specializes the \emph{fixed-time speed selector}
to the same physical \(z\), with
\(F_\pm=\operatorname{Re,Im}\bigl[r\chi_L(r)
\mathcal S_{\HH}(r)\bigr],\quad\chi_L(r)=1-\operatorname{cutoff}(r/L)\), and derives the original
weighted membership; continuity is still a visible input.
The new \texttt{\detokenize{ActualHartreeOnePhysicalSourceSpectralQuadraticThreeC1.lean}}
composes that genuinely one-source specialization with the
quadratic spectral and three-\(C^1\) field outputs; its
strict CI is pending and must not be counted prematurely.
Even if accepted, the localized inner source is not
automatically identical to the uncut outer source on the
overlap: the cutoff, two selected speed directions and the
first-order spectral term all contribute to
\(\mathfrak M_\delta\).
The source-difference norm and time/parameter budgets must
therefore be derived at \emph{each physical recursion layer}.


The original source/partition theorem
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_moment\_locked\_plateau\_scaled\_source}
yields a stronger statement \emph{on the physical source plateau}.
Let
\[
 F_\pm=\operatorname{Re,Im}\bigl[y\chi_L(y)
          \mathcal S_{\mathrm H}(y)\bigr],\qquad
 F_+^{\rm adj}=F_+ +p_2G_\omega,\quad
 F_-^{\rm adj}=F_-+p_1G_b ,
\]
and use the same real selector \(p=(p_1,p_2)\) in the
normalized outer forcing \(F_{\rm adj}\). Since
\(\chi_L(y)=1\) for \(0<y\le L\),
\[
 S_{\rm in}^{\rm adj}(y)
     +a^{-2}F_{\rm adj}(y/a)=0
 \quad(0<y\le L),\qquad
 \boxed{\ \mathfrak M_\delta(y)
   =-a^{-2}\delta\,u_0(y/a)\quad(0<y\le L).\ }
\]
Thus the \emph{entire true quartic Hartree forcing} and both
physical real speed channels cancel exactly on the backward
overlap \(R=L/2\le y\le2R=L\); there is no need to assume an
independent matching envelope or a vanishing spectral source.
The first-order \(\delta u_0\) remains and cannot be replaced
by the shifted-operator quadratic term \(\delta u_1\).
The complementary support region \(y>L\) and its nonlocal
feedback require separate control.


A direct route to this missing left-hand estimate is to conjugate
the \emph{original} Kummer equation by \(x(r)=r\,u_0(r)\).
For the normalized first-order coefficient
\(2/r+\tfrac{i}{2}r\), this gives the regular equation
\[
 x''(r)+\frac{ir}{2}x'(r)
      +\left(\lambda-\frac i2\right)x(r)=rF_{\rm adj}(r),
 \qquad \lambda=\operatorname{frozenZeroCoeff}(\mu,\theta).
\]
Writing \(r=r_0t\), \(t\in[1/2,1]\),
\(r_0=L/a\), yields
\[
 \partial_t^2 x+\frac{i r_0^2t}{2}\partial_t x+
 r_0^2\left(\lambda-\frac i2\right)x
     =r_0^2 (rF_{\rm adj})(r_0t).
\]
Thus the \emph{proposed dyadic backward Green estimate}, to be
proved with the actual original source rather than postulated, is
\[
\begin{split}
 &\sup_{r_0/2\le r\le r_0}
   \left(\lvert r u_0(r)\rvert+
         \lvert r^2u_0'(r)\rvert\right)\\
 &\qquad \le C_D
  \left(\lvert r_0U\rvert+
        \lvert r_0^2DU\rvert+
        r_0^2\!\sup_{r_0/2\le r\le r_0}
                         \lvert r F_{\rm adj}(r)\rvert\right),
 \qquad 0<r_0\le1,\quad |\lambda|\le D.
\end{split}
\]
This is a \emph{proof target}, not an already verified Lean
declaration.  Its key feature is that the normalized IVP
coefficients are bounded independently of \(r_0^{-1}\).
With a backward bound \(H_{\leftarrow}\) on
\(\lvert r u_0(r)\rvert\), the spectral forcing then satisfies
\[
 \left\|\mathbf1_{[R,2R]}(y)\,
   y a^{-2}\delta\,u_0(y/a)\right\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dy)}
 \le C\frac{|\delta|H_{\leftarrow}}{a}\,R^{9/4}
 \le C'|\delta|H_{\leftarrow}L^{-3/4},
 \quad R=L/2,\ a\ge L^3.
\]
The resulting spatial gain \(L^{-3/4}=\ell^{3/64}\)
does \emph{not} by itself establish a positive
\emph{time} exponent, nor does it control the moving
basis, selected-speed derivative, or four-region
nonlocal Hartree feedback. The required physical
\(H_{\leftarrow}\), \(\delta(s)\), and corresponding
two-parameter estimates remain to be derived.

The actual weighted Green bound already established for
\(u_0(x)\) covers \(x\in[L/a,B]\), whereas this backward overlap
requires \(x=y/a\in[L/(2a),L/a]\).  Applying that right-hand
estimate on the left interval would be an \emph{invalid
extrapolation}.  The new original-PDE plateau lemma
\texttt{\detokenize{ActualHartreePlateauSpectralOriginalSourceCancel.lean}}
expresses the exact first-order forcing in Lean; its strict
CI is pending.  The next quantitative proof must propagate
the original matched Cauchy jet \emph{backward} through this
dyadic interval with constants independent of \(a/L\), then
control the genuine spatial/time weighted norm and parameter
Lipschitz difference of \(a^{-2}\delta u_0(y/a)\).

The fixed-inner defect \(E_{\rm in}\) has the previously
verified \(R^{-4}+a^{-2}\) norm budget.  It is \emph{not}
legitimate to apply the common-source matched-shell gain unless
\(\mathfrak M_\delta=0\), or to replace \(\mathfrak M_\delta\) by
the \emph{quadratic shifted-operator} defect
\(-\delta u_1\).  For the actual quartic Hartree sources
\(S_{\rm in}\) and \(F_{\rm adj}\), neither this equality nor the
needed weighted source-norm/time-order estimate has yet been
derived from the same orbit.  The next genuine proof target is
\[
 \bigl\|
  \mathbf 1_{(R,2R)}(y)\,
  y\,\mathfrak M_{\delta(s)}(s,y)
 \bigr\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dy)}
 \le C\,s^{-\rho-\kappa},\qquad\kappa>0,
\]
with the required two-parameter Lipschitz differences produced
from the actual PDE. This target is displayed as an
\emph{unproved obligation}, not inserted as a hypothesis into
any acceptance certificate.  Its displayed algebraic

theorem; the previously accepted Green/Volterra components
remain the only formal inputs.  No change to the formal

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Exact unshifted spectral PDE source: strict kernel acceptance.}
For \(u_0=G_K[F_{\rm adj};U,DU]\) and
\(u_1=G_K[\delta u_0;0,0]\), put \(w_\delta=u_0-u_1\).
The \emph{fixed-reference, unshifted} Kummer equation and its physical
pullback have now both passed the independent strict Lean audit
\texttt{42562774111}, original job \texttt{128982599861},
evidence artifact \texttt{13650098975}. In particular,
\[
  \mathcal L_K w_\delta=F_{\rm adj}-\delta u_0,\qquad
  \mathcal L_K^{(a)}[w_\delta(\cdot/a)](y)
   =a^{-2}[F_{\rm adj}(y/a)-\delta u_0(y/a)] .
\]

\noindent\textbf{Matched backward shell without an independent

The preexisting true two-channel matched shell theorem establishes
\[
  \|u(r)-v_0(r)\|\le
 8R^2 A_R,\qquad

for the genuine common forcing and zero Cauchy mismatch at \(2R=L\).

  \quad}
\]
Unlike the right-side bound on \(u_0(x)\) for \(x\ge L/a\),

\paragraph{Actual quartic-source realization of the backward spectral shell.}
The preceding fixed-source spectral estimate is now composed with

Writing \(L=\operatorname{actualHartreeSlowInnerRadius}(\ell)\),

\[
 F_+=\operatorname{Re}(r\chi_L\mathcal S_{\rm H})+
             p_2G_{\omega},\qquad
 F_-=\operatorname{Im}(r\chi_L\mathcal S_{\rm H})+
             p_1G_b,
\]
let \(J\) be the canonical Fredholm Cauchy jet at \(L\), and put
\[
 v(y)=\operatorname{OriginMatchedKummer}
    (L/a,B,F_{\rm adj},J_1,J_2)(y/a),\qquad
 E_\delta(y)=a^{-2}\delta v(y).
\]
The original quartic plateau identity proves, rather than assumes,
\[
 \mathcal L^a_{\mu,\theta}v
      =-\frac{F_+(y)+iF_-(y)}{y},\qquad R\le y\le L;
 \qquad (v(L),v'(L))=J_{\rm inner}.
\]
Consequently, for \(a\ge L^3\) and the displayed weak Kummer
absorption condition, the backward same-source estimate gives
\[
 \big\|\mathbf 1_{(R,2R)}\,yE_\delta(y)
       \big\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dy)}
 \le 20\,|\delta|\,
 \left[C_{\rm val}\bigl(\|F_+\|_{L^{2,7/4}}
                   +\|F_-\|_{L^{2,7/4}}\bigr)
       +8R^2\,\mathcal A_{\rm shell}\right]R^{-3/4}.
\]
The new source-specific Lean proof target

constructs these two adjusted weighted memberships and the plateau

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Operator-identification gate for the actual moving Hartree PDE.}
The exact operator choice for the original Hartree PDE has to be
established from the physical renormalized equation, rather than
inferred from an informal original/shifted label for a
\emph{frozen} Kummer Green computation. For
\(u_0=G_{K_0}[F_{\rm adj}]\), \(u_1=G_{K_0}[\delta u_0]\) and
\(w_\delta=u_0-u_1\), both of the following are correct:
\[
 \boxed{\quad
  \mathcal L_{K_0}w_\delta-F_{\rm adj}=-\delta u_0,
  \qquad
  (\mathcal L_{K_0}+\delta)w_\delta-F_{\rm adj}
       =-\delta u_1 .
 \quad}
\]
The first identity was strictly audited by
\texttt{42562774111}; the second by the existing matched
spectral Green theorem. If \(\delta(s)\) denotes the
\emph{actual coefficient displacement}
\(q_0(\mu(s),\theta(s))-q_0(\mu_0,\theta_0)\), then
\(\mathcal L_{\mu(s),\theta(s)}
=\mathcal L_{\mu_0,\theta_0}+\delta(s)\)
as frozen spatial differential expressions and the
corresponding \emph{moving-parameter} spatial residual is
\(-\delta u_1\), quadratic under the source-derived
Volterra bound. By contrast, for a reference operator
\(\mathcal L_{K_0}\) with an additional independently
introduced correction parameter, \(-\delta u_0\) survives.
Neither algebraic identity alone identifies the precise
physical renormalized Hartree operator, whose moving basis,
selected speeds, time transport and nonlocal couplings
must be inserted from the same PDE orbit.
Consequently the first-order backward-shell estimate is
a valid \emph{reference-operator bound}; it is not proof
that the full actual moving-parameter Hartree residual
necessarily contains such an uncanceled term. This
distinction avoids an invalid comparison of two different
operators and does not change original \(11/13\) acceptance.



\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Strict same-source moving-spectrum cancellation in the true quartic Hartree plateau.}
For the unique physical two-channel adjusted source at any one frozen
physical slice, set
\[
  \delta:=q_0(\mu,\theta)-q_0(\mu_0,\theta_0),\qquad
  u_0:=G_{K_0}[F_{\rm adj};J_+,J_-],\qquad
  u_1:=G_{K_0}[\delta u_0;0,0],\qquad
  v_\delta(y):=(u_0-u_1)(y/a).
\]
On the genuine inner-source plateau \(0<y\le L\), the physical
quartic Hartree source and both real scale/phase sources have the
same exact source identity as the original matched Green field.
Consequently the \emph{current-coefficient} operator obeys
\begin{equation}
  \mathcal L_{\mu,\theta}^{(a)}v_\delta
   +\mathcal S_{\rm in}
     =-a^{-2}\delta u_1(y/a).
\label{eq:hbl-actual-spectral-quadratic-plateau}
\end{equation}
The \(-a^{-2}\delta u_0\) term of the fixed-reference operator
has canceled against its genuine changed zero-order coefficient.
This is an exact PDE-source identity, not a hypothesis of second-order
smallness.  Independent strict Lean CI \texttt{42565184656},
original job \texttt{128990057404}, artifact \texttt{13651342799},
has \emph{Success} for the precise physical-source/variable-spectrum
theorem
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_plateau\_current\_moving\_spectrum\_exact\_quadratic\_source}.
Pinned Lean 4.35.0-rc3 and Mathlib
\texttt{a98628e16c11f5167f16124105ddce53efa9bfe5},
trust zero, warning-as-error and exact target axiom audit are
strictly satisfied, with only \texttt{propext},
\texttt{Classical.choice}, and \texttt{Quot.sound}.

The distinct reference-operator first-order source
\(-a^{-2}\delta u_0\) on the physical backward shell

estimate
\[
 \|\mathbf 1_{(R,2R)}y\,a^{-2}\delta u_0(y/a)\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dy)}
   \le 20R^{-3/4}|\delta|D_R,\qquad a\ge(2R)^3,
\]

The needed zero-Cauchy-jet Volterra estimate \emph{below} its

Neither the exact current-coefficient cancellation nor the

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Backward zero-left Volterra quadratic source: exact spatial scale budget.}
The original physical backward plateau is
\([R,2R]\) with \(R=L/2\); its normalized interval is
\([r_0/2,r_0]\) with \(r_0=L/a\). This interval is to the
\emph{left} of the actual zero-Cauchy-jet anchor \(r_0\);
the previously proved forward Green estimate has no automatic
validity there. For the genuine matched reference field
\(u_0\), the source-locked two-channel Fredholm estimate gives
\[
 |u_0(r)|\le D_R,\qquad r_0/2\le r\le r_0,
 \qquad
 D_R=C_{\rm val}N_F+8R^2\mathcal A_{\rm shell}.
\]
Let \(q(r)=\delta u_0(r)\), and let
\(u_1=G_{K_0}[q;0,0]\) denote the original
\emph{zero-left Cramer} correction.
The genuine Wronskian representation, after canceling its
left coefficient, reads
\[
 r u_1(r)=
 -y_K(r)\int_{r_0}^{r}\frac{x_K(v)\,q(v)}
              {vW_K(v)}\,dv
 +x_K(r)\int_{r_0}^{r}\frac{y_K(v)\,q(v)}
              {vW_K(v)}\,dv,
 \qquad r_0/2\le r\le r_0,
\]
with the minus sign of the upper-end Green primitive and the plus
sign of the lower-end primitive explicitly retained. In the absolute-value
estimate their orientation is immaterial. Under the real
inverse-linear bound
\[
 |q(v)|\le \frac{M}{v},\qquad
 M=r_0|\delta|D_R,
 \qquad r_0/2\le v\le r_0,
\]
the exactly normalized Wronskian and bounded regularized
Kummer modes yield
\begin{equation}
 \sup_{r_0/2\le r\le r_0}|u_1(r)|
 \le2G^2M=2G^2r_0|\delta|D_R.
 \label{eq:hbl-backward-zerojet-spectral-value}
\end{equation}
The new strict Lean candidate
\texttt{ActualHartreeBackwardZeroLeftSpectralVolterra.lean}
is assigned CI \texttt{42567116515}; its initial
CI \texttt{42566011025} failed on an unused positive-anchor
Lean proof binder, not an invalid integral identity.
The source-to-field composition is a second candidate
\texttt{ActualHartreeMatchedBackwardQuadraticSpectralSource.lean},
CI \texttt{42566992080}. Neither is marked accepted until
the actual strict logs and axiom audits succeed.

Combining \eqref{eq:hbl-actual-spectral-quadratic-plateau},
\eqref{eq:hbl-backward-zerojet-spectral-value}, and the
original conjugated moving-shell volume estimate, the
\emph{conditional} quadratic bound is
\[
 \left\|
 \mathbf 1_{(R,2R)}y\,a^{-2}\delta u_1(y/a)
 \right\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dy)}
 \le40G^2\,\frac{LR^{13/4}}{a^3}
       |\delta|^2D_R.
\]
The physical separation \(a\ge L^3\), \(L=2R\), gives
\begin{equation}
 \boxed{\quad
 \left\|\mathbf 1_{(R,2R)}
       y\,a^{-2}\delta u_1(y/a)\right\|_{L^{2,7/4}}
 \le \frac{5}{32}G^2R^{-19/4}|\delta|^2D_R.
 \quad}
 \label{eq:hbl-backward-spectral-weighted-quadratic}
\end{equation}
This is a \emph{calculated spatial-scale consequence},
not yet an independently accepted Lean
\(L^{2,7/4}\) theorem; in particular its
source regularity and actual measurable field must be
formally transferred. The geometric
\(R^{-19/4}=2^{19/4}\ell^{19/64}\)
is a genuine positive \(\ell\)-power
(\(L=\ell^{-1/16}\)) without an assumed Type--II rate.
It is \emph{not} automatically the required positive
\(s\)-power. Moving time/basis/selected-speed derivatives,
the three remaining nonlocal Hartree regions and parameter
Lipschitz must still enter the original PDE norm.
The revised theorem-environment score remains \(11/13\).

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Finite genuine backward spectral Neumann iteration and its exact spatial power.}
The strictly accepted reverse Cramer--Volterra estimate applies
iteratively to the \emph{same original} normalized Green operator.
Put $r_0=L/a=2R/a$, and keep the physically produced first field
$u_0=G_{K_0}[F_{\rm adj};J_+,J_-]$ and its source-derived
$D_R$ from above. For each finite $j\ge1$, define
\[
  u_j=G_{K_0}[\delta u_{j-1};0,0],
  \qquad
  w_N=\sum_{j=0}^{N}(-1)^ju_j,\qquad
  c_R=2G^2r_0|\delta|.
\]
For continuous generated sources whose genuine Green primitives
are interval integrable, the \emph{same} exact Wronskian
formula on $[r_0/2,r_0]$ gives inductively
\begin{equation}
 \sup_{r_0/2\le r\le r_0}|u_j(r)|
 \le c_R^j D_R,\qquad j\in\mathbb N.
 \label{eq:hbl-finite-backward-neumann-spatial}
\end{equation}
Indeed $|u_{j-1}(x)|\le c_R^{j-1}D_R$ and $x\le r_0$
imply
$|\delta u_{j-1}(x)|\le
 r_0|\delta|c_R^{j-1}D_R/x$;
the reverse zero-jet Green estimate supplies exactly the extra
factor $2G^2r_0|\delta|$ with no inverse-left-radius loss.
The \emph{current-coefficient} spatial equation then telescopes
algebraically to
\[
 (\mathcal L_{K_0}+\delta)w_N-F_{\rm adj}
   =(-1)^N\delta u_N.
\]
The conjugated physical-shell estimate and
$a\ge(2R)^3$ consequently give the quantitative spatial bound
\begin{equation}
 \left\|\mathbf1_{(R,2R)}
   y\,a^{-2}\delta u_N(y/a)\right\|_{L^{2,7/4}}
 \le\frac5{16}\left(\frac{G^2}{2}\right)^N
 R^{-(11/4+2N)}|\delta|^{N+1}D_R.
 \label{eq:hbl-finite-backward-neumann-norm}
\end{equation}
Its $N=1$ specialization recovers
\eqref{eq:hbl-backward-spectral-weighted-quadratic} exactly.
This calculation identifies a \emph{finite} route to arbitrarily high
\emph{spatial} spectral powers without presupposing a larger physical
source envelope. A new Lean module
\texttt{ActualHartreeBackwardSpectralNeumannFiniteGain.lean}
encodes the actual recursive Cramer--Green solutions and the
all-finite-$N$ bound \eqref{eq:hbl-finite-backward-neumann-spatial};
the corresponding dedicated pinned strict CI must succeed before
this new target is marked Lean-accepted.
The norm estimate \eqref{eq:hbl-finite-backward-neumann-norm}
and the current-operator telescoping are analytic consequences here,
not yet independently audited all-$N$ Lean targets.

For the purpose of auditing \emph{conditional} slow-time budgets only,
if the physical PDE eventually supplies
$|\delta(s)|\lesssim s^{-(2\beta-1)}$,
$D_R(s)\lesssim\ell(s)^{1/8}$, and
$\ell(s)\lesssim s^{-\beta}$, the $N$-th source has
\[
 \rho_N=(N+1)(2\beta-1)
             +\frac{19+8N}{64}\,\beta.
\]
Since $\beta>1/2$, this exponent increases with $N$,
but none of those three dynamical inputs is granted by the
Neumann recursion. In particular,
\eqref{eq:hbl-finite-backward-neumann-norm} controls only
the backward overlap of a frozen operator:
the actual moving basis, interfaces, selected-speed derivatives,
outside-plateau Riesz couplings, parameter Lipschitz, and
the genuine Hartree finite physical source recursion have
\emph{not} been deduced. No replacement of the original
\texttt{thm:finite-profile-stage3}, no assumed Type--II rate,
and no increase beyond $11/13$ is asserted.

\noindent\textbf{No-loss backward original Cramer slope, pending independent strict proof.}
In addition to the reverse zero-left value estimate already strictly

\[
 \begin{split}
 r^2u'(r)
   &=[r\,y_K'(r)-y_K(r)]\!
       \left(A_K(r)-A_K(r_0)\right)\\
   &\quad +[r\,x_K'(r)-x_K(r)]B_K(r),\\
 |r^2u'(r)|&\le
   2(r_0+1)G^2M(r_0-r),

 \end{split}
\]
The integrals inside $A_K(r)-A_K(r_0)$ and $B_K(r)$ are
taken with the genuine backward orientation. Neither term
contains a negative power of $r_0$, and the zero-Cauchy
left-anchor data are retained. The independent theorem file is
\texttt{ActualHartreeBackwardZeroLeftSpectralVolterraSlope.lean},
with strict workflow
\texttt{hbl-213-backward-zero-left-spectral-volterra-slope.yml}.

\noindent\textbf{Original-PDE principal-defect reduction (November 9).}
Write $\mathcal R_{\rm H}(U;b,\omega)$ for the genuine complete
radial quartic Hartree evolution residual and let
$\mathcal P_{U;b,\omega}^{\dot b,\dot\omega}[H]$ be its explicitly
defined first-order linearized correction, including the speed
increment acting on the original background $U$.  The same global
physical field $U+H$, with no separate artificially normalized source,
obeys the exact algebraic identity
\[
\begin{aligned}
 \mathcal R_{\rm H}(U+H;b+\dot b,\omega+\dot\omega)
   ={}&
   \mathcal R_{\rm H}(U;b,\omega)
   +\mathcal P_{U;b,\omega}^{\dot b,\dot\omega}[H]\\
   &+\bigl(i\dot b\,\Lambda H+\dot\omega H\bigr)
    +\mathcal N_{\rm H}(U+H)-\mathcal N_{\rm H}(U)
      -D\mathcal N_{\rm H}(U)[H].
\end{aligned}
\]
For the already certified complex-Hartree Taylor and cross-speed
bounds, with $s\ge1$, $\kappa>0$, $\kappa\le\rho$, and the actual
pointwise field envelopes, the proposed independently audited
Lean theorem gives
\[
\begin{aligned}
 &\bigl|\mathcal R_{\rm H}(U+H;b+\dot b,\omega+\dot\omega)(s,r)\bigr|\\
 &\quad\le
 \bigl|\mathcal R_{\rm H}(U;b,\omega)(s,r)
       +\mathcal P_{U;b,\omega}^{\dot b,\dot\omega}[H](s,r)\bigr|\\
 &\qquad+
 \left[
   C_{\rm speed}(C_{H,\Lambda}+C_H)
   +63(M+C_H)^5C_H^2W(r)^5
 \right]s^{-(\rho+\kappa)} .
\end{aligned}
\]
The right-hand principal defect is \emph{not} assumed small, and this
pointwise estimate is \emph{not} yet an $X_s$ source norm bound.
The original four-zone physical Fredholm/Kummer construction, moving
endpoints and nonlocal interactions must generate a genuine estimate
for that principal defect, together with its direct two-parameter
difference.  Once proved at fixed order $9/4$, existing backward
fixed-point theorems may be applied; until then revised~\S2.13
and the final dynamical theorem remain unaccepted.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Two-scale Hartree envelope warning.}
The global single-bubble majorant used in the conditional one-global
nonlinear remainder theorem cannot be applied with a time-uniform
coefficient to the actual nonzero regular radiation
$Z_\ell(r)=\sqrt{\ell}\,z(\ell r)$.  For $z(1)\ne0$ and
$0<\ell\le1$, the original Hartree bubble satisfies
\[
 \frac{|Z_\ell(\ell^{-1})|}{W(\ell^{-1})}
  = |z(1)|\sqrt{\ell}\sqrt{1+\ell^{-2}}
  \ge |z(1)|\,\ell^{-1/2}.
\]
Thus a bound $|V_c+Z_\ell|\le M W$ generally requires
$M\gtrsim\ell^{-1/2}$, and the fifth-power coefficient in the
bubble-Taylor estimate may incur $\ell^{-5/2}$.  The proof must
instead exploit the original, separately certified two-scale
majorants
\[
 (1+r)|V_c(r)|\le D,\qquad
 (1+\ell r)|Z_\ell(r)|\le E\sqrt{\ell},
\]
together with regional Riesz and moving-interface bounds.
The principal-defect reduction is an exact nonlocal PDE identity
under its displayed premises, but does not yet provide uniform
four-zone source-norm gain or the locked $9/4$ profile.

\noindent\textbf{Sharp one-step quadratic Hartree remainder: $9/8$ to $9/4$.}
The fixed-order single-orbit construction has a shorter possible chain than

linearized principal equation
\[
 \mathcal P_{U}[H;b,\omega,\Delta b,\Delta\omega]
  =-\mathcal R_{\rm H}(U;b,\omega),
\]
the exact original nonlinear identity is
\[
 \mathcal R_{\rm H}(U+H;b+\Delta b,\omega+\Delta\omega)
  =i\Delta b\,\Lambda H+\Delta\omega\,H+
    \{\mathcal N_{\rm H}(U+H)-\mathcal N_{\rm H}(U)
                   -D\mathcal N_{\rm H}(U)H\}.
\]
The existing native Hartree Taylor and speed-cross proofs show that,
at each physical radius $r>0$, if the actual correction, its dilation
and both compatible real speed increments have order $s^{-\rho}$
under the stated bubble envelopes and regularity, then
\[
 \left|\mathcal R_{\rm H}
    (U+H;b+\Delta b,\omega+\Delta\omega)(s,r)\right|
 \le\left[
 C_{\rm speed}(C_{\Lambda H}+C_H)
 +63(M+C_H)^5C_H^2W(r)^5
 \right]s^{-2\rho}.
\]
Thus the \emph{sharp} quadratic exponent at $\rho=9/8$ is
$2\rho=9/4$ exactly, rather than merely
$\rho+\kappa_{\rm phys}$ as in the arbitrary-level ledger.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Half-radial two-scale gain: proposed weighted source bridge.}
For every $0<\ell\le1$ and $r\ge0$ the following elementary identity
contains the uniform scale gain:
\[
 (1+\ell r)^2-\ell(1+r)
    = (1-\ell)+\ell r+\ell^2r^2\ge0,
 \qquad
 \frac{\ell}{(1+\ell r)^2}\le\frac1{1+r}.
\]
Thus the original Strauss bound
$|Z_\ell(r)|\le E\sqrt\ell/(1+\ell r)$ implies
\[
 (1+r)|Z_\ell(r)|^2\le E^2.
\]
Combining with the moving physical monopole
$(1+r)|V_c(r)|\le D$, we obtain
\[
 (1+r)|V_c(r)+Z_\ell(r)|^2
     \le 2(D^2+E^2)=:K^2,
\]
uniformly in $\ell$.  This is the correct half-radial estimate;
the stronger uniform full-bubble majorant is generally false.
The first two inequalities are under exact Lean audit in
\texttt{ActualHartreeScaledRadiationHalfPower.lean};
no Success is claimed before the original CI finishes.

For the \emph{already strictly verified} original Riesz decay
$|I_2[\mathcal D_{4,\ge2}]|\le C_\mathcal D e^2(1+r)^{-1}$,
the genuine density-remainder times original local cubic therefore
satisfies the following paper-level consequence, conditional only
on the displayed physical correction bound:
\[
 \begin{split}
 \bigl|I_2[\mathcal D_{4,\ge2}(U,H)](r)
                   |U(r)|^2U(r)\bigr|
 &\le C_\mathcal D K^3 e^2(1+r)^{-5/2},\\
 \int_0^\infty(1+r)^{7/2}
 \bigl|I_2[\mathcal D_{4,\ge2}]\,|U|^2U\bigr|^2\,dr
 &\le 2C_\mathcal D^2K^6e^4.
 \end{split}
\]
Here $U=V_c+Z_\ell$, and $C_\mathcal D$ incorporates the
polynomial in its uniform $L^\infty$ bound and $e$.
For $e=C_Hs^{-9/8}$ this individual \emph{actual nonlocal}
source contribution has the desired $s^{-9/4}$ norm order,
without any negative $\ell$ power.
This is a candidate source-class lemma, not yet a strictly audited
weighted-L2 Lean theorem. The remaining nonlocal Taylor products,
linearized four-zone principal equation, moving-time effects and
direct parameter differences are not implied by this one bound.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Current true quadratic Riesz atom and full spectral-PDE telescoping audit (November 9).}
For the genuine complex two-scale background
\(U=V_c+Z_\ell\) and the actual Taylor density
\(\mathcal D_{4,\ge2}(U,H)\), the radial nonlocal atom is
\[
  F(r)=I_2[\mathcal D_{4,\ge2}(U,H)](r)\,|U(r)|^2U(r).
\]
With the explicitly stated physical input
\(\lvert H(r)\rvert\le e\,\mathrm{bubble}(r)\), the original
fifth-weight bound \texttt{ActualHartreeTrueQuadraticRieszCubicFiveWeight.lean}
has strict CI \texttt{42649080513} (Success).
Writing \(P=C_R(6M^2+4Me+e^2)e^2\) and
\(K=2(D^2+E^2)\), this source satisfies
\[
 (1+r)^5 |F(r)|^2\le P^2K^3\qquad(r>0).
\]
The exact elementary change of weights yields
\begin{align*}
 (1+r^2)^{7/4}&\le(1+r)^{7/2},\\
 \int_0^\infty(1+r^2)^{7/4}|F(r)|^2\,dr
 &\le P^2K^3\int_0^\infty(1+r)^{-3/2}\,dr
   =2P^2K^3.
\end{align*}
Thus this \emph{one} genuine Riesz--cubic nonlinear atom has a
native \(L^{2,7/4}\) estimate of quadratic order in \(e\), provided
the displayed source and correction-envelope hypotheses hold.
The corresponding exact Lean \texttt{MemLp} target is
\texttt{ActualHartreeTrueQuadraticRieszCubicWeightedL2.lean}.
Its successive strict compilation failures \texttt{42649085863}
and \texttt{42650866787} were concrete real-power/natural-power
cast mismatches; commit \texttt{4dda33f} replaced both invalid

Strict replay \texttt{42652808504} failed at the same casts.
Commit \texttt{3750282} switched to explicit \texttt{congrArg}
and \texttt{Real.rpow\_natCast}; run \texttt{42654440810}
then exposed an unused-variable linter error. Commit \texttt{3dda10e8}
corrected the binding, and the complete pinned
\texttt{--trust=0}, \texttt{warningAsError} replay
\textbf{strictly succeeded}: CI \texttt{42655852845},
job \texttt{129294010050}, artifact \texttt{13692702508}.
Both exact theorem checks, including the native weighted \(L^2\)
specialization, report only \texttt{propext},
\texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}. The theorem
is a genuine original Riesz--cubic weighted estimate, but is
\emph{conditional on the displayed physical }\(H\le eW\)
\emph{ bound}; it does not by itself construct the full matched

Separately the original finite four-piece nonlinear Hartree PDE

has strict CI \texttt{42648496792} (Success), job

The exact spectral PDE telescope is a nonlinear \emph{identity},

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Six-term full original Hartree Taylor remainder:
two-scale weighted \(L^2\) route (paper computation; Lean pending).}
For a measurable complex radial physical background $U$ and correction
$H$, assume for $r>0$
\[
 t=1+r,\qquad t|U(r)|^2\le K,\qquad |U(r)|\le M,
 \qquad |H(r)|\le eW(r),\quad 0\le e\le 1,
\]
with $M,K\ge0$, and $W(r)=(1+r^2)^{-1/2}$. Since
$W(r)\le 2/t$ and $t^{-2}\le W(r)^2$, the actual quartic-density
Taylor terms $\mathcal D_0=|U|^4$,
$\mathcal D_1=4|U|^2\mathrm{Re}(\overline U H)$,
and $\mathcal D_R=\mathcal D_{4,\ge2}(U,H)$ satisfy
\[
 |\mathcal D_0|\le K^2W^2,\qquad
 |\mathcal D_1|\le 8MKeW^2,\qquad
 |\mathcal D_R|\le QW^2,\quad
 Q=e^2(6M^2+4Me+e^2).
\]
The genuine positive logarithmic radial inverse-square
Riesz estimate, already strictly verified in the physical source
library, now yields absolute convergence and
\[
 |P_0(r)|\le\frac{A_0}{t},\quad
 |P_1(r)|\le\frac{A_1}{t},\quad
 |P_R(r)|\le\frac{A_R}{t},
 \quad
 (A_0,A_1,A_R)
 =C_R(K^2,8MKe,Q).
\]
Here $P_j=I_2[\mathcal D_j]$ and $C_R$ is the actual
verified radial Riesz decay constant.
The exact original cubic Taylor coefficients
$C_0=|U|^2U$, $C_1=D(|U|^2U)[H]$,
$C_R=\mathcal C_{3,\ge2}(U,H)$ obey
\[
 t^3|C_0|^2\le K^3,\qquad
 |C_1|\le\frac{B_1}{t^2},\quad B_1=6Ke,
 \qquad
 |C_R|\le\frac{B_R}{t^2},\quad
 B_R=4(3M+e)e^2.
\]
In particular, the exact nonlocal Taylor expansion is
\[
\begin{aligned}
 \mathcal N_{\mathrm H}(U+H)-\mathcal N_{\mathrm H}(U)
  -D\mathcal N_{\mathrm H}(U)[H]
   &= P_0C_R+P_1(C_1+C_R)+P_R(C_0+C_1+C_R)\\
   &= P_RC_0+\mathcal E_{\rm five}.
\end{aligned}
\]
Set $S=A_0B_R+A_1(B_1+B_R)+A_R(B_1+B_R)$.
The five remaining terms satisfy $|\mathcal E_{\rm five}|
\le St^{-3}$; the slowest actual term obeys
$t^5|P_RC_0|^2\le A_R^2K^3$. Thus,
\[
 t^5|\mathcal R_{\mathrm H}^{\ge2}(U,H)|^2
 \le 2A_R^2K^3+2S^2,
\]
and, using
$\langle r\rangle^{7/2}\le(1+r)^{7/2}$ and
$\int_0^\infty(1+r)^{-3/2}\,dr=2$,
\[
 \boxed{\ 
 \bigl\|\mathcal R_{\mathrm H}^{\ge2}(U,H)
       \bigr\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dr)}^2
  \le 4(A_R^2K^3+S^2).\ }
\]
For $0\le e\le1$, both $A_R$ and $S$ are $O(e^2)$
with a constant depending only on the displayed physical
$M,K,C_R$. Hence if $e(s)\le C_H s^{-9/8}$,
the \emph{complete original nonlinear Taylor remainder} has
$L^{2,7/4}$ norm $O(s^{-9/4})$ without a negative
power of $\ell$.

The preceding sharp $S$ is an analytic estimate for the exact six-term
formula; it is \emph{not} the conclusion of the older strict six-product
formal theorem, which used the weaker $S_{\rm loose}$ and hence cannot
supply the claimed quadratic source order.  A new strict sharp target
is pending.\par

The exact \emph{six complex-product fifth-weight algebraic estimate}
has now passed pinned strict Lean CI \texttt{42659015432},
job \texttt{129305194717}, artifact \texttt{13691974697},
with precise \texttt{\#check}, \texttt{\#print axioms},
\texttt{--trust=0}, warning-as-error and only the three allowed
transitive axioms.  It is the abstract coefficient inequality for
the six \emph{actual} original Hartree Taylor products, \emph{not}
yet a strict assembled theorem deriving every one of the physical
Riesz and cubic coefficient bounds for the same two-scale orbit
nor their combined weighted integral estimate.
The hypotheses that a real Fredholm/Kummer four-piece physical
correction $H$ obeys the uniform $eW$ envelope, solves the
original linearized principal equation, and has the necessary
moving-time/parameter bounds must still be \emph{constructed}.
The full original $\S2.13$ and final dynamical theorem remain
unaccepted.


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Strict source-derived original nonlocal two-point Riesz Lipschitz (9 November).}
The exact complex quartic-density remainder
$\mathcal D_{4,\ge2}(U,H)$ has an independently strict Lean
two-point estimate, CI \texttt{42656338008}, job
\texttt{129295702688}, artifact \texttt{13692717201}.
The corresponding \emph{genuine inverse-square radial Hartree
Riesz potential} of the source difference has now passed strict
CI \texttt{42657807431}, job \texttt{129300930631},
artifact \texttt{13692188589}; its full \texttt{--trust=0}
and warning-as-error compilation plus exact theorem axiom replay
report only \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice},

$|U|\le M$, $|H_j|\le eW$ and $|H_1-H_2|\le dW$ on $r>0$,

Riesz integrals and
\[
 \left|I_2\!\left[
  \mathcal D_{4,\ge2}(U,H_1)
  -\mathcal D_{4,\ge2}(U,H_2)\right](r)\right|
 \le {C_R(12M^2e+12Me^2+4e^3)d\over1+r}.
\]
This is a direct \emph{original nonlocal source difference}

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Actual Kummer replacement: the half-clock loss in the main proof.}
The previously proved (and separately strict-audited) genuine compact
physical Kummer repair is supported in its moving annulus
$X(s)<r\le 2X(s)$, where $X(s)=a(s)R$.
Its true selected value/slope jet only supplies
\[
 |p(s)|+R|q(s)|\le C_Ds^{-\rho},
 \qquad X(s)\le C_Xs^{1/2}.
\]
The exactly matched repair estimate in
\texttt{ActualKummerPhysicalTailWeightedLipschitz.lean}
therefore gives
\[
 |H_K(s,r)|
 \le (1+2C_X)C_D
 s^{-(\rho-1/2)}W(r),
\]
and the actual two-point selected-jet difference has the same
half-order loss. Thus for $\rho=9/8$ the strongest globally
source-derived $W$ coefficient is $e(s)=O(s^{-5/8})$,
whereas the full nonlinear Hartree Taylor weighted-$L^2$
theorem needs $e(s)=O(s^{-9/8})$ to deliver
$s^{-9/4}$. Simply inserting the weaker global bound
would give only $O(s^{-5/4})$.

The obstruction is \emph{geometric}, not a Lean compiler
or Kummer $C^1$ error. A complete proof must either establish
an extra half-order on the actual selected upper jet, exploit the
original shell support and two-scale nonlocal radial Riesz geometry
\emph{before} taking the full $W$ norm, or perform enough
genuinely constructed physical correction steps to recover the loss.
The new strictly accepted six-product nonlocal source estimates
and original quadratic Riesz parameter differences give genuine
analytic inputs but do not alone construct the required corrected
four-zone Hartree trajectory. Consequently revised~\S2.13 and the
final PDE-derived Type--II rate are still open at 11/13.



\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Localized moving Kummer shell: recovery of three eighths of a time order (9 November; paper-level conditional estimate, not a Lean acceptance).}
A global substitution of the true Kummer repair bound
$|H_K|\lesssim s^{-\rho+1/2}W$ loses a full half-order
before exploiting its annular support.  The correct \emph{original
three-dimensional radial Riesz} calculation gives a sharper
conditional bound and identifies the remaining loss.

Let $X\ge1$, $A_X=\{x\in\mathbb R^3:X\le|x|\le2X\}$,
and assume the radial physical background and its original
Hartree potential satisfy
\[
 |U(r)|\le C_0(1+r)^{-1/2},\qquad
 |I_2[|U|^4](r)|\le C_1(1+r)^{-1}.
\]
Suppose $H$ is supported in $A_X$ and obeys
$|H|\le e$ and $eX^{1/2}\le c_0$, where $e\ge0$.
With the genuine quartic density
$\mathcal D_1=D(|U|^4)[H]$ and
$\mathcal D_R=|U+H|^4-|U|^4-\mathcal D_1$,
the original complex cubic expansion gives, on the shell,
\[
 |\mathcal D_1|\lesssim eX^{-3/2},\quad
 |\mathcal D_R|\lesssim e^2X^{-1},\quad
 |C_1|\lesssim eX^{-1},\quad
 |C_R|\lesssim e^2X^{-1/2}.
\]
The constants depend on $C_0,c_0$.
For every $r\ge0$, the actual inverse-square kernel yields
\[
 \begin{split}
 |I_2[\mathcal D_R](r)|
 &\le C e^2\min\{1,X^2/r^2\},\\
 |I_2[\mathcal D_1](r)|
 &\le C eX^{-1/2}\min\{1,X^2/r^2\},
 \end{split}
\]
where the minimum is interpreted as $1$ at $r=0$.
Indeed, on writing $x=Xz$ and $y=Xw$,
\[
 \int_{A_X}\frac{dy}{|x-y|^2}
 =X\int_{1\le|w|\le2}\frac{dw}{|z-w|^2}
 \le C X \min\{1,X^2/|x|^2\}.
\]
The dimension-three local integrability of $|z-w|^{-2}$
and the estimate $|z-w|\ge |z|/2$ for $|z|\ge4$
give the uniform constant in this bound.

Expanding the \emph{complete} original nonlinear Taylor source,
\[
 \mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}
  =P_0C_R+P_1(C_1+C_R)+P_R(C_0+C_1+C_R),
 \quad P_j=I_2[\mathcal D_j],
\]
therefore gives
\[
 |\mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}(r)|
 \le C e^2X^{-3/2}\quad(X\le r\le2X).
\]
Off the shell, $C_1=C_R=0$; although $P_1$ is generally
nonzero there, its multiplier vanishes, so only $P_RC_0$ remains.  Consequently,
\[
 |\mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}(r)|
 \le C e^2(1+r)^{-3/2}
   \begin{cases}
    1,&0\le r\le4X,\\
    X^2/r^2,&r>4X.
   \end{cases}
\]
The two displayed bounds, integrated against the native weight,
give
\[
 \begin{split}
 \|\mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dr)}^2
 &\le C e^4\left[
   \int_0^{4X}(1+r)^{1/2}\,dr
  +X^4\int_{4X}^{\infty}r^{-7/2}\,dr\right]\\
 &\le C e^4X^{3/2},\\
 \boxed{\ 
 \|\mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dr)}
 \le C e^2X^{3/4}.\ }
 \end{split}
\]
Thus for a \emph{constructed} physical shell with
$e(s)\le C_Ds^{-\rho}$ and $X(s)\le C_Xs^{1/2}$,
\[
 \|\mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}(s)\|_{L^{2,7/4}}
 \lesssim s^{-2\rho+3/8}
 =s^{-15/8}\quad\text{when }\rho=9/8.
\]
This improves on the coarse global-envelope estimate
$s^{-5/4}$ and produces a genuine \emph{relative}
$s^{-3/4}$ gain over the first $s^{-9/8}$ shell amplitude.
It does \emph{not} recover the previously desired
$s^{-9/4}$ quadratic order.  More importantly, it is a
conditional \emph{static spatial} estimate: the same-source
moving four-region correction, its entire time-differentiated
linearized PDE defect, the selected-speed parameter difference,
and the finite physical profile recurrence have not been constructed
or strictly checked.  One must verify that the original recurrence
can close with the weaker $3/4$ positive relative gain rather than
silently assuming the stronger full-bubble $s^{-9/4}$ bound.
This computation does not change the accepted 11/13 environments.

For the worst-case admissible shell size $X(s)\asymp s^{1/2}$,
the absolute-value bound $e^2X^{3/4}$ reaches $s^{-9/4}$
only if
\[
 2\rho-\frac38\ge\frac94,
 \qquad \rho\ge\frac{21}{16}
   =\frac98+\frac{3}{16}.
\]
Thus the currently proposed $\rho=9/8$ misses this
\emph{particular} target by $s^{-3/8}$ in the nonlinear
source, or equivalently needs $s^{-3/16}$ additional
decay in $e$. A genuine signed cancellation, tighter physical
shell radius, or a finite PDE iteration requiring merely the
verified \emph{positive} $3/4$ relative gain could bypass this
criterion. None is established by the present static estimate.

The $X^{3/4}$ exponent cannot be reduced using only the preceding
size, support and energy bounds.  To see this, take smooth nonnegative
radial cutoffs $\phi,\psi$ supported in $[1,2]$ and bounded below
by a positive constant on $[4/3,5/3]$, and put
\[
 U_X(r)=X^{-1/2}\phi(r/X),\qquad
 H_X(r)=e\psi(r/X),\quad eX^{1/2}\le1.
\]
These are finite-energy radial profiles, with
$\|\nabla U_X\|_{L^2(\mathbb R^3)}=O(1)$,
and satisfy uniform bounds of the displayed background class.
Every coefficient in the positive real Hartree Taylor expansion is
nonnegative.  On $4X/3\le r\le5X/3$,
\[
 \mathcal D_R(U_X,H_X)\gtrsim e^2X^{-1},
 \quad I_2[\mathcal D_R](r)\gtrsim e^2,
 \quad C_0(r)\gtrsim X^{-3/2},
\]
hence
\[
 \|\mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}(U_X,H_X)\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dr)}
 \gtrsim e^2X^{3/4}.
\]
Thus an $e^2$ bound uniform in $X$ is \emph{false for this
general radial-energy size class}. A better order for the
\emph{actual} Hartree orbit must exploit additional dynamical
structure (cancellation, stronger selected jet or radial-energy tail
smallness), not just a sharper use of the same absolute-value
inequalities.  No such dynamical improvement is yet certified.

The same argument has a \emph{static two-point} companion.
Let $H_1,H_2$ be supported in the same $A_X$, and assume
$|H_j|\le e$, $|H_1-H_2|\le d$ and $eX^{1/2}\le c_0$.
The explicit original quartic and cubic polynomials give on $A_X$
\[
 \begin{array}{ll}
 |\mathcal D_R(U,H_1)-\mathcal D_R(U,H_2)|
       \le C X^{-1}ed,&
 |C_R(U,H_1)-C_R(U,H_2)|
       \le C X^{-1/2}ed,\\
 |\mathcal D_1(U,H_1)-\mathcal D_1(U,H_2)|
       \le C X^{-3/2}d,&
 |C_1(U,H_1)-C_1(U,H_2)|
       \le C X^{-1}d.
 \end{array}
\]
The same scaled genuine Riesz kernel and the exact six-product
difference then yield
\[
 \boxed{\ 
 \|\mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}(U,H_1)-
   \mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}(U,H_2)\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dr)}
 \le C\,e\,d\,X^{3/4}.\ }
\]
In particular, the coefficient for two corrections at a fixed
physical shell is $O(s^{-\rho+3/8})=O(s^{-3/4})$
when $\rho=9/8$.

The original pointwise cubic-remainder difference estimate used above,
\[
 \bigl\|C_R(u,h)-C_R(u,k)\bigr\|
 \le (6Me+3e^2)d,\qquad
 \|u\|\le M,\quad \|h\|,\|k\|\le e,\quad
 \|h-k\|\le d,
\]
is now \emph{independently strict Lean success} in
\texttt{ActualHartreeCubicRemainderDirectLipschitz.lean}:
CI \texttt{42672215921}, original job \texttt{129348755922},
artifact \texttt{13700110697}, source commit \texttt{d870286}.
The actual job used pinned Lean 4.35.0-rc3, pinned Mathlib,
\texttt{--trust=0}, warning-as-error, exact \texttt{\#check}
and \texttt{\#print axioms}; the sole transitive axioms are
\texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}.
This proves the original \emph{cubic factor} of the static
difference rather than borrowing a generic assumed Lipschitz
source bound.  The full shell-sensitive nonlocal \(L^{2,7/4}\)
two-point result in the preceding display remains a paper
calculation, not a separately accepted Lean target.

This is \emph{not} the required full parameter-Lipschitz theorem
for the physical moving orbit: varying $X(s,\theta)$ changes the
support, its boundary jets and time derivative, and the other
matched noncompact regions and the selected speeds must be included.




\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Strict original six-term nonlocal weighted estimate
(9 November; static source integration).}
The abstract fifth-weight six-product estimate is now joined to the
\emph{actual} radial inverse-square Riesz potentials of the complex
quartic density and its linear and nonlinear variations, rather than
assuming those three potential bounds independently.  Namely, for
measurable $U,H$ with
\[
 |U(r)|\le M,\qquad (1+r)|U(r)|^2\le K,\qquad
 |H(r)|\le e\,W(r)\quad (r>0),\qquad M,K,e\ge0,
\]
put $C=C_{\rm Riesz}>0$ and
\[
 A_0=K^2C,\quad A_1=8MKeC,\quad
 A_R=(6M^2+4Me+e^2)e^2C,\quad
 Q=2\left[
 A_R^2K^3+
 \bigl((A_0+A_1+A_R)(6Ke+4(3M+e)e^2)\bigr)^2\right].
\]
Then the entire original complex nonlinear Hartree Taylor remainder
$\mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}(U,H)$ is measurable and belongs to the
native weighted space $L^{2,7/4}(dr)$, with
\[
 \left\|\mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}(U,H)
 \right\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dr)}^2
 \le Q\int_0^\infty(1+r)^{-3/2}\,dr=2Q.
\]
For $e\le1$ this particular, literally verified bound only yields
an $O(e)$ weighted norm estimate: its coefficient
$S_{\rm loose}=(A_0+A_1+A_R)(B_1+B_R)$ contains the spurious
first-order product $A_0B_1=O(e)$, even though the actual Taylor
remainder contains no $P_0C_1$.  The mathematically correct source
coefficient is
$S_{\rm sharp}=A_0B_R+(A_1+A_R)(B_1+B_R)=O(e^2)$.
The independent Lean theorem
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_six\_original\_nonlocal\_Taylor\_products\_fifth\_weight\_sharp}
has now passed strict CI \texttt{42675245110} (original job
\texttt{129354762216}, evidence \texttt{13700530607}, exact
\texttt{\#check}/\texttt{\#print axioms} and only the three allowed
transitive axioms).  This algebraic proof is independently accepted;
its three-stage original Riesz source-to-native-weighted-$L^2$ sharp
assembly has now \emph{strictly succeeded} in CI \texttt{42675724789},
original job \texttt{129356428536}, artifact \texttt{13699654205}.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Explicit quadratic coefficient of the six-term source.}
The sharp Taylor grouping excludes the nonexistent first-order
product $P_0 C_1$.  When $0\le e\le1$ and $M,K\ge0$, put
\[
 C_*=(6M^2+4M+1)C_R,\qquad
 S_*=4K^2C_R(3M+1)
 +(8MKC_R+C_*)(6K+4(3M+1)).
\]
The genuine quartic Riesz and cubic Taylor constants then satisfy
\[
 A_R\le C_*e^2,\quad
 A_1+A_R\le(8MKC_R+C_*)e,\quad
 B_R\le4(3M+1)e^2,\quad
 B_1+B_R\le(6K+4(3M+1))e
\]
and thus
\[
 S_{\rm sharp}=A_0B_R+(A_1+A_R)(B_1+B_R)\le S_*e^2,
 \qquad
 Q_{\rm sharp}\le 2(C_*^2K^3+S_*^2)e^4.
\]
The final quadratic weighted source bound, \emph{provided that the
physical bubble envelope for $H$ is independently established}, is
\[
 \boxed{\;
 \|\mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}(U,H)\|_{L^{2,7/4}(dr)}
 \le 2\sqrt{C_*^2K^3+S_*^2}\;e^2 .
 \;}
\]
The pointwise sharp six-product inequality is strict Lean
CI \texttt{42675245110}.  The entire \emph{genuine} inverse-square
Hartree source-to-native weighted-$L^2$ sharp theorem has independently
passed strict Lean CI \texttt{42675724789}, original job
\texttt{129356428536}, artifact \texttt{13699654205}.
Its original job log certifies \texttt{--trust=0}, warning-as-error,

Riesz rows from the primitive field bounds.
Its explicit factor $e^2$ is \emph{not} a temporal gain for

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The actual unweighted spectral Cramer derivative on a
shrinking matching annulus.}
For the original complete matched Green field
\(u_0(r)=u_{\rm Cramer}(r;F,U,DU)\), let
\(q(r)=d\,u_0(r)\) and \(u_1=G_{\rm Cramer}[q;0,0]\).
The only spectral source-size input is the original profile bound
\[
 \sup_{a\le r\le b}|r\,u_0(r)|\le H,\qquad
 |q(r)|\le |d|H/r,\qquad 0<a\le b<R.
\]
Assume the regularized origin Kummer pair and its first derivatives are
bounded by \(G\), and its origin coefficients have bound \(D\).
The refined inverse-linear Green moment difference and the original
regularized Wronskian derivative identity yield the pointwise
\emph{unweighted} estimate
\[
 \boxed{\quad
 \big|\partial_r u_1(r)\big|
 \le C_{\rm spec}(D,G,b)\,|d|H,\qquad
 C_{\rm spec}(D,G,b)
 =(b+1)G\{1+(D+1)Gb^2\}+2(D+1)G^2b^2.
 \quad}
\]
In particular the exact spectral-corrected profile satisfies
\[
 \big|\partial_r(u_0-u_1)(r)-\partial_r u_0(r)\big|
 \le C_{\rm spec}(D,G,b)\,|d|H,
\]
with no negative power of the shrinking left endpoint \(a\).
For \(r_0=L/a_{\rm phys}\) and \(y=a_{\rm phys}r\), the
corresponding physical radial-energy consequence is
\[
 \int_L^{a_{\rm phys}b} y^2
 \left|\partial_y u_1(y/a_{\rm phys})\right|^2\,dy
 \le \frac{a_{\rm phys}}3(b^3-r_0^3)
       C_{\rm spec}(D,G,b)^2|d|^2H^2.
\]
The factor \(a_{\rm phys}\) is genuine and is not suppressed by this
spatial gain.  The origin data \(U,DU\), first profile and spectral source
are those of the original source-matched Cramer field; the unique real
Hartree selector and physical source-to-\(H\) producer are supplied by
the preceding matched-source results.  The proposed precise Lean
statements

\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_original\_spectral\_corrected\_derivative\_gap\_no\_left\_radius}

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Necessary power budget for absolute time integration of the shell-sensitive source.}
The static annular estimate, applied with the conditional scales
$e(s)\lesssim s^{-9/8}$ and $X(s)\lesssim s^{1/2}$, yields at best
\[
 \|\mathcal R_{\rm H}^{\ge2}(s)\|_{L^{2,7/4}}\lesssim s^{-15/8}.
\]
A nonoscillatory, undamped future-time Duhamel integration on this
specific absolute source class gives the sharp scalar model
\[
 \int_s^\infty \tau^{-15/8}\,d\tau
   =\frac87s^{-7/8},\qquad
 \frac{(8/7)s^{-7/8}}{s^{-9/8}}
   =\frac87s^{1/4}\longrightarrow\infty.
\]
Thus a spatial gain $s^{-3/4}$ relative to the first $s^{-9/8}$
profile is not sufficient, by itself, to establish a time-integrated
recurrence in the same $s^{-9/8}$ class: a full $s$ power is lost
in the primitive. This is only an obstruction to absolute,
undamped time-Duhamel closure. It does not preclude oscillatory
resolvent gains, a localized inverse, cancellations from the
unique real speed, or a different PDE-based recurrence.

For a source shell with actual amplitude $s^{-\rho}$ and spatial
radius $s^\alpha$, the static shell-bound exponent is
$-2\rho+3\alpha/4$. A bare time primitive can preserve a
$s^{-\rho}$ first-profile class only if
\[
 2\rho-\frac34\alpha-1\ge\rho,
 \qquad\boxed{\rho\ge 1+\frac34\alpha}.
\]
With $\rho=9/8$ this requires $\alpha\le1/6$; with
$\alpha=1/2$ it requires $\rho\ge11/8$. At the currently
proposed $(\rho,\alpha)=(9/8,1/2)$, the actual nonlinear PDE
inverse would have to gain a further factor $s^{-1/4}$ beyond
the plain absolute primitive, or the physical source must exhibit
compensating cancellation or stronger bounds. None of these
additional estimates has been proved.

The four exact real-power/threshold relations have now
\emph{strictly passed} their independent pinned Lean audit,
\texttt{ActualHartreeShellDuhamelExponent.lean}, workflow
\texttt{hbl-213-shell-duhamel-exponent.yml},
CI \texttt{42710992024}, original job \texttt{129480154155},
evidence artifact \texttt{13715119032}. The cleaned workflow
was independently replayed in CI \texttt{42711293523},
job \texttt{129481203391}, artifact \texttt{13716580579}
and also passed the same four strict theorem/axiom checks. The original job
confirms Lean 4.35.0-rc3, the exact pinned Mathlib commit,
\texttt{--trust=0}, warning-as-error and four individual
\texttt{\#check}/\texttt{\#print axioms} reports with only

The time integral itself is elementary paper calculus;
the formal four-declaration check covers the exact power identities
and the two algebraic necessary conditions, not an indefinite or
improper integral theorem.
These necessary exponent relations alone establish neither a
Hartree PDE recurrence nor revised original Section 2.13;
the formal count remains $11/13$.
