\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The same source's moving Green coefficient time calculus (10 November).}
Fix one origin-normalized Kummer pair \(K\) on the physical annulus.
Set \(r_0(s)=\ell(s)^{-1/16}/a(s)\), and let
\(F(s,r)\) be the \emph{same} genuine quartic nonlocal Hartree Riesz
source, adjusted by the unique two real Cramer-selected modulation
directions. The two Green coefficients at the common fixed upper
endpoint \(B\) are
\[
 A_*(s)=\int_{r_0(s)}^{B}
    \frac{X(r)F(s,r)}{W(r)}\,dr,\qquad
 B_*(s)=\int_{r_0(s)}^{B}
    \frac{Y(r)F(s,r)}{W(r)}\,dr.
\]
Under the original interval-integrability, pointwise differentiability,
and integrable source-time dominating conditions, as well as the actual
physical parameter derivatives, the two (componentwise) Leibniz
identities are
\[
\begin{aligned}
 A_*'(s)
 &=\int_{r_0(s)}^B
     \frac{X(r)\partial_sF(s,r)}{W(r)}\,dr
    -r_0'(s)\frac{X(r_0(s))F(s,r_0(s))}{W(r_0(s))},\\
 B_*'(s)
 &=\int_{r_0(s)}^B
     \frac{Y(r)\partial_sF(s,r)}{W(r)}\,dr
    -r_0'(s)\frac{Y(r_0(s))F(s,r_0(s))}{W(r_0(s))},
\end{aligned}
\]
where the lower-endpoint velocity follows by the physical chain rule,
\[
 r_0'(s)=
 \frac{-\frac1{16}\ell(s)^{-17/16}\ell'(s)a(s)
           -\ell(s)^{-1/16}a'(s)}{a(s)^2}.
\]
The cross term in the joint source/endpoint difference is controlled,
on its actual moving interval, by
\(O(|t-s|\,|r_0(t)-r_0(s)|)\). Thus its time derivative vanishes
without positing an independently vanishing error term.

The original \emph{regular} coefficient \(B_*\) is already
strictly audited in GitHub CI \texttt{42727196085}, original job
\texttt{129535736440}, evidence \texttt{13723108682}. The
original \emph{singular} coefficient \(A_*\) has now been coded in
\texttt{ActualOriginGreenMovingSingularChannel.lean} and
\texttt{ActualHartreeOnePhysicalMovingSingularGreenFullDerivative.lean};
its separate strict CI \texttt{42728546668} is now
\emph{Success}, original job \texttt{129540373450}, evidence
\texttt{13723728856}, with five exact \texttt{\#check} and
\texttt{\#print axioms} passing the required toolchain,
\texttt{--trust=0}, warning-as-error and only the allowed three axioms.

Moreover, the original singular-channel ratio itself has a
radius-uniform cancellation.  Since the actual normalized
regular mode is \(K.x(r)\), the physical singular density numerator
is \(X(r)=K.x(r)/r\); the exact original Wronskian satisfies
\(\lvert W(r)^{-1}\rvert=r^2\) for \(r>0\).
Therefore for \(0<r\le B\le\mathrm{scale}\), provided
\(\lVert K.x(r)\rVert\le G\),
\[
 \boxed{\quad
 \left\lVert\frac{X(r)}{W(r)}\right\rVert
   =r\,\lVert K.x(r)\rVert\le G B .\quad}
\]
No factor \(r_0(s)^{-1}\) is hidden in this
original outgoing-channel time multiplier. The precise result
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_original\_singular\_Green\_density\_ratio\_no\_left\_radius}
is \emph{strictly verified}: corrected CI \texttt{42730062712},
original job \texttt{129545514697}, artifact \texttt{13724177375}.
Original CI \texttt{42729750826} failed solely because
\texttt{field\_simp} had already closed the goal, making the trailing
\texttt{ring} tactic superfluous; this was removed before successful
reverification. This establishes the *geometric absence* of a shrinking
left-radius loss under the explicit original regularized-mode bound,
not an a priori time-decay estimate.

For both original physical Green coefficients, the source derivative
majorants, differentiability of the physical path, and original
Wronskian density bounds are explicit analytic premises. In particular these identities do not imply a
uniform moving Kummer-basis derivative, the required weighted
\(s^{-9/4}\) four-region PDE residual, the parameter-Lipschitz
recurrence or the dynamically derived Type--II rate. The original
section acceptance remains \(11/13\).


\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Sharper moving-interface source-density factors from the true origin IVP.}
The original normalized Kummer fundamental functions satisfy
\[
 X(r)=\frac{K.x(r)}{r},\qquad
 Y(r)=\frac{K.y(r)}{r},\qquad
 \left|W(r)^{-1}\right|=r^2,\qquad 0<r\le B.
\]
Under the actual regularized ODE bound
\(\lVert K.x(r)\rVert+\lVert K.y(r)\rVert\le 2G\),
the exact two Green source multipliers obey
\[
 \left|\frac{X(r)}{W(r)}\right|\le Gr,\qquad
 \left|\frac{Y(r)}{W(r)}\right|\le Gr.
\]
The common weakened upper bound \(GB\) has been separately strictly
accepted in CI \texttt{42732663207}, job \texttt{129554267366},
artifact \texttt{13724284456}, including both exact Lean theorem
types and permitted axiom audits.

More sharply, the original regular Frobenius solution is
\(X(r)=1+O(r^2)\).  If the frozen coefficient has budget \(D\ge0\)
and its original normalized IVP has budget \(G\ge0\), the exact
origin-ODE asymptotic estimate implies
\[
 \left|\frac{X(r)}{W(r)}\right|
 \le \bigl(1+(D+1)GB^2\bigr)r^2,\qquad 0<r\le B.
\]
This \(r^2\) is a genuine *quadratic positive radius gain*
on the singular/outgoing Green source density, and should not be
confused with an inverse power of the moving left interface.
For the physical moving slit
\(I_{s,t}=[r_0(s),r_0(t)]\) with \(0<r\le R\), the exact mixed
source/endpoint identity consequently has the conditional bound
\[
 \|\Delta_{s,t}A\|
 \le \bigl(1+(D+1)GR^2\bigr)R^2 E\,
        |t-s|\,|r_0(t)-r_0(s)|,
\]
provided the *same genuine Cramer-selected nonlocal Hartree source*
has \(\|\partial_\tau F(\tau,r)\|\le E\) on the intervening
time segment, with the original Riesz dominated-integral hypotheses.
No independently specified positive-time source order is introduced.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Conditional source-time weight audit (10 November 2026).}
The strictly proved Frobenius estimate for the original moving outgoing
Green density is
\[
 \left|\frac{X(r)}{W(r)}\right|
 \le C_{\mathrm{Frob}}(R)r^2,\qquad
 C_{\mathrm{Frob}}(R)=1+(D+1)GR^2,\qquad 0<r\le R.
\]
The audited mixed Green result with an extra \(R^2\) requires
\emph{uniform} \(\lvert\partial_t F_{\mathrm{adj}}(t,r)\rvert\le E\)
on the moving slit. The original weighted source envelope
\(\lvert F_{\mathrm{adj}}(t,r)\rvert\lesssim Q(t)r^{-2}\)
does not imply any such derivative bound.
Indeed, even if an independent original-PDE calculation yielded only
\(\lvert\partial_t F_{\mathrm{adj}}(t,r)\rvert\le E(t)r^{-2}\),
the fixed-basis Green density would obey only
\[
 \left|\partial_t\left(
   \frac{X(r)F_{\mathrm{adj}}(t,r)}{W(r)}\right)\right|
 \le C_{\mathrm{Frob}}(R)E(t).
\]
Integrating over the \emph{actual} moving slit gives the conditional bound
\[
 |\Delta_{s,t}A|
 \le C_{\mathrm{Frob}}(R)\sup_{\tau\in[\min(s,t),\max(s,t)]}E(\tau)\,
   |t-s|\,|r_0(t)-r_0(s)|,
\]
without an additional \(R^2\). Thus the Frobenius radius gain removes
the possible inverse-radius loss but cannot alone furnish a time-positive
source estimate. Recovering an \(R^\eta\) gain requires a
PDE-derived improvement
\(\lvert\partial_tF_{\mathrm{adj}}\rvert
\lesssim E(t)r^{-2+\eta}\), \(\eta>0\), or a proved
oscillatory/compatibility cancellation before absolute values.
This is a conditional power audit, not an additional Lean-accepted
theorem, and does not change the original \(11/13\) count.



\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Primitive half-radial velocity criterion for the genuine Riesz time rows (10 November; strict CI in progress).}
For the original three-dimensional radial inverse-square Hartree potential
\[
 {\cal I}_2[m](r)=\frac{2\pi\kappa}{r}
 \int_0^\infty \rho\log\frac{r+\rho}{|r-\rho|}\,m(\rho)\,d\rho,
 \qquad W(\rho)=(1+\rho^2)^{-1/2},
\]
the already established exact radial row estimate yields
\[
 |{\cal I}_2[m](r)|\le C_{\rm row} A,\qquad r>0,
 \quad |m(\rho)|\le A W(\rho)^2,\ \rho>0.
\]
For the \emph{actual} complex field $z$ and its true time velocity $\dot z$,
the exact quartic polarization gives
\[
 \partial_t|z|^4=4|z|^2\operatorname{Re}(\overline z\dot z),
 \qquad |\partial_t|z|^4|\le8|z|^3|\dot z|.
\]
Hence the \emph{primitive} bounds
\[
 |z(t,\rho)|\le M_z(t)\sqrt{W(\rho)},\qquad
 |\dot z(t,\rho)|\le D_z(t)\sqrt{W(\rho)}
\]
give the derived, rather than postulated, weighted velocity estimate
\[
 |\partial_t|z(t,\rho)|^4|
 \le8M_z(t)^3D_z(t)W(\rho)^2.
\]
For the same real core $V$ and complex radiation $z$, the true mixed
quartic density $m=|V+z|^4-|V|^4-|z|^4$ obeys
\[
 |\partial_t m(t,\rho)|
 \le8\!\left[(M_V+M_z)^3(D_V+D_z)
             +M_V^3D_V+M_z^3D_z\right]W(\rho)^2,
\]
whenever $|V|,|z|,|\partial_tV|,|\partial_tz|$ are respectively bounded by
$M_V\sqrt W,M_z\sqrt W,D_V\sqrt W,D_z\sqrt W$.
Under the original measurable/integrable logarithmic-kernel dominated
differentiation hypotheses, these produce the \emph{true potential}
time derivatives and the radius-uniform bounds
\[
 \left|\partial_t{\cal I}_2[|z|^4](t,r)\right|
 \le8C_{\rm row}M_z^3D_z,\qquad
 \left|\partial_t{\cal I}_2[m](t,r)\right|
 \le8C_{\rm row}\left[(M_V+M_z)^3(D_V+D_z)
             +M_V^3D_V+M_z^3D_z\right],
 \quad r>0.
\]
The three prospective Lean modules are
\texttt{ActualHartreeRieszTimeUniformFromOriginalVelocity.lean},
\texttt{ActualHartreeHalfRadialVelocityTimeRiesz.lean}, and
\texttt{ActualHartreeMixedHalfRadialVelocityTimeRiesz.lean};
the strict replacement workflows are respectively
\texttt{42741408285}, \texttt{42741413915}, and \texttt{42741684534}.
These statements are \emph{not accepted until their actual strict CI
and exact axiom audits have succeeded}. They retain the genuine
nonlocal three-Riesz kernels and differentiated physical fields, rather
than assuming a differentiated Hartree potential envelope.

The necessary half-radial bounds on the \emph{two-stage critically scaled}
core/radiation velocities have not yet been propagated by the physical
four-region PDE. Even after those are obtained, the two evolving
Cramer-selected modulation directions must be combined with the
primitive source derivative using the exact existing bound
\[
 \|\partial_tF_{\rm adj}(t,r)\|
 \le \|\partial_tF_0(t,r)\|+
 \frac{1024C_s}{B^2r}
       \|\dot{\mathfrak Z}+p_b\dot{\mathfrak b}
                              +p_\omega\dot{\mathfrak w}\|
 +|p_b|\|\partial_tS_b\|+|p_\omega|\|\partial_tS_\omega\|.
\]
In particular the primitive Riesz row bounds \emph{alone} do not
establish the required weighted estimate
$r\|\partial_tF_{\rm adj}(t,r)\|\lesssim E(t)$, much less the
full four-region $s^{-9/4}$ residual or its parameter Lipschitz norm.
A true $r^{-1}$ source-time budget would leave an additional
$R$ factor after multiplication by the Frobenius $r^2$ Green
multiplier, whereas the currently established possible $r^{-2}$
budget leaves none. The original revised proof count remains $11/13$.



\medskip
\noindent\textbf{True Cramer source to one-radius Frobenius gain (10 November; strict CI pending).}
The already proved unique real Cramer-selected derivative
\(\partial_tF_{\rm adj}=D\), with exact original Hartree source
\(F_0\) and two differentiated scale/phase directions, has the
radius-weighted consequence
\[
 \boxed{\quad
 r\|D\|\le r\|\dot F_0\|
   +\frac{1024C_s}{B^2}
     \|\dot{\mathfrak Z}+p_b\dot{\mathfrak b}
                          +p_\omega\dot{\mathfrak w}\|
   +|p_b|r\|\dot S_b\|+|p_\omega|r\|\dot S_\omega\|.
 \quad}
\]
The \(r^{-1}\) bound of the original differentiation theorem
has cancelled \emph{exactly}; no modulus of the moving left interface
\(r_0^{-1}\) is introduced. A new Lean target
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_true\_moving\_selected\_source\_radius\_weighted\_time\_budget}
uses the true physical source and real Cramer determinant directly;
its submitted strict CI is
\texttt{42742271094} (pending at writing).

Inserting the physically differentiated \emph{same selected source}
into the exact Frobenius outgoing Green kernel gives a sharper
moving-slit estimate at the threshold
\[
 r\|\partial_tF_{\rm adj}(t,r)\|\le E(t),
 \quad 0<r\le R,\qquad
 \left|\frac{X(r)}{W(r)}\right|
 \le C_{\mathrm{Frob}}(R)r^2.
\]
For a uniform bound \(E\) on the true intervening time segment,
the original moving-interface mixed difference then satisfies
\[
 \boxed{\quad
 \|\Delta_{s,t}A\|
 \le C_{\rm Frob}(R)\,R E\,
      |t-s|\,|r_0(t)-r_0(s)|.
 \quad}
\]
In particular an \(r^{-1}\), rather than \(r^{-2}\),
physical source derivative leaves a full \emph{positive spatial
radius} \(R\) after multiplying the exact Green density.
This remains different from an \emph{a priori positive time-order}.
The genuine nonlocal selected-source Green theorem is submitted
as \texttt{actual\_Hartree\_true\_moving\_singular\_Green\_cross\_Frobenius\_one\_inverse\_radius},
CI \texttt{42742808852} (pending at writing).
Neither theorem is accepted before the exact strict jobs succeed.

The final step is not an abstract weighted-source premise: the
right-hand side of the first displayed inequality must be estimated
from the SAME physical two-stage scaled core/radiation PDE orbit and
its differentiated moment/Cramer data, with correct \(s\)-dependence,
and then inserted into all four Kummer-region residuals and their
Lipschitz comparison. No finite physical recurrence or Type-II rate
is proved here; unchanged formal count \(11/13\).



\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Strict original PDE telescope and physical Riesz velocity evidence
(10 November; no new original theorem acceptance).}
The nonlinear finite spectral four-piece Hartree PDE identity
\[
  \mathcal E_{\rm H}\bigl(U_0+\sum_{j=1}^n w_j\bigr)
  -\mathcal E_{\rm H}(U_0)
 =\sum_{j=1}^n
   \left[
     \mathcal E_{\rm H}\bigl(U_0+\sum_{k=j}^n w_k\bigr)
    -\mathcal E_{\rm H}\bigl(U_0+\sum_{k=j+1}^n w_k\bigr)
   \right]
\]
is now strictly compiled for the \emph{literal original complex quartic
nonlocal Hartree residual}, with each $w_j$ the genuine
spectral-corrected four-region field. The original pinned
\texttt{--trust=0}, warning-as-error job is
\texttt{42648496792}, job \texttt{129268151514}; the exact

\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_finite\_spectral\_Fredholm\_four\_piece\_full\_PDE\_telescopes}
reports only \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice},

\texttt{42530158681} was superseded, not silently relabelled.
The true same-selected-source spectral three-$C^1$ and quadratic
tests additionally passed in \texttt{42648496711},
\texttt{42650515902} and \texttt{42650515961}.
This formula is a \emph{telescoping identity}; it gives no upper bound
for any nonlinear summand and no $s^{-9/4}$ conclusion.

The original quartic Riesz derivative rows from the full true
half-radial physical field/velocity polarizations have since passed

\texttt{42743242786} (job \texttt{129589176394}; two declarations).
The source-time Cramer radius-weight cancellation has also passed
in \texttt{42742271094}, job \texttt{129585960999}.
Every cited successful raw audit uses the fixed compiler and Mathlib,

The first targeted true-source moving-slit Frobenius one-radius theorem,

The transitive axiom list is exactly
\texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}.

For application of this lemma to the physically derived
Cramer source-time inequality, a separate geometric bridge is
still necessary: the latter is obtained only for
\(1/a(\tau)\le r\le B\), whereas the former needs a source
time derivative at every \(\tau\in[s,t]\) and every
\(r\) in the slit between the \emph{two moving} lower endpoints.
Consequently it is necessary to prove
\[
  \frac{1}{a(\tau)}
   \le \min\left\{
        \frac{\ell(s)^{-1/16}}{a(s)},
        \frac{\ell(t)^{-1/16}}{a(t)}
      \right\},\qquad
 \tau\in[\min(s,t),\max(s,t)],
\]
together with \(R\le B\) for the full moving slit
\(r\le R\), or another explicit annulus extension from the
same PDE orbit, before combining the two strict theorems.
These geometric inequalities are sufficient to make the
original Cramer-source estimate applicable to every intermediate
\(\tau,r\). They do \emph{not} themselves control the resulting
right-hand side in time, and have not yet been proved
for the final physical recurrence.

The remaining core PDE obligation is not the algebraic
Cramer radius cancellation, but the \emph{same-orbit} quantitative
right-hand side
\[
 r\|\partial_sF_{\rm adj}\|
 \le r\|\dot F_0\|+
    \frac{1024C_s}{B^2}
     \|\dot{\mathfrak Z}+p_b\dot{\mathfrak b}
       +p_\omega\dot{\mathfrak w}\|
   +|p_b|r\|\dot S_b\|+|p_\omega|r\|\dot S_\omega\|,
\]
together with the moving Kummer basis/endpoint derivatives and a

acceptance is exactly \textbf{11/13}.



\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The moving-slit physical-shell bridge from scale variation
(10 November 2026; independent strict Lean replay pending).}
For the \emph{actual} modulation scale $a(\tau)>0$ and slow inner
cutoff $L(\tau)=\ell(\tau)^{-1/16}$, put
\[
 r_-(\tau)=\frac{L(\tau)}{a(\tau)},\qquad
 J_{s,t}=[\min(s,t),\max(s,t)],\qquad
 V_{s,t}=\int_{J_{s,t}}|a'(\tau)|\,d\tau.
\]
Assume $a$ is absolutely continuous on $J_{s,t}$,
$\ell(s),\ell(t)>0$ and the two actual slow inner endpoints
satisfy $r_-(s),r_-(t)\le B$. The explicit scale-variation conditions
\[
 \boxed{\quad
 V_{s,t} L(s)\le a(s)(L(s)-1),\qquad
 V_{s,t} L(t)\le a(t)(L(t)-1)
 \quad}
\]
imply for every $\tau\in J_{s,t}$ and every normalized radius
$x$ between the two slow inner endpoints,
\[
 \boxed{\quad
 \frac{1}{a(\tau)}
 \le \min\{r_-(s),r_-(t)\}
 \le x \le \max\{r_-(s),r_-(t)\}\le B .
 \quad}
\]
Indeed, the physical-scale fundamental theorem of calculus gives
$|a(\tau)-a(s)|,|a(\tau)-a(t)|\le V_{s,t}$ and therefore
$a(\tau)\ge a(s)-V_{s,t}\ge a(s)/L(s)$, and similarly
$a(\tau)\ge a(t)/L(t)$. Inverting these inequalities proves the
two endpoint comparisons without postulating a new source envelope.
Thus the previously verified \emph{original} Cramer source-time
theorem is valid at each moving-slit radius and each intermediate
time, provided its original parameter/derivative hypotheses hold
along the orbit. Equivalently, the scale criterion is
\[
 \frac{V_{s,t}}{a(s)}\le 1-\ell(s)^{1/16},
 \qquad
 \frac{V_{s,t}}{a(t)}\le 1-\ell(t)^{1/16}.
\]
This criterion is locally achievable whenever $0<\ell(s)<1$,
$a(s)>0$, and $a,\ell$ are continuous with locally integrable
$a'$: at $t=s$ the scale variation is zero and the endpoint
slack is strictly positive. It does \emph{not} assert a global
large-time comparison or a Type--II rate. The necessary bound on
$V_{s,t}$ for the final \emph{same} nonlinear Hartree modulation
orbit must be produced from its genuine dynamical equations.

The corresponding Lean geometry theorem,
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_original\_Cramer\_shell\_covers\_moving\_slow\_slit},
is checked against the two actual slow-inner radii in
\texttt{ActualHartreeMovingSlitCramerScaleVariation.lean}; it
derives the shell inclusion from the primitive variation and margin
inequalities, not from an assumed shell inclusion. Its dedicated
strict CI \texttt{42756618784} completed with \emph{Success}
(10 November 2026, 04:45 UTC), original job
\texttt{129632373174}, artifact \texttt{13736666241}.
The raw job log confirms the exact theorem type, trust-zero
warning-as-error compilation, pinned Mathlib and the transitive
axioms \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, \texttt{Quot.sound}. No original-environment
count is increased. Even after this geometric step, one must obtain
the physical source/moment derivative time budget, moving-basis
bounds, genuine full nonlinear residual positive gain, parameter
Lipschitz bounds and finite PDE recursion before §2.13 can close.



\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Local moving-slit inclusion needs no quantitative
scale-variation assumption (new proof candidate).}
The preceding finite-$V_{s,t}$ criterion is useful on a large interval
but is unnecessarily strong for differentiating the Green coefficient
locally. Fix a physical slice $s_0$ with
\[
 a(s_0)>0,\qquad 0<\ell(s_0)<1,\qquad
 r_-(s_0)=\frac{\ell(s_0)^{-1/16}}{a(s_0)}<B.
\]
Assume only that $a,\ell$ are continuous at $s_0$.
The strict physical radius gap
\[
 \frac{1}{a(s_0)}<r_-(s_0)<B
\]
allows a middle radius $m$ with
$1/a(s_0)<m<r_-(s_0)$. By continuity, a sufficiently
small open interval $J=(s_-,s_+)$ about $s_0$ satisfies
\[
 \frac{1}{a(\tau)}<m<r_-(t)<B
 \qquad (t,\tau\in J).
\]
Consequently, every $t\in J$, every intermediate
$\tau\in[\min(s_0,t),\max(s_0,t)]$ and every radius
$x$ between the \emph{actual} moving endpoints satisfy
\[
 \boxed{\quad \frac{1}{a(\tau)}
 \le x\le B.\quad}
\]
No $V_{s,t}$, no prescribed Type--II exponent and no separate
source envelope are inputs to this \emph{local geometric} argument.
The exact original Hartree radii and real-Cramer source shell
are used in
\texttt{ActualHartreeMovingSlitCramerScaleVariation.lean},
theorems
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_original\_Cramer\_shell\_local\_from\_continuous\_orbit}
and
\texttt{actual\_Hartree\_original\_Cramer\_shell\_local\_segment\_from\_continuous\_orbit}.
Both are submitted for independent pinned trust-zero
\texttt{\#check}/\texttt{\#print axioms} CI; at this checkpoint
CI \texttt{42763633557} is \emph{pending}, not yet strict
accepted. This reduces the local moving-slit \emph{domain} question
to ordinary physical parameter continuity, but the
original time-weighted source derivative and its
nonlinear four-region remainder bound remain unproved.
The revised original manuscript acceptance remains
\textbf{11/13}; neither local geometry declaration increases it.



\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Two-stage dynamical Type--II proof reduction, after the
Rapha\"el--Rodnianski method (10 November 2026).}
An inspection of Rapha\"el--Rodnianski,
\emph{Publ. Math. IH\'ES} 115 (2012), \S6--\S7,
shows a useful logical separation: a coarse modulation/energy
bootstrap first yields actual finite-time blowup; a precise flux
law and sharp blowup rate are proved only afterwards.
The corresponding HBL reduction below is purely kinematic until
the original nonlocal PDE produces an actual trapped modulation orbit.
It does \emph{not} replace the final theorem's required emergent
power-law asymptotic.

Let \(q_0,\lambda_0>0\) and \(0<m\le M\). Suppose one
\emph{genuine Hartree PDE orbit}, with its physically selected two
real modulation channels, generates \(C^1\) coordinates
\(q(s)>0\), \(\lambda(s)>0\), \(\mu(s)\) such that
\[
 q(s_0)=q_0,\qquad \lambda(s_0)=\lambda_0,\qquad
 q_s=4\mu,\qquad
 \frac{\lambda_s}{\lambda}=\frac{1+4\mu}{2q},
 \qquad m\le\mu(s)\le M.
\]
The last inequality must be shown to be an invariant physical
trapping interval; it is \emph{not} currently a PDE result.
Integration gives the exact comparison
\[
 q_0+4m(s-s_0)\le q(s)\le q_0+4M(s-s_0).
\]
The coupled clock identities integrate \emph{without} first
specifying a terminal time or any exponent:
\[
 \begin{aligned}
 \frac{d}{ds}\log\frac q{\lambda^2}&=-\frac1q,\\
 \tau(s):=\frac{q(s)}{\lambda(s)^2}
 &=\frac{q_0}{\lambda_0^2}
   \exp\left(-\int_{s_0}^s\frac{du}{q(u)}\right),\\
 \tau_s&=-\lambda^{-2}.
 \end{aligned}
\]
Because \(q(s)\le q_0+4M(s-s_0)\),
\[
 \int_{s_0}^{s}\frac{du}{q(u)}
 \ge\frac1{4M}\log\left(1+\frac{4M(s-s_0)}{q_0}\right)
 \longrightarrow+\infty.
\]
Therefore \(\tau(s)\downarrow0\); with \(t_s=\lambda^{-2}\)
and \(t(s_0)=t_0\) one obtains
\[
 T:=t_0+\frac{q_0}{\lambda_0^2}<\infty,\qquad
 t(s)\uparrow T,\qquad
 \tau(s)=T-t(s).
\]
Moreover \(q(s)\to+\infty\), whence
\[
 \boxed{\quad
 \frac{\lambda(s)^{-1}}{\sqrt{T-t(s)}}
   =q(s)^{-1/2}\longrightarrow0.
 \quad}
\]
This is the strictly super-self-similar \emph{Type--II concentration
criterion}, not yet a Hartree blowup existence theorem: the same
physical solution must still be constructed with bounded
\(\dot H^1\) and a nonextendible concentrating bubble.

Only \emph{after} that coarse PDE construction should the
sharp original conclusion be recovered. An
\emph{integrated} physical modulation-flux estimate
\[
 \int_{s_0}^{\infty}
 \left(1+\log\frac{s}{s_0}\right)|\mu_s(s)|\,ds<\infty
 \quad(s_0>0)
\]
already suffices for the final scalar asymptotics, provided the
resulting \(\mu_\infty>9/16\). Indeed
\[
 \int_{s_0}^\infty
    \frac{|\mu(s)-\mu_\infty|}{s}\,ds
 \le\int_{s_0}^\infty
      \log(s/s_0)|\mu_s(s)|\,ds<\infty,
\]
and with \(q(s)\sim4\mu_\infty s\) the exact clock formula
implies
\[
 \lambda(t)^{-1}
 =C_\lambda(T-t)^{1/2+2\mu_\infty}(1+o(1)).
\]
This removes the earlier need to prove the pointwise
\(|\mu_s(s)|\lesssim s^{-1-\delta}\) merely for the
\emph{scalar rate-conversion step}. It does \emph{not} claim
that the weighted physical flux has been bounded, nor may it
replace the physical PDE, four-region matching, or
HLS/nonlocal error estimates.
The sharp final HBL theorem retains its original statement.
In particular, that original statement itself includes the
pointwise conclusion
\(\lvert\mu_s\rvert\lesssim s^{-1-\delta}\).
The logarithmically weighted integral above replaces this
pointwise hypothesis only for deriving the \emph{power law};
it does \emph{not} prove or remove the separately stated
pointwise \(\mu_s\) bound. Retaining the original theorem
therefore still requires an eventual PDE proof of that bound.

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{The quantitative defect that cannot be deleted.}
For the conditional original Hartree spatial-shell budget currently
in the verification ledger (not yet a full moving-PDE estimate),
the quadratic source estimate \(s^{-15/8}\) gives under
an absolute backward Duhamel bound
\[
 \int_s^\infty u^{-15/8}\,du=\frac87 s^{-7/8}.
\]
To close an \(o(s^{-9/8})\) correction using \emph{that} absolute
inverse alone, the source must improve to
\(O(s^{-17/8-\eta})\) for some \(\eta>0\), or the true
projected nonlocal propagator must supply a further
\(s^{-1/4-\eta}\) gain. The formerly pursued
\(s^{-9/4}\) residual is stronger than this \emph{one}
absolute-Duhamel threshold by \(s^{-1/8}\); it must
not be weakened in any other manuscript estimate without
a complete balance check.
The previously strictly verified Frobenius moving-slit
spatial \(R\) gain cannot be substituted into the
\emph{full} PDE source unless the same original Cramer-selected
time derivative, moving basis, all four regional nonlocal terms,
and their common-domain parameter controls preserve it.

Thus the shortest currently honest PDE spine is:
original Hartree source and strict Cramer/Green/C\textsuperscript{1}
transfer; one genuinely quantitative full-four-region projected
source/inverse estimate closing this defect; one true physical
strong solution with modulation trapping; the preceding
\emph{automatic} Type--II criterion; then the weighted-flux
refinement for the original sharp rate.
No new \texttt{theorem}/\texttt{lemma} environment is created
by this organizational reduction. Original revised acceptance
remains \(11/13\). A detailed reference comparison and proof
is available in
\texttt{verification/HBL\_213\_TYPEII\_TWO\_STAGE\_PROOF\_REDUCTION\_20261110.md}.


\medskip

The source
\texttt{HBL/TypeIIDynamicSelection/ActualHartreePhysicalFiniteProfile.lean}
takes a finite list of \texttt{ActualHartreeLayerPacket} objects
and the strict recurrence of their formal weights \emph{as data}.
The \texttt{ActualHartreePacketCertified} definition even
contains the tautology
\(\mathtt{Q.tangent=Q.tangent}\), not an original Hartree

The shortest main proof must supply this missing original-PDE
packet producer, or replace the packet construction by
a rigorously source-aligned projected Hamiltonian energy
bootstrap. Further conditional recursion environments will
not be counted towards the current original \(11/13\).
